FUNCTION renderCallDetailPage
LINES 19936-26256
TERMS dash-priority, dash-activity, dash-greeting, vx-activity, renderDashboard

function renderCallDetailPage(call, history) {
  if (!call || !call.id) { console.error('Call data incomplete'); return; }
  var _detailPage = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  if (!_detailPage || !_detailPage.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
  
  const timestamp = (typeof parseCallTimestamp === 'function' ? parseCallTimestamp(call.created_at) : null) || new Date(call.created_at);
  const callerName = call.caller_name || '';
  const displayPhone = call.caller_phone || call.phone_number || '';
  const dur = call.duration_seconds || 0;
  
  // ─── AVATAR (Initialen) ───
  const initials = callerName
    ? callerName.split(' ').map(w => (w[0] || '').toUpperCase()).join('').slice(0, 2) || 'UN'
    : 'UN';
  const avatarEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-avatar');
  if (avatarEl) {
    avatarEl.textContent = initials;
    avatarEl.style.borderRadius = '10px'; // eckig, konsistent mit Task-Detail
  }
  
  // ─── TITLE: Name (wenn vorhanden), sonst Nummer ───
  const titleEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-title');
  if (titleEl) {
    titleEl.textContent = callerName || displayPhone || 'Unbekannte Nummer';
  }
  
  // ─── SUBTITLE: Nummer (wenn Name vorhanden) — sonst "Unbekannte Nummer" ───
  const subtitleEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-subtitle');
  if (subtitleEl) {
    if (callerName && displayPhone) {
      subtitleEl.textContent = displayPhone;
    } else if (!callerName && displayPhone) {
      subtitleEl.textContent = 'Unbekannter Anrufer';
    } else {
      subtitleEl.textContent = '';
    }
  }
  
  // ─── DATE TOP-RIGHT ───
  const dateEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner-date');
  if (dateEl) {
    var _weekdayDate = vxFormatDateTimeCH(timestamp, { weekdayDate: true });
    var _time = vxFormatDateTimeCH(timestamp, { timeOnly: true });
    dateEl.innerHTML = '<span>' + _weekdayDate + '</span><span class="vx-cd-date-time">' + _time + '</span>';
  }

  const leadQ = String(call.lead_quality || '').toLowerCase();
  let qualityValue = leadQ === 'warm' ? 'Warm' : (leadQ === 'hot' ? 'Hot' : (leadQ === 'cold' ? 'Cold' : '–'));
  
  const statusVal = String(call.dashboard_status || 'new').toLowerCase();
  const statusLabel = (typeof vxGetInquiryDisplayStatus === 'function')
    ? vxGetInquiryDisplayStatus({ id: call.id, fields: call })
    : (statusVal === 'closed' || statusVal === 'done' ? 'Erledigt'
      : statusVal === 'archived' || statusVal === 'archiviert' ? 'Erledigt'
      : statusVal === 'in_progress' ? 'In Bearbeitung'
      : statusVal === 'follow_up_scheduled' ? 'Geplant'
      : 'Neu');
  const statusColor = statusVal === 'closed' || statusVal === 'done' ? '#059669'
    : statusVal === 'in_progress' ? '#1A6FE8'
    : statusVal === 'follow_up_scheduled' ? '#E8C547'
    : '#1A6FE8';
  
  // ─── BODY CONTENT (Card-Patterns wie Dashboard) ───
  const callId = call.id;
  const stateMeta = (typeof vxGetCallDisplayState === 'function' ? vxGetCallDisplayState(call) : null) || vxGetDefaultCallDisplayState();
  const summary = vxGetCallSummary(call);
  const callTranscript = vxGetCallTranscript(call);
  const noteVal = call.notes_customer_voxera || call.notes || '';
  
  let html = '';
  html += vxRenderDetailBackLinkHtml();
  var durStr = dur >= 60 ? Math.floor(dur/60) + ':' + String(dur%60).padStart(2,'0') : (dur || '0') + 's';
  var safeCategory = (typeof getCallCategoryDisplayLabel === 'function') ? (getCallCategoryDisplayLabel({ id: call.id, fields: call }) || 'Anruf') : (call.category || 'Anruf');
  var nextActionRaw = String(call.next_action || '').trim();
  var nextActionNorm = nextActionRaw.toLowerCase();
  var isNoAction = !nextActionRaw || nextActionNorm === 'keine aktion nötig' || nextActionNorm === 'keine aktion' || nextActionNorm.indexOf('keine aktion') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('kein handlungsbedarf') >= 0;
  var isCallback = !!call.callback_requested || String(call.category || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('rückruf') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('rückruf') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('zurückruf') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('anrufen') >= 0;
  var isTest = String(call.category || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('test') >= 0 || String(summary || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('testanruf') >= 0;
  var priorityNorm = String(call.priority || call.urgency || '').toLowerCase();
  var categoryNorm = String(call.category || '').toLowerCase();
  var relevanceLabel = leadQ === 'hot' ? 'Hohe Relevanz' : (leadQ === 'warm' ? 'Mittlere Relevanz' : (leadQ === 'cold' ? 'Niedrige Relevanz' : (priorityNorm ? priorityNorm.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + priorityNorm.slice(1) : 'Nicht bewertet')));
  var statusBusinessLabel = statusVal === 'closed' || statusVal === 'done' ? 'Erledigt'
    : (statusVal === 'archived' || statusVal === 'archiviert' ? 'Archiviert'
      : (call.follow_up_at ? 'Geplant' : 'Offen'));
  var isOffer = categoryNorm.indexOf('offerte') >= 0 || categoryNorm.indexOf('angebot') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('offerte') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('angebot') >= 0;
  var isAppointment = categoryNorm.indexOf('termin') >= 0 || nextActionNorm.indexOf('termin') >= 0;
  var hasConcreteAction = !!nextActionRaw && !isNoAction;
  var hasHandlungsbedarf = !isNoAction && !isTest && (isCallback || isOffer || isAppointment || hasConcreteAction || leadQ === 'hot' || leadQ === 'warm');
  var actionTitle = 'Nächsten Schritt prüfen';
  var actionText = 'Anfrage prüfen und den passenden nächsten Schritt festlegen.';
  var actionHint = 'Voxera hat eine offene Anfrage erkannt.';
  if (isNoAction || isTest) {
    actionTitle = 'Kein Handlungsbedarf erkannt';
    actionText = 'Der Anruf enthält kein konkretes Anliegen. Du kannst ihn abschliessen oder archivieren.';
    actionHint = isTest ? 'Testanruf oder nicht verwertbarer Inhalt erkannt.' : 'Kein konkreter nächster Schritt erkannt.';
  } else if (isCallback) {
    actionTitle = 'Rückruf empfohlen';
    actionText = nextActionRaw || 'Die Anruferin oder der Anrufer möchte zurückgerufen werden. Rufe direkt an oder plane einen Follow-up.';
    actionHint = 'Rückrufsignal erkannt.';
  } else if (isOffer) {
    actionTitle = 'Anfrage prüfen';
    actionText = nextActionRaw || 'Es handelt sich wahrscheinlich um eine Offertenanfrage. Prüfe die Zusammenfassung und erstelle bei Bedarf eine Aufgabe.';
    actionHint = 'Offerten- oder Angebotsbezug erkannt.';
  } else if (isAppointment) {
    actionTitle = 'Termin klären';
    actionText = nextActionRaw || 'Der Anruf betrifft wahrscheinlich einen Termin. Prüfe die Angaben und plane bei Bedarf einen Follow-up.';
    actionHint = 'Terminbezug erkannt.';
  } else if (hasConcreteAction) {
    actionTitle = 'Nächster Schritt erkannt';
    actionText = nextActionRaw;
    actionHint = 'KI-Empfehlung aus dem Gespräch.';
  }
  var actionTitleSafe = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml(actionTitle) : actionTitle;
  var actionTextSafe = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml(actionText) : actionText;
  var actionHintSafe = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml(actionHint) : actionHint;
  var dateLong = timestamp ? vxFormatDateTimeCH(timestamp, { dateOnly: true }) : '';
  var timeShort = timestamp ? vxFormatDateTimeCH(timestamp, { timeOnly: true }) : '';

  // ─── HEADER PILLS — max. 2: Status + Handlungsbedarf/Rückruf
  // Relevanz lebt NICHT im Header (sie steht in der Details-Karte).
  var _pillBase = 'font-size:12px;font-weight:700;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;white-space:nowrap;line-height:1;';
  var _detailPills = [];
  function _addDetailPill(label, style) {
    if (!label || _detailPills.length >= 2) return;
    _detailPills.push('<span style="' + _pillBase + style + '">' + ((typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml(label) : label) + '</span>');
  }
  _addDetailPill(statusBusinessLabel, 'border:0.5px solid ' + statusColor + '33;color:' + statusColor + ';background:' + statusColor + '14;');
  if (isCallback) _addDetailPill('Rückruf', 'color:#1A6FE8;background:#EFF6FF;border:0.5px solid #BFDBFE;');
  else if (isNoAction || isTest) _addDetailPill('Kein Handlungsbedarf', 'color:#64748B;background:#F8FAFC;border:0.5px solid #E2E8F0;');
  else if (isOffer) _addDetailPill('Anfrage prüfen', 'color:#1A6FE8;background:#EFF6FF;border:0.5px solid #BFDBFE;');
  else if (isAppointment) _addDetailPill('Terminbezug', 'color:#475569;background:#F8FAFC;border:0.5px solid #E2E8F0;');
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // VOXERA DETAIL HEADER — clean rebuild 2026-05-17
  // Visual sprache: same dark Voxera-Kopfkarte as Heute/Anfragen page headers.
  // Struktur:
  //   Desktop split:  [avatar][identity ........... ][primary][menu][focus]
  //                                                   [pills row]
  //   Desktop focus:  [back-pill][avatar][identity .][primary][menu][split-toggle]
  //   Mobile:         [back-pill]                    (Aktionen leben in Bottom-Bar)
  //                   [avatar][identity .........]
  //                   [pills row]
  //
  // Behaviour-Regeln:
  //   - Buttons rechtsbündig (flex-shrink:0, min-width auf Wrapper)
  //   - Badges max. 3, mit overflow-Ellipsis
  //   - Primaryaktion ist kontextabhängig (Anrufen / Erledigen / Aufgabe erstellen)
  //   - Auf Mobile: keine Header-Aktion-Buttons (Bottom-Bar übernimmt)
  //   - Auf Mobile: kein Fokus/Expand-Button (Detail = bereits Vollansicht)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  try {
    var _banner = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
    if (_banner) {
      var _esc = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml : function(v){
        return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){
          return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c] || c;
        });
      };
      var _headerTitle = _esc(callerName || displayPhone || 'Unbekannte Nummer');
      var _headerPhone = displayPhone ? _esc(displayPhone) : '';
      var _headerDate = timeShort ? _esc(timeShort) : '';
      var _headerDuration = durStr ? _esc(durStr) : '';
      var _metaParts = [];
      if (_headerPhone) _metaParts.push(_headerPhone);
      else _metaParts.push('Keine Telefonnummer');
      if (_headerDate) _metaParts.push(_headerDate);
      if (_headerDuration) _metaParts.push(_headerDuration);
      var _headerMeta = _metaParts.join(' · ');
      var _headerMetaHtml = _metaParts.map(function(part){
        return '<span class="vxd-meta-item">' + _esc(part) + '</span>';
      }).join('');

      // Badges: max. 2, bereits oben in _detailPills berechnet
      var _statusPillsHtml = _detailPills.slice(0, 2).join('');

      var _hasPhone = !!displayPhone;
      var _isFocusMode = !!(document.body && document.body.classList && document.body.classList.contains('vx-requests-focus-mode'));

      // Primaryaktion bestimmen — eine einzige sinnvolle Hauptaktion
      // - Rückruf + Nummer  → Anrufen
      // - Kein Handlungsbedarf / Test → Erledigen
      // - Offerte / Termin → Aufgabe erstellen
      // - sonst → Erledigen
      // legacyId stellt sicher, dass Legacy-JS (Loading/Success-Feedback in
      // vxCallDetailErledigt etc.) den richtigen Button findet.
      var _primary;
      if (isCallback && _hasPhone) {
        _primary = {
          label: 'Anrufen',
          icon: 'ph-phone',
          onclick: 'vxCallDetailAnrufen(event)',
          title: 'Anrufen: ' + _headerPhone,
          disabled: false,
          legacyId: 'vx-cd-qa-call'
        };
      } else if (isNoAction || isTest) {
        _primary = {
          label: 'Erledigen',
          icon: 'ph-check',
          onclick: 'vxCallDetailErledigt(event)',
          title: 'Als erledigt markieren',
          disabled: false,
          legacyId: 'vx-cd-qa-done'
        };
      } else {
        _primary = {
          label: 'Erledigen',
          icon: 'ph-check',
          onclick: 'vxCallDetailErledigt(event)',
          title: 'Als erledigt markieren',
          disabled: false,
          legacyId: 'vx-cd-qa-done'
        };
      }

      var _focusLabel = _isFocusMode ? 'Split' : 'Fokus';
      var _focusTitle = _isFocusMode ? 'Zur Split-Ansicht wechseln' : 'Fokusmodus öffnen';
      var _focusIcon = _isFocusMode ? 'ph-arrows-in' : 'ph-arrows-out';

      var _secondaryHeaderLabel = '';
      if (isCallback) _secondaryHeaderLabel = 'Rückruf';
      else if (isNoAction || isTest) _secondaryHeaderLabel = 'Kein Handlungsbedarf';
      else if (isOffer) _secondaryHeaderLabel = 'Anfrage prüfen';
      else if (isAppointment) _secondaryHeaderLabel = 'Terminbezug';
      else if (hasConcreteAction) _secondaryHeaderLabel = 'Handlungsbedarf';

      var _statusPillClass = (statusBusinessLabel === 'Erledigt' || statusBusinessLabel === 'Archiviert') ? ' vxdr-pill--success' : ' vxdr-pill--status';
      var _pillHtml = '<span class="vxdr-pill' + _statusPillClass + '">' + _esc(statusBusinessLabel) + '</span>'
        + (_secondaryHeaderLabel ? '<span class="vxdr-pill vxdr-pill--context">' + _esc(_secondaryHeaderLabel) + '</span>' : '');

      var _pageForFocus = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
      var _isEmbeddedSplit = !!(_pageForFocus && document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right') && document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right').contains(_pageForFocus));
      var _focusMode = !!((document.body && document.body.classList && document.body.classList.contains('vx-requests-focus-mode')) || (_pageForFocus && !_isEmbeddedSplit && (_pageForFocus.classList.contains('is-fullscreen') || _pageForFocus.classList.contains('vx-detail-focus'))));
      var _layoutClass = _focusMode ? 'vxdr-focus' : 'vxdr-split';
      var _focusToggleTitle = _focusMode ? 'Zur Split-Ansicht wechseln' : 'Fokusmodus öffnen';
      var _focusToggleIcon = _focusMode ? 'ph-arrows-in' : 'ph-arrows-out';

      _banner.className = 'vxdr-banner';
      _banner.removeAttribute('style');
      _banner.innerHTML = ''
        + '<section class="vxdr-header ' + _layoutClass + '" aria-label="Anrufdetails">'
        +   '<button type="button" class="vxdr-back" aria-label="Zurück zu Anfragen" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxHandleDetailBack();">'
        +     '<i class="ph-bold ph-arrow-left" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Anfragen</span>'
        +   '</button>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-identity">'
        +     '<div class="vxdr-avatar" id="call-detail-avatar">' + _esc(initials) + '</div>'
        +     '<div class="vxdr-copy">'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-title-row"><div class="vxdr-title" id="call-detail-title">' + _headerTitle + '</div></div>'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-meta" id="call-detail-subtitle" aria-label="' + _esc(_headerMeta) + '">' + _headerMetaHtml + '</div>'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-pills">' + _pillHtml + '</div>'
        +     '</div>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-actions">'
        +     '<button type="button" class="vxdr-btn vxdr-primary" id="' + _primary.legacyId + '" onclick="event.stopPropagation();' + _primary.onclick + '" title="' + _esc(_primary.title) + '"' + (_primary.disabled ? ' disabled aria-disabled="true"' : '') + '>'
        +       '<i class="ph-bold ' + _primary.icon + '" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="vxdr-btn-label">' + _esc(_primary.label) + '</span>'
        +     '</button>'
        +     (_focusMode
        ? '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn vxdr-focus-toggle" id="vx-cd-qa-expand" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxCallDetailExpand();" aria-label="Ansicht verkleinern" title="Ansicht verkleinern"><i class="ph-bold ph-arrows-in" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>'
        : '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn vxdr-focus-toggle" id="vx-cd-qa-expand" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxCallDetailExpand();" aria-label="' + _esc(_focusToggleTitle) + '" title="' + _esc(_focusToggleTitle) + '"><i class="ph-bold ' + _focusToggleIcon + '" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>')
        +     '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn" id="vxd-menu-trigger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdToggleMenu(event);" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Weitere Aktionen" title="Weitere Aktionen"><i class="ph-bold ph-dots-three-vertical" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-menu" id="vxd-menu" role="menu" hidden>'
        +     (document.getElementById('tab-archiv') && document.getElementById('tab-archiv').classList.contains('active')
                ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxArchivRestore(window._vxCurrentCallId);"><i class="ph-bold ph-arrow-u-up-left" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Wiederherstellen</span></button>'
                : '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxCallDetailFaelligkeit();"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Follow-up planen</span></button>'
                + (isCallback || isOffer || isAppointment ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxCallDetailErledigt(event);"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Als erledigt markieren</span></button>' : '')
                + (_hasPhone && !isCallback ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxCallDetailAnrufen(event);"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Anrufen</span></button>' : '')
                + '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxCallDetailArchivieren();"><i class="ph-bold ph-archive" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Archivieren</span></button>'
              )
        +   '</div>'
        + '</section>';
    }
  } catch (_headerErr) {
    console.warn('[voxera] detail header render skipped', _headerErr);
  }

  // ─── CARD: Nächster Schritt — primary action cockpit ───
  html += '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:12px;box-shadow:0 10px 28px rgba(15,23,42,.04);">';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:flex-start;padding:15px 16px 12px;">';
  html +=     '<div style="width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:12px;background:#EEF4FF;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;"><i class="ph-bold ph-flag" style="font-size:17px;color:#1A6FE8;" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>';
  html +=     '<div style="min-width:0;flex:1;">';
  html +=       '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin-bottom:4px;">' + actionTitleSafe + '</div>';
  html +=       '<div style="font-size:13px;color:#465670;line-height:1.55;">' + actionTextSafe + '</div>';
  html +=       '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#94A3B8;margin-top:5px;">' + actionHintSafe + '</div>';
  html +=     '</div>';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:0 16px 15px;flex-wrap:wrap;">';
  if (isNoAction || isTest) {
    html += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailErledigt()" style="height:36px;padding:0 14px;border-radius:10px;border:0.5px solid #D7DEEA;background:#fff;color:#0D1F3C;font-size:13px;font-weight:750;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>Als erledigt markieren</button>';
    html += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailArchivieren()" style="height:36px;padding:0 14px;border-radius:10px;border:0.5px solid #E2E8F0;background:#F8FAFC;color:#64748B;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><i class="ph-bold ph-archive" aria-hidden="true"></i>Archivieren</button>';
  } else {
    if (isCallback && displayPhone) html += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailAnrufen()" style="height:38px;padding:0 16px;border-radius:10px;border:none;background:#1A6FE8;color:#fff;font-size:13px;font-weight:800;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;box-shadow:0 8px 18px rgba(26,111,232,.18);"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone" aria-hidden="true"></i>Anrufen</button>';
    html += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailFaelligkeit()" style="height:38px;padding:0 16px;border-radius:10px;border:0.5px solid #D7DEEA;background:#fff;color:#0D1F3C;font-size:13px;font-weight:750;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-plus" aria-hidden="true"></i>Follow-up planen</button>';
    html += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailErledigt()" style="height:38px;padding:0 16px;border-radius:10px;border:0.5px solid #D7DEEA;background:#fff;color:#0D1F3C;font-size:13px;font-weight:750;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>Als erledigt markieren</button>';
  }
  html +=   '</div>';
  html += '</div>';

  // ─── CARD: Zusammenfassung (KI) ───
  html += '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:12px;">';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--line);">';
  html +=     '<i class="ph-bold ph-sparkle" style="color:#1A6FE8;flex-shrink:0;font-size:16px;" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
  html +=     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.01em;">Zusammenfassung</div>';
  html += summary ? '<div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748B;margin-left:auto;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;">Automatisch erstellt</div>' : '';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html +=   '<div style="padding:13px 14px;">';
  if (!stateMeta.isTemporary && summary) {
    html += '<div style="font-size:13px;color:#37415A;line-height:1.7;">' + summary + '</div>';
  } else {
    html += '<div style="text-align:center;padding:10px 0;">';
    html +=   '<div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--slate);font-style:italic;">' + (callTranscript ? 'Gesprächsverlauf vorhanden — Zusammenfassung wird noch erstellt.' : 'Keine Zusammenfassung vorhanden.') + '</div>';
    html +=   '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--slate2);margin-top:4px;">Vermutlich wurde aufgelegt oder der Anruf war zu kurz.</div>';
    html += '</div>';
  }
  html +=   '</div>';
  html += '</div>';

  // ─── CARD: Details grid ───
  html += '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:12px;">';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--line);">';
  html +=     '<i class="ph-bold ph-info" style="color:#1A6FE8;flex-shrink:0;font-size:16px;" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
  html +=     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.01em;">Anrufdetails</div>';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html +=   '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:0;padding:12px 14px;">';
  function _cdMeta(label, value, sub) { return '<div style="padding:7px 12px 7px 0;min-width:0;"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:750;color:#94A3B8;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;margin-bottom:4px;">' + label + '</div><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#0D1F3C;line-height:1.35;word-break:break-word;">' + (value || '–') + '</div>' + (sub ? '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#64748B;margin-top:2px;">' + sub + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }
  html += _cdMeta('Kategorie', safeCategory, null);
  html += _cdMeta('Relevanz', relevanceLabel, priorityNorm ? ('Dringlichkeit: ' + priorityNorm) : null);
  html += _cdMeta('Status', statusBusinessLabel, null);
  html += _cdMeta('Handlungsbedarf', hasHandlungsbedarf ? 'Ja' : 'Nein', actionTitle);
  html += _cdMeta('Dauer', durStr, null);
  html += _cdMeta('Zeitpunkt', timeShort, dateLong);
  html += _cdMeta('Quelle', 'Telefonanruf', displayPhone || null);
  html +=   '</div>';
  html += '</div>';

  // ─── CARD: Audioaufnahme + Transkript (secondary accordion, geordnet nach Sprecher/Abschnitten) ───
  html += (typeof vxRenderCallAudioCardHtml === 'function') ? vxRenderCallAudioCardHtml(call) : '';
  html += (typeof vxRenderTranscriptCardHtml === 'function')
    ? vxRenderTranscriptCardHtml(callTranscript)
    : '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:14px;"><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 14px;"><i class="ph-bold ph-file-text" style="color:#1A6FE8;flex-shrink:0;font-size:16px;" aria-hidden="true"></i><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.01em;">Gesprächsverlauf</div></div><div style="border-top:0.5px solid var(--line);padding:12px 14px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--ink2);white-space:pre-wrap;max-height:320px;overflow-y:auto;">' + escapeHtml(callTranscript || 'Kein Gesprächsverlauf vorhanden.') + '</div></div>';

  // ─── CARD: Notiz ───
  html += '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:12px;">';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--line);">';
  html +=     '<i class="ph-bold ph-pencil-simple" style="color:#1A6FE8;flex-shrink:0;font-size:16px;" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
  html +=     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.01em;">Persönliche Notiz</div>';
  html +=     '<div id="vx-cd-save-indicator" class="vx-cd-save-indicator" style="margin-left:auto;opacity:0;">';
  html +=       '<span class="dot"></span><span class="indicator-text"></span>';
  html +=     '</div>';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html +=   '<div style="padding:12px 14px;">';
  html +=     '<textarea id="call-detail-notes" style="width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;font-family:inherit;font-size:13px;resize:vertical;min-height:74px;background:var(--vx-bg,#F7F8FA);color:var(--ink,#1A1A2E);box-sizing:border-box;line-height:1.6;outline:none;" placeholder="Notiz hinzufügen...">' + noteVal + '</textarea>';
  html +=     '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-top:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">';
  html +=       '<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--slate);">Speichert automatisch · Strg+S für sofortiges Speichern</span>';
  html +=     '</div>';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html += '</div>';

  // ─── Kontakt-Historie ───
  html += renderTimelineHTML(history);
  
  const contentEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-content');
  if (contentEl) contentEl.innerHTML = html;

  // Lucide icons re-initialize
  /* phosphor: no init needed */
  
  // ─── Morphing-Banner Scroll Listener ───
  vxSetupMorphingBanner();
  
  // ─── ELITE: Auto-Save für Notizen aktivieren ───
  if (typeof vxSetupNotesAutoSave === 'function') {
    vxSetupNotesAutoSave(callId);
  }

  // [PATCH v2.20 — Follow-up Default: Morgen 09:00 vorauswählen]
  // Pflichtdatum: Nutzer muss nicht mehr manuell auf einen Quick-Chip klicken.
  // Der Chip "Morgen 09:00" ist bereits als is-active markiert (HTML),
  // hier setzen wir den unsichtbaren Input-Wert passend dazu.
  setTimeout(function() {
    var defaultInput = document.getElementById('followup-date');
    if (defaultInput && !defaultInput.value) {
      var tomorrow9 = new Date();
      tomorrow9.setDate(tomorrow9.getDate() + 1);
      tomorrow9.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
      defaultInput.value = vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput ? vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(tomorrow9) : '';
    }
  }, 50);
  

  // ─── VOXERA: Header-actions are rendered as a stable mockup-aligned group ───
  // Keep all further action logic in the content cards to avoid header crowding/overlap.
  var headerCallBtn = document.getElementById('vx-cd-header-call');
  if (headerCallBtn && !displayPhone) {
    headerCallBtn.disabled = true;
    headerCallBtn.style.opacity = '0.45';
    headerCallBtn.style.cursor = 'not-allowed';
  }

  // ─── ELITE: Strg+S für manuelles Speichern ───
  var notesEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-notes');
  if (notesEl && !notesEl._vxKbdSetup) {
    notesEl._vxKbdSetup = true;
    notesEl.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 's') {
        e.preventDefault();
        saveCallNotes(callId);
      }
    });
  }
}

// Morphing-Banner: ELITE smooth scroll
// - requestAnimationFrame (kein Layout-Thrashing)
// - Progress-based (smoother Übergang über 20→120px statt binary toggle)
// - Easing für natürliches Gefühl
// - GPU-accelerated wo möglich
function vxSetupMorphingBanner() {
  try {
    const page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    const banner = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
    const statsWrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-stats-wrap');
    if (!page || !banner) return;
    var isMobile = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches;
    
    // Smooth scroll range
    const SCROLL_START = 10;
    const SCROLL_END = 100;
    
    // Performance state
    let lastScrollY = -1;
    let rafId = null;
    
    // Cache DOM
    const avatar = document.getElementById('call-detail-avatar');
    const title = document.getElementById('call-detail-title');
    const subtitle = document.getElementById('call-detail-subtitle');
    const dateEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner-date');
    const quickActions = document.getElementById('call-detail-quick-actions');
    
    // Pre-compute styles für batched writes (vermeidet Reflows zwischen Reads)
    const styles = {
      banner: banner.style,
      avatar: avatar ? avatar.style : null,
      title: title ? title.style : null,
      subtitle: subtitle ? subtitle.style : null,
      date: dateEl ? dateEl.style : null,
      qa: quickActions ? quickActions.style : null,
      stats: statsWrap ? statsWrap.style : null
    };
    
    // Cubic-Bezier easing — "ease-out" Apple-Style
    // Sehr smooth: schnell Start, langsam Ende
    function easeOutQuart(t) {
      return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 4);
    }
    
    function lerp(a, b, t) {
      return a + (b - a) * t;
    }
    
    // Apply state - alle DOM-Writes in einem rAF-Tick (kein Layout-Thrashing)
    function applyState(scrollY) {
      if (isMobile) return;
      // Progress 0..1 mit smooth easing
      const raw = (scrollY - SCROLL_START) / (SCROLL_END - SCROLL_START);
      const linearP = raw < 0 ? 0 : (raw > 1 ? 1 : raw);
      const p = easeOutQuart(linearP);
      
      // ─── BANNER: padding (nur 1 Property) ───
      styles.banner.padding = lerp(16, 10, p) + 'px ' + lerp(18, 14, p) + 'px';
      
      // ─── AVATAR: scale via transform (GPU, kein Reflow!) ───
      if (styles.avatar) {
        const sc = lerp(1, 0.78, p);
        styles.avatar.transform = 'scale(' + sc + ')';
      }
      
      // ─── TITLE: font-size (causes Reflow aber unvermeidlich) ───
      if (styles.title) {
        styles.title.fontSize = lerp(17, 14, p) + 'px';
      }
      
      // ─── SUBTITLE: opacity + height (height collapse separat) ───
      if (styles.subtitle) {
        // Phase 1 (0-0.4): Opacity weg
        const opP = linearP < 0.4 ? linearP / 0.4 : 1;
        styles.subtitle.opacity = 1 - opP;
        // Phase 2 (0.3-0.7): Height collapse — überlappend für smoothness
        const hP = linearP < 0.3 ? 0 : (linearP > 0.7 ? 1 : (linearP - 0.3) / 0.4);
        styles.subtitle.maxHeight = lerp(20, 0, hP) + 'px';
        styles.subtitle.marginTop = lerp(2, 0, hP) + 'px';
      }
      
      // ─── DATE: opacity + width ───
      if (styles.date) {
        const dP = linearP < 0.5 ? linearP / 0.5 : 1;
        styles.date.opacity = 1 - dP;
        // Width nur am Ende collapsen (sonst springt Layout)
        if (linearP > 0.5) {
          styles.date.maxWidth = lerp(200, 0, (linearP - 0.5) / 0.5) + 'px';
        } else {
          styles.date.maxWidth = '200px';
        }
      }
      
      // ─── QUICK ACTIONS: subtle scale (GPU) ───
      if (styles.qa) {
        styles.qa.transform = 'scale(' + lerp(1, 0.88, p) + ')';
        styles.qa.transformOrigin = 'right center';
      }
      
      // ─── KPI STRIP: opacity zuerst, dann height ───
      if (styles.stats) {
        // Opacity fade in 0-0.5
        const oP = linearP < 0.5 ? linearP / 0.5 : 1;
        styles.stats.opacity = 1 - oP;
        // Height collapse in 0.3-1 (überlappend)
        const hP = linearP < 0.3 ? 0 : (linearP - 0.3) / 0.7;
        styles.stats.maxHeight = lerp(200, 0, hP) + 'px';
        styles.stats.marginBottom = lerp(14, 0, hP) + 'px';
        styles.stats.pointerEvents = linearP > 0.9 ? 'none' : 'auto';
      }
      
      // is-scrolled class for additional CSS hooks (z.B. Shadow)
      if (linearP > 0.2) {
        if (!banner._isScrolled) {
          banner.classList.add('is-scrolled');
          banner._isScrolled = true;
        }
      } else {
        if (banner._isScrolled) {
          banner.classList.remove('is-scrolled');
          banner._isScrolled = false;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // [v2.67] Scroll-Container erkennen: im Split-Panel scrollt scroll-wrap, nicht page
    function getScrollEl() {
      var splitRight = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right');
      if (splitRight && splitRight.contains(page)) {
        return document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap') || page;
      }
      return page;
    }

    function onScroll() {
      if (rafId !== null) return;
      rafId = window.requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        rafId = null;
        var scrollEl = getScrollEl();
        const sy = scrollEl.scrollTop;
        if (sy !== lastScrollY) {
          applyState(sy);
          lastScrollY = sy;
        }
      });
    }

    // idempotent listener setup — beide Container abdecken
    if (page._vxBannerOnScroll) {
      page.removeEventListener('scroll', page._vxBannerOnScroll);
      var sw = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');
      if (sw && sw._vxBannerOnScroll) sw.removeEventListener('scroll', sw._vxBannerOnScroll);
    }
    page._vxBannerOnScroll = onScroll;
    page.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    var scrollWrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');
    if (scrollWrap) {
      scrollWrap._vxBannerOnScroll = onScroll;
      scrollWrap.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    }

    // Initial state / mobile reset
    if (isMobile) {
      if (typeof vxResetCallDetailViewState === 'function') vxResetCallDetailViewState();
    } else {
      var initScrollEl = getScrollEl();
      applyState(initScrollEl.scrollTop || 0);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[scroll] setup error:', err);
  }
}
window.vxSetupMorphingBanner = vxSetupMorphingBanner;

function vxResetCallDetailViewState() {
  var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  var banner = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
  var statsWrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-stats-wrap');
  var avatar = document.getElementById('call-detail-avatar');
  var title = document.getElementById('call-detail-title');
  var subtitle = document.getElementById('call-detail-subtitle');
  var dateEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner-date');
  var quickActions = document.getElementById('call-detail-quick-actions');
  var wrap = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');

  if (page) page.scrollTop = 0;
  if (wrap) wrap.scrollTop = 0;

  // [v2.66] quickActions NICHT style-resetten — bleibt immer display:flex
  [avatar, title, subtitle, dateEl, statsWrap].forEach(function(el) {
    if (el) el.removeAttribute('style');
  });
  if (quickActions) { quickActions.style.transform = ''; quickActions.style.transformOrigin = ''; }
  if (banner) {
    banner.style.padding = '';
  }
  if (subtitle) { subtitle.style.opacity = ''; subtitle.style.maxHeight = ''; subtitle.style.marginTop = ''; }
  if (dateEl) { dateEl.style.opacity = ''; dateEl.style.maxWidth = ''; }
  if (statsWrap) { statsWrap.style.opacity = ''; statsWrap.style.maxHeight = ''; statsWrap.style.marginBottom = ''; statsWrap.style.pointerEvents = ''; }
  if (banner) {
    banner.classList.remove('is-scrolled');
    banner._isScrolled = false;
  }
}
window.vxResetCallDetailViewState = vxResetCallDetailViewState;

// Helper für Pill-Toggle (nur 1 aktiv)
function vxTogglePill(btn) {
  if (!btn) return;
  var container = btn.parentNode;
  if (container) {
    container.querySelectorAll('.vx-cd-pill').forEach(function(p) {
      p.classList.remove('is-active');
    });
  }
  btn.classList.add('is-active');
}
window.vxTogglePill = vxTogglePill;

// Nachfassen Schnellfälligkeiten: weniger roher Datepicker, schneller planbar.
function vxPad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }
function vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(date) {
  if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
  return date.getFullYear() + '-' + vxPad2(date.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + vxPad2(date.getDate()) + 'T' + vxPad2(date.getHours()) + ':' + vxPad2(date.getMinutes());
}
function vxRoundUpToMinutes(date, step) {
  var d = new Date(date.getTime());
  var minutes = d.getMinutes();
  var rounded = Math.ceil(minutes / step) * step;
  if (rounded >= 60) { d.setHours(d.getHours() + 1); rounded = 0; }
  d.setMinutes(rounded, 0, 0);
  return d;
}
function vxSetFollowupQuickDue(btn, mode) {
  var input = document.getElementById('followup-date');
  var wrap = document.getElementById('followup-date-custom-wrap');
  if (!input) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('.vx-cd-due-chip').forEach(function(chip) { chip.classList.remove('is-active'); });
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('is-active');

  var now = new Date();
  var due = new Date(now.getTime());
  if (mode === 'custom') {
    if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'flex';
    if (!input.value) {
      input.value = vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 60000), 5));
    }
    try { input.focus(); } catch(e) {}
    return;
  }

  if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
  if (mode === 'in30') {
    due = vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 60000), 5);
  } else if (mode === 'laterToday') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 17, 0, 0, 0);
    if (due.getTime() <= now.getTime() + 10 * 60000) {
      due = vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * 60 * 60000), 15);
    }
    if (due.getDate() !== now.getDate()) {
      due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
    }
  } else if (mode === 'tomorrow9') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  } else if (mode === 'plus2d') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 2, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  }
  input.value = vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(due);
}
window.vxSetFollowupQuickDue = vxSetFollowupQuickDue;

// [PATCH v2.20 — Quick-Due für manuelle Task-Modal]
// Analog zu vxSetFollowupQuickDue, aber für #manual-task-quick-chips + #manual-task-datetime
function vxManualTaskQuickDue(btn, mode) {
  var input = document.getElementById('manual-task-datetime');
  if (!input) return;
  document.querySelectorAll('#manual-task-quick-chips .vx-cd-due-chip').forEach(function(c) {
    c.classList.remove('is-active');
  });
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('is-active');
  var now = new Date();
  var due;
  if (mode === 'custom') {
    input.style.display = 'block';
    if (!input.value) {
      due = vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 60000), 5);
      input.value = vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(due);
    }
    try { input.focus(); } catch(e) {}
    return;
  }
  input.style.display = 'none';
  if (mode === 'in30') {
    due = vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 60000), 5);
  } else if (mode === 'laterToday') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 17, 0, 0, 0);
    if (due.getTime() <= now.getTime() + 10 * 60000) {
      due = vxRoundUpToMinutes(new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * 60 * 60000), 15);
    }
    if (due.getDate() !== now.getDate()) {
      due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
    }
  } else if (mode === 'tomorrow9') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  } else if (mode === 'plus2d') {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 2, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  } else {
    due = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  }
  input.value = vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(due);
}
window.vxManualTaskQuickDue = vxManualTaskQuickDue;

// [PATCH 2026-05-24 — Unified quick due chips for new action modals]
function vxResolveQuickDueDate(mode) {
  var now = new Date();
  if (mode === 'today9') return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate(), 9, 0, 0, 0);
  if (mode === 'tomorrow9') return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  if (mode === '2days' || mode === 'plus2d') return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 2, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  if (mode === 'nextweek') return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 7, 9, 0, 0, 0);
  return null;
}

function vxSetModalQuickDue(container, input, button) {
  if (!container || !input || !button) return;
  var due = vxResolveQuickDueDate(button.dataset.dueMode || '');
  if (!due) return;
  input.value = (typeof vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput === 'function')
    ? vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(due)
    : (due.getFullYear() + '-' + String(due.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(due.getDate()).padStart(2, '0') + 'T' + String(due.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(due.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'));
  container.querySelectorAll('[data-due-mode]').forEach(function(chip){ chip.classList.remove('is-active'); });
  button.classList.add('is-active');
}

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  var button = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('#followup-quick-chips [data-due-mode], #manual-task-quick-chips [data-due-mode]') : null;
  if (!button) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  e.stopPropagation();
  var followupContainer = button.closest('#followup-quick-chips');
  var manualTaskContainer = button.closest('#manual-task-quick-chips');
  if (followupContainer) vxSetModalQuickDue(followupContainer, document.getElementById('followup-datetime'), button);
  if (manualTaskContainer) vxSetModalQuickDue(manualTaskContainer, document.getElementById('manual-task-datetime'), button);
}, true);

// Neuer Follow-Up Save mit Pills
function saveFollowUpV2(callId) {
  var sb = (typeof getSupabaseAuthClient === 'function') ? getSupabaseAuthClient() : null;
  if (!sb) { toast('Authentifizierung erforderlich', 'warning'); return; }

  var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
  var rec = recordsSource.find(function(r) { return r.id === callId; });
  if (!rec) { toast('Anruf nicht gefunden', 'warning'); return; }
  var f = rec.fields || {};

  var activePill = document.querySelector('.vx-cd-pill.is-active');
  if (!activePill) { toast('Bitte eine Aktion wählen', 'warning'); return; }

  var dueDateInput = document.getElementById('followup-date');
  var dueDate = dueDateInput ? dueDateInput.value : '';
  if (!dueDate) { toast('Bitte Datum/Uhrzeit wählen', 'warning'); return; }

  var taskType = activePill.dataset.action || 'task';
  var taskTitleMap = { callback: 'Rückruf', offerte: 'Offerte', termin: 'Termin', task: 'Sonstiges' };
  var taskTitle = taskTitleMap[taskType] || 'Aufgabe';

  // Notiz aus Textarea
  var noteInput = document.getElementById('followup-note');
  var noteText = noteInput ? (noteInput.value || '').trim() : '';

  // Button als Loading markieren
  var saveBtn = document.querySelector('[onclick*="saveFollowUpV2"]');
  if (saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = true; saveBtn.textContent = '…'; }

  var followUpLocalValue = normalizeFollowUpLocalValue(dueDate) || String(dueDate || '').trim();
  var followUpDueAt = followUpLocalValue.slice(0, 10);
  var followUpDueTime = normalizeManualTaskDueTime(followUpLocalValue.slice(11, 16));
  if (!followUpDueAt || !followUpDueTime) { toast(MANUAL_TASKS_DUE_TIME_REQUIRED_MESSAGE, 'warning'); return; }

  var payload = {
    title: taskTitle + (f.caller_name ? ': ' + f.caller_name : ''),
    note: noteText,
    status: 'new',
    priority: 'medium',
    type: taskType,
    due_at: followUpDueAt,
    due_time: followUpDueTime,
    phone: f.caller_phone || f.phone_number || ''
  };

  createManualTask(payload).then(function(created) {
    if (!Array.isArray(allManualTaskRecords)) allManualTaskRecords = [];
    if (created && created.id) {
      var exists = allManualTaskRecords.some(function(r) { return String(r.id) === String(created.id); });
      if (!exists) allManualTaskRecords.unshift(created);
    }

    // [PATCH 2026-05-13 v2.3 — Rückruf-Konsolidierung (Weg 2: DB-seitig)]
    // Wenn der Call callback_requested=true hatte UND die Folge-Aufgabe NICHT
    // ein Rückruf ist (sondern Offerte/Termin/Aufgabe), wird der Callback-Status
    // am Call ausgeschaltet. Damit verschwindet die Doppelzählung in KPI und Liste.
    var isCallbackFollowup = (taskType === 'callback' || taskType === 'rueckruf');
    var hadCallbackRequest = (f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true');
    if (!isCallbackFollowup && hadCallbackRequest) {
      sb.from('calls')
        .update({ callback_requested: false })
        .eq('id', callId)
        .then(function(rr) {
          if (rr.error) {
            console.warn('[v2.3] callback_requested-Reset fehlgeschlagen:', rr.error);
          } else {
            // Lokal sofort aktualisieren
            if (rec.fields) rec.fields.callback_requested = false;
            // Re-Render
            if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') {
              try { renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords); } catch(e) {}
            }
          }
        });
    }

    renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    vxNotifMarkReadByRecord(recordId);
    renderAnrufe();
    renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    toast('Nachfassen gespeichert', 'success');
    // Zurück ins Heute-Cockpit: dort ist die neue Aufgabe unmittelbar sichtbar.
    if (typeof hideCallDetail === 'function') hideCallDetail();
    var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-dashboard');
    if (typeof showTab === 'function') showTab('dashboard', navEl);
    if (typeof loadData === 'function') setTimeout(loadData, 300);
  }).catch(function(err) {
    console.error('[followup] save error:', err);
    toast(err.message || 'Fehler beim Speichern', 'warning');
    if (saveBtn) { saveBtn.disabled = false; saveBtn.textContent = 'Speichern'; }
  });
}
window.saveFollowUpV2 = saveFollowUpV2;

function renderTimelineHTML(history) {
  const eventCount = history ? history.length : 0;
  const maxInitialEvents = 5;
  let inner = '';

  function vxTimelineDateLabel(date) {
    if (!(date instanceof Date) || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
    var tz = 'Europe/Zurich';
    var fmt = function(d) { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-CH', {timeZone:tz,year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(d); };
    var now = new Date();
    if (fmt(date) === fmt(now)) return 'Heute';
    if (fmt(date) === fmt(new Date(now.getTime() - 86400000))) return 'Gestern';
    return fmtDate(date);
  }

  if (!history || history.length === 0) {
    inner = '<div style="padding:20px 16px;text-align:center;color:var(--slate);font-size:13px;">Keine weitere Historie für diese Nummer.</div>';
  } else {
    inner = '<div style="padding:14px 16px;">';
    inner += '<div style="position:relative;padding-left:24px;">';
    inner += '<div style="position:absolute;left:6px;top:6px;bottom:6px;width:2px;background:var(--line);"></div>';

    history.forEach(function(e, idx) {
      const t = (typeof parseCallTimestamp === 'function')
        ? (parseCallTimestamp(e.timestamp) || new Date(e.timestamp))
        : new Date(e.timestamp);
      const hiddenStyle = idx >= maxInitialEvents ? 'display:none;' : '';
      const extraClass = idx >= maxInitialEvents ? ' vx-timeline-extra' : '';
      const whenLabel = vxTimelineDateLabel(t) + (Number.isNaN(t.getTime()) ? '' : ' · ' + formatTimeCH(t) + ' Uhr');

      if (e.type === 'call_inbound') {
        const dur = e.duration || 0;
        const isClickable = !!e.call_id;
        const clickHandler = isClickable
          ? 'onclick="vxOpenCallFromHistory(\'' + e.call_id + '\')" style="cursor:pointer;"'
          : '';
        const hoverStyle = isClickable ? 'background:var(--surface2);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;transition:background .15s;' : 'background:var(--surface2);border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;';

        inner += '<div class="vx-timeline-item' + extraClass + '" style="position:relative;padding-bottom:16px;' + hiddenStyle + '">';
        inner += '<div style="position:absolute;left:-23px;top:4px;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;border:3px solid #1A6FE8;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--surface);"></div>';
        inner += '<div ' + clickHandler + ' style="' + hoverStyle + '">';
        inner += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:3px;">';
        inner += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--slate);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;font-weight:600;">' + whenLabel + '</div>';
        if (isClickable) inner += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:#1A6FE8;font-weight:600;">Detail →</div>';
        inner += '</div>';
        inner += '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);">Eingehender Anruf · ' + Math.floor(dur/60) + ' Min ' + (dur%60) + ' Sek</div>';

        const metaParts = [];
        if (e.category) metaParts.push(vxFormatCategoryLabelSafe(e.category));
        if (e.lead_quality) metaParts.push('Lead: ' + e.lead_quality);
        if (metaParts.length > 0) {
          inner += '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--slate);margin-top:3px;">' + metaParts.join(' · ') + '</div>';
        }
        var _transcriptText = e.transcription || e.transcript || "";
        var _historySummary = (e.summary || '').trim();
        if (!_historySummary && _transcriptText) _historySummary = 'Transkript vorhanden';
        inner += '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2);margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5;">' + (_historySummary || '<em style="color:var(--slate);">Keine Zusammenfassung</em>') + '</div>';
        inner += '</div></div>';
      } else {
        const typeMap = { callback: 'Rückruf', information: 'Offerte', offerte: 'Offerte', appointment: 'Termin', termin: 'Termin', general: 'Aufgabe', task: 'Aufgabe', follow_up: 'Nachfassen' };
        const actionLabel = typeMap[e.action] || typeMap[e.status] || 'Nachfassen';
        const isDone = e.status === 'closed' || e.status === 'resolved' || e.status === 'completed' || e.status === 'done';
        const dueLabel = e.due_at ? formatTaskDueLabel({ due_at: e.due_at }, { emptyLabel: '' }) : '';
        const dueDate = e.due_at ? new Date(e.due_at) : null;
        const isOverdue = dueDate && dueDate < new Date() && !isDone;
        const dotColor = isDone ? '#059669' : (isOverdue ? '#DC2626' : '#D97706');
        const statusBadge = isDone
          ? '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#059669;background:#D1FAE5;border-radius:4px;padding:1px 6px;margin-left:6px;">✓ Erledigt</span>'
          : (isOverdue ? '<span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#DC2626;background:#FEE2E2;border-radius:4px;padding:1px 6px;margin-left:6px;">Überfällig</span>' : '');

        inner += '<div class="vx-timeline-item' + extraClass + '" style="position:relative;padding-bottom:16px;' + hiddenStyle + '">';
        inner += '<div style="position:absolute;left:-23px;top:4px;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;border:3px solid ' + dotColor + ';box-shadow:0 0 0 2px var(--surface);' + (isDone ? 'opacity:.6;' : '') + '"></div>';
        inner += '<div style="background:' + (isDone ? 'var(--surface)' : 'var(--surface2)') + ';border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;' + (isDone ? 'opacity:.75;' : '') + '">';
        inner += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--slate);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:3px;">' + whenLabel + '</div>';
        inner += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;"><span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);">Nachfassen: ' + actionLabel + '</span>' + statusBadge + '</div>';
        if (dueLabel) {
          const dueColor = isOverdue ? '#DC2626' : 'var(--slate)';
          inner += '<div style="font-size:11px;color:' + dueColor + ';margin-top:3px;">Fällig: ' + dueLabel + '</div>';
        }
        const autoTitles = ['Rückruf', 'Offerte', 'Termin', 'Sonstiges', 'Aufgabe', 'Nachfassen'];
        const noteText = (e.notes || '').trim();
        const titleText = (e.title || '').trim();
        const showNote = noteText && !autoTitles.some(function(t) { return noteText === t || titleText === noteText; });
        if (showNote) inner += '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink2);margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5;font-style:italic;">"' + noteText + '"</div>';
        inner += '</div></div>';
      }
    });

    inner += '</div>'; // /timeline
    if (eventCount > maxInitialEvents) {
      inner += '<button type="button" onclick="vxTimelineShowAll(this)" style="margin:2px 0 0 24px;padding:7px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;background:var(--surface);color:var(--blue);font-size:12px;font-weight:700;font-family:inherit;cursor:pointer;">Alle ' + eventCount + ' Ereignisse anzeigen</button>';
    }
    inner += '</div>'; // /padding
  }

  let html = '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-lg);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:14px;box-shadow:0 10px 24px rgba(15,23,42,.035);">';
  html +=   '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--line);">';
  html +=     '<i class="ph-bold ph-clock-counter-clockwise" style="color:#1A6FE8;flex-shrink:0;font-size:16px;" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
  html +=     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#0D1F3C;letter-spacing:-.01em;">Kontakt-Historie</div>';
  html +=     '<span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:10px;font-weight:750;color:#64748B;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;">' + eventCount + ' Ereignis' + (eventCount !== 1 ? 'se' : '') + '</span>';
  html +=   '</div>';
  html +=   '<div style="background:var(--surface);">' + inner + '</div>';
  html += '</div>';

  return html;
}

function vxTimelineShowAll(btn) {
  var card = btn && btn.closest ? btn.closest('div') : null;
  var root = btn && btn.parentNode ? btn.parentNode : document;
  try {
    root.querySelectorAll('.vx-timeline-extra').forEach(function(el) { el.style.display = ''; });
  } catch(e) {}
  if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
}
window.vxTimelineShowAll = vxTimelineShowAll;

// ─── Timeline: Nachfassen als erledigt markieren ────────────────────────────
function vxTimelineMarkDone(btn) {
  var taskId = btn && btn.dataset && btn.dataset.taskId;
  if (!taskId) return;

  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.textContent = '…';

  // recordId in allManualTaskRecords anhand task_entity_id finden
  var tasks = (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : [];
  var taskRecord = tasks.find(function(t) {
    return String((t.fields && t.fields.task_entity_id) || t.id || '') === String(taskId);
  });

  if (!taskRecord) {
    toast('Aufgabe nicht gefunden', 'warning');
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = '✓ Als erledigt markieren';
    return;
  }

  updateManualTaskStatus(taskRecord.id, 'closed').then(function() {
    toast('Nachfassen erledigt', 'success');
    // Timeline neu laden
    var callId = window._vxCurrentCallId;
    if (callId && typeof loadAndRenderCallDetail === 'function') {
      loadAndRenderCallDetail(callId);
    }
    // Abgeschlossen und Archiv bleiben bewusst getrennt. Kein automatisches Archivieren.
    // Liste aktualisieren
    if (typeof loadData === 'function') setTimeout(loadData, 600);
  }).catch(function(err) {
    toast(err.message || 'Fehler beim Erledigen', 'warning');
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = '✓ Als erledigt markieren';
  });
}

function toggleTranscript(id) {
  const e = document.getElementById(id);
  if (e) e.style.display = e.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function saveCallNotes(callId, options) {
  options = options || {};
  var sb = (typeof getSupabaseAuthClient === 'function') ? getSupabaseAuthClient() : null;
  if (!sb) { 
    showToast('⚠️ Authentifizierung erforderlich');
    return Promise.resolve({ error: true });
  }
  
  const notesEl = document.getElementById('call-detail-notes');
  const notes = notesEl ? notesEl.value : '';
  
  // Save-Indicator setzen
  vxSetSaveIndicator('saving', 'Speichern…');
  
  return sb.from('calls').update({ 
    notes_customer_voxera: notes, 
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString() 
  }).eq('id', callId).then(function(r) {
    if (r.error) {
      console.error('saveCallNotes error:', r.error);
      vxSetSaveIndicator('error', 'Fehler beim Speichern');
      if (!options.silent) showToast('⚠️ Fehler beim Speichern');
      return r;
    }
    
    // ─── ELITE: Lokalen Cache aktualisieren ───
    var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
    var rec = recordsSource.find(function(x) { return x.id === callId; });
    if (rec && rec.fields) {
      rec.fields.notes_customer_voxera = notes;
    }
    
    vxSetSaveIndicator('saved', 'Gespeichert');
    if (!options.silent) showToast('✓ Notiz gespeichert');
    
    // Save-Indicator nach 3 Sek. ausblenden
    setTimeout(function() { vxSetSaveIndicator('idle', ''); }, 3000);
    return r;
  });
}
window.saveCallNotes = saveCallNotes;

// Save-Indicator Helper
function vxSetSaveIndicator(state, text) {
  var el = document.getElementById('vx-cd-save-indicator');
  if (!el) return;
  el.className = 'vx-cd-save-indicator ' + (state === 'idle' ? '' : state);
  el.style.opacity = state === 'idle' ? '0' : '1';
  var textEl = el.querySelector('.indicator-text');
  if (textEl) textEl.textContent = text;
}
window.vxSetSaveIndicator = vxSetSaveIndicator;

// ─── Auto-Save für Notizen (debounced) ───
var _vxNotesSaveTimer = null;
var _vxNotesLastValue = '';
function vxSetupNotesAutoSave(callId) {
  var el = document.getElementById('call-detail-notes');
  if (!el || el._vxAutoSaveSetup) return;
  el._vxAutoSaveSetup = true;
  _vxNotesLastValue = el.value;
  
  el.addEventListener('input', function() {
    // Debounce: erst nach 1.2 Sek ohne Tipp-Aktivität speichern
    if (_vxNotesSaveTimer) clearTimeout(_vxNotesSaveTimer);
    vxSetSaveIndicator('saving', 'Tippen…');
    
    _vxNotesSaveTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      if (el.value === _vxNotesLastValue) return;
      _vxNotesLastValue = el.value;
      saveCallNotes(callId, { silent: true });
    }, 1200);
  });
}
window.vxSetupNotesAutoSave = vxSetupNotesAutoSave;

// ═══ QUICK ACTIONS ═══

// Anrufen
function vxCallDetailAnrufen() {
  if (window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task') {
    vxTaskDetailCall(window._vxCurrentTaskId);
    return;
  }
  var callId = window._vxCurrentCallId;
  if (!callId) return;
  var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
  var rec = recordsSource.find(function(r) { return r.id === callId; });
  if (!rec) { showToast('⚠️ Anruf nicht gefunden'); return; }
  var phone = (rec.fields && (rec.fields.caller_phone || rec.fields.phone || rec.fields.tel_link)) || '';
  if (!phone) { showToast('⚠️ Keine Telefonnummer vorhanden'); return; }
  if (typeof vxOpenTelHref === 'function') vxOpenTelHref(phone, 'Keine gültige Telefonnummer vorhanden');
  else window.location.href = 'tel:' + String(phone).replace(/[^+0-9]/g, '');
}
window.vxCallDetailAnrufen = vxCallDetailAnrufen;

// E-Mail (öffnet mailto: mit voraus-gefüllten Daten)
function vxCallDetailMail() {
  if (window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task') {
    vxTaskDetailNotify('Für diese Aufgabe ist keine E-Mail-Aktion hinterlegt.', 'info');
    return;
  }
  var callId = window._vxCurrentCallId;
  if (!callId) return;
  var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
  var rec = recordsSource.find(function(r) { return r.id === callId; });
  if (!rec) { showToast('⚠️ Anruf nicht gefunden'); return; }
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  var email = f.caller_email || '';
  var name = f.caller_name || '';
  var subject = encodeURIComponent('Rückmeldung zu Ihrem Anruf');
  var body = encodeURIComponent('Guten Tag' + (name ? ' ' + name : '') + ',\n\nvielen Dank für Ihren Anruf.\n\n');
  if (email) {
    window.location.href = 'mailto:' + email + '?subject=' + subject + '&body=' + body;
  } else {
    showToast('Keine E-Mail-Adresse vorhanden — öffne leere E-Mail');
    window.location.href = 'mailto:?subject=' + subject + '&body=' + body;
  }
}
window.vxCallDetailMail = vxCallDetailMail;

// [v2.66] Fälligkeit ändern — routet korrekt für Call und Task
function vxCallDetailFaelligkeit(options) {
  var id = window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task'
    ? window._vxCurrentTaskId
    : window._vxCurrentCallId;
  if (!id) return;
  if (typeof openFollowupDirect === 'function') openFollowupDirect(id, options || null);
}
window.vxCallDetailFaelligkeit = vxCallDetailFaelligkeit;

function vxCallDetailCreateTask(recordOrId) {
  var id = recordOrId || (window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task'
    ? window._vxCurrentTaskId
    : window._vxCurrentCallId);
  if (!id) return;
  if (typeof openManualTaskModal === 'function') openManualTaskModal();
}
window.vxCallDetailCreateTask = vxCallDetailCreateTask;

// Detail-Page: Anfrage archivieren
function vxCallDetailArchivieren() {
  var id = window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task'
    ? window._vxCurrentTaskId
    : window._vxCurrentCallId;
  if (!id) {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('⚠️ Eintrag nicht gefunden');
    else if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Eintrag nicht gefunden');
    return;
  }

  openConfirmModal({
    title: 'Anfrage archivieren?',
    text: 'Diese Anfrage wird aus der normalen Ansicht ausgeblendet und bleibt im Archiv auffindbar.',
    confirmLabel: 'Archivieren',
    loadingLabel: 'Archiviere...',
    tone: 'primary',
    onConfirm: async function() {
      var result = false;
      if (typeof doStatusChange === 'function') {
        result = await doStatusChange(id, CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED, null);
      }
      if (result !== false) {
        try {
          var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
          var rec = recordsSource.find(function(r) { return String(r && r.id) === String(id); });
          if (rec && rec.fields) rec.fields.dashboard_status = CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED;
        } catch(e) {}
        if (typeof vxRefreshOperationalViews === 'function') vxRefreshOperationalViews(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
        if (typeof vxScheduleSingleDataRefresh === 'function') vxScheduleSingleDataRefresh(350);
      }
      return result;
    }
  });
}
window.vxCallDetailArchivieren = vxCallDetailArchivieren;

// [v2.66] Expand — öffnet Split-Panel-Detail als Fullscreen
function vxCallDetailExpand() {
  var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  var splitRight = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right');
  var splitEmpty = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-empty');
  if (!page) return;

  var isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;
  if (isMobile) return; // Mobile ist bereits Fokusansicht.

  // useDetailV2-Modus: detailV2El ist Host, call-detail-page ist nicht in splitRight
  var _detailV2El = document.getElementById('requests-detail-v2');
  var _isDetailV2Active = !!(_detailV2El && _detailV2El.style.display === 'block');
  if (_isDetailV2Active && !(page.classList.contains('is-fullscreen') || page.classList.contains('vx-detail-focus'))) {
    var _currentId = String(window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || '').trim();
    if (_currentId) {
      if (window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task' && typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') {
        showTaskDetail(_currentId, { forceFullscreen: true });
      } else if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') {
        showCallDetail(_currentId, { forceFullscreen: true });
      }
    }
    return;
  }

  // Toggle: Im Fokusmodus zurück in die Split-Ansicht, ohne den geöffneten Datensatz zu verlieren.
  if (page.classList.contains('is-fullscreen') || page.classList.contains('vx-detail-focus')) {
    var currentId = String(window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || page.dataset.vxDetailId || '').trim();
    var anrufeTabActive = document.getElementById('tab-anrufe') && document.getElementById('tab-anrufe').classList.contains('active');
    window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId = currentId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || '';

    // Wenn die Detailseite aus „Heute / Jetzt wichtig“ geöffnet wurde, existiert noch keine Split-Spalte.
    // Reduzieren bedeutet dann: zum Anfragen-Tab wechseln und denselben Eintrag dort in der Split View öffnen.
    if (!anrufeTabActive && currentId && typeof showTab === 'function') {
      document.body.classList.remove('vx-requests-focus-mode');
      page.classList.remove('vx-detail-focus');
      page.classList.remove('is-fullscreen');
      var nav = document.getElementById('nav-anrufe') || document.getElementById('mnav-anrufe');
      showTab('anrufe', nav, { historyMode: 'push' });
      return;
    }

    document.body.classList.remove('vx-requests-focus-mode');
    page.classList.remove('vx-detail-focus');
    page.classList.remove('is-fullscreen');
    if (currentId && window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task' && typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') {
      showTaskDetail(currentId, { fromBackStack: true });
    } else if (currentId && typeof showCallDetail === 'function') {
      showCallDetail(currentId, { fromBackStack: true });
    } else if (typeof hideCallDetail === 'function') {
      hideCallDetail();
    }
    return;
  }

  if (splitRight && splitRight.contains(page)) {
    document.body.appendChild(page);
    document.body.classList.add('vx-requests-focus-mode');
    page.classList.add('vx-detail-focus');
    page.style.position = 'fixed';
    page.style.top = '0';
    page.style.left = '232px';
    page.style.right = '0';
    page.style.bottom = '0';
    page.style.zIndex = '9999';
    page.style.overflow = '';
    page.style.overflowY = 'auto';
    page.style.overflowX = 'hidden';
    page.classList.add('is-open');
    page.classList.add('is-fullscreen');
    page.style.display = 'block';

    var sw = document.getElementById('call-detail-scroll-wrap');
    if (sw) {
      sw.style.overflow = '';
      sw.style.overflowY = '';
      sw.style.overflowX = '';
      sw.style.height = '';
      sw.style.width = '';
      sw.style.minHeight = '100%';
    }
    if (splitEmpty) splitEmpty.style.display = '';

    var backBtn = page.querySelector('.vx-back-btn[aria-label="Zurück"]');
    if (backBtn) {
      backBtn.style.display = '';
      backBtn.title = 'Zurück zur Liste';
    }
    var expandBtn = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-expand');
    if (expandBtn) {
      expandBtn.classList.add('vxdr-focus-toggle');
      expandBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Zur Split-Ansicht wechseln');
      expandBtn.title = 'Zur Split-Ansicht wechseln';
      expandBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-bold ph-arrows-in" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
      expandBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
      expandBtn.style.visibility = 'visible';
      expandBtn.style.opacity = '1';
    }
    vxSetScrollLock('call-detail-page', true);
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (typeof vxSetupMorphingBanner === 'function') vxSetupMorphingBanner();
    }, 50);
  }
}
window.vxCallDetailExpand = vxCallDetailExpand;


// Als erledigt markieren
function vxCallDetailErledigt() {
  if (window._vxCurrentDetailType === 'task') {
    vxTaskDetailDone(window._vxCurrentTaskId);
    return;
  }
  var callId = window._vxCurrentCallId;
  if (!callId) return;
  if (typeof vxGuardDoneAction === 'function' && vxGuardDoneAction(callId, 'detail.done')) return;
  var btn = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done');
  if (btn && btn.classList.contains('is-success')) return;
  if (btn && btn.classList.contains('is-loading')) return; // bereits in Bearbeitung
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('is-loading');

  // [PATCH v2.40] is-loading immer entfernen wenn Modal abgebrochen wird
  function resetBtn() {
    var b = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done');
    if (b) b.classList.remove('is-loading');
  }

  if (typeof confirmTaskDone === 'function') {
    // Wir wrappen onSuccess und fügen onCancel hinzu via Modal-Observer
    var _origOpen = window.openConfirmModal;
    confirmTaskDone(callId, null, 'call-detail-quick-action', function() {
      var b = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done');
      if (b) {
        b.classList.remove('is-loading');
        b.classList.add('is-success');
      }
      toast('Als erledigt markiert', 'success');
      var recordsSource = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
      var rec = recordsSource.find(function(r) { return r.id === callId; });
      if (rec && rec.fields) rec.fields.dashboard_status = 'closed';
      if (typeof vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion === 'function') vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion();
    });
    // Safety-Net: nach 10s is-loading entfernen falls Modal geschlossen ohne Callback
    setTimeout(resetBtn, 10000);
  } else {
    var sb = (typeof getSupabaseAuthClient === 'function') ? getSupabaseAuthClient() : null;
    if (!sb) {
      resetBtn();
      showToast('⚠️ Authentifizierung erforderlich');
      return;
    }
    sb.from('calls').update({ dashboard_status: 'closed', updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', callId).then(function(r) {
      resetBtn();
      if (r.error) {
        showToast('⚠️ Fehler beim Speichern');
      } else {
        var b = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done');
        if (b) b.classList.add('is-success');
        showToast('✓ Als erledigt markiert');
        setTimeout(function() { if (typeof hideCallDetail === 'function') hideCallDetail(); }, 1200);
      }
    });
  }
}
window.vxCallDetailErledigt = vxCallDetailErledigt;

function saveFollowUp(callId) {
  var sb = (typeof getSupabaseAuthClient === 'function') ? getSupabaseAuthClient() : null;
  if (!sb) { showToast('⚠️ Authentifizierung erforderlich'); return; }
  const call = (state && state.calls) ? state.calls.find(function(c){ return c.id === callId; }) : null;
  if (!call) { showToast('⚠️ Anruf nicht gefunden'); return; }
  const callback = document.getElementById('followup-callback') && document.getElementById('followup-callback').checked;
  const email = document.getElementById('followup-email') && document.getElementById('followup-email').checked;
  const task = document.getElementById('followup-task') && document.getElementById('followup-task').checked;
  const dueDateInput = document.getElementById('followup-date') ? document.getElementById('followup-date').value : '';
  if (!callback && !email && !task) { showToast('⚠️ Bitte mindestens eine Aktion wählen'); return; }
  if (!dueDateInput) { showToast('⚠️ Bitte Datum/Uhrzeit wählen'); return; }
  // Determine task type (callback > email > task)
  const taskType = callback ? 'callback' : (email ? 'email' : 'task');
  const taskTitle = callback ? 'Rückruf' : (email ? 'E-Mail senden' : 'Aufgabe');
  
  // customer_tasks DB schema: id, customer_id, type, title, note, phone, due_at, status, created_at
  const legacyFollowUpValue = normalizeFollowUpLocalValue(dueDateInput) || String(dueDateInput || '').trim();
  const legacyFollowUpDueAt = legacyFollowUpValue.slice(0, 10);
  const legacyFollowUpDueTime = normalizeManualTaskDueTime(legacyFollowUpValue.slice(11, 16));
  if (!legacyFollowUpDueAt || !legacyFollowUpDueTime) { showToast('⚠️ Bitte Datum und Uhrzeit wählen'); return; }
  const taskData = {
    customer_id: call.customer_id,
    phone: call.caller_phone || call.phone_number || '',
    type: taskType,
    title: taskTitle + (call.caller_name ? ': ' + call.caller_name : ''),
    note: '',
    due_at: legacyFollowUpDueAt,
    due_time: legacyFollowUpDueTime,
    status: 'new'
  };
  sb.from('customer_tasks').insert([taskData]).then(function(r) {
    if (r.error) {
      showToast('⚠️ Fehler beim Speichern');
    } else {
      showToast('✓ Nachfassen gespeichert');
      ['followup-callback', 'followup-email', 'followup-task'].forEach(function(id) {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.checked = false;
      });
      const dateEl = document.getElementById('followup-date');
      if (dateEl) dateEl.value = '';

      // [PATCH 2026-05-13 v2.3 — Rückruf-Konsolidierung (Weg 2: DB-seitig)]
      // Wenn der ursprüngliche Anruf "callback_requested=true" hatte UND
      // eine konkretere Folge-Aufgabe angelegt wurde (Offerte/Termin/Info/Task),
      // wird der Rückruf-Status am Anruf ausgeschaltet — die Folge-Aufgabe
      // übernimmt jetzt die operative Verantwortung. Damit verschwindet die
      // Doppelzählung (1x als Rückruf am Call + 1x als Folge-Aufgabe).
      //
      // Bedingung: nur ausschalten wenn die Folge-Aufgabe KEIN Rückruf ist
      //   (sonst würden wir uns selbst widersprechen).
      var isCallbackFollowup = (taskType === 'callback');
      var hadCallbackRequest = (call.callback_requested === true || call.callback_requested === 'true');
      if (!isCallbackFollowup && hadCallbackRequest) {
        sb.from('calls')
          .update({ callback_requested: false })
          .eq('id', call.id)
          .then(function(rr) {
            if (rr.error) {
              console.warn('[v2.3] callback_requested-Reset fehlgeschlagen:', rr.error);
            } else {
              // Lokal sofort aktualisieren, damit UI sich richtig zeigt
              if (call.fields) call.fields.callback_requested = false;
              call.callback_requested = false;
              // UI neu rendern
              if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function' && typeof allRecords !== 'undefined') {
                try { renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords); } catch(e) {}
              }
              if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function' && typeof allRecords !== 'undefined') {
                try { renderAnrufe(); } catch(e) {}
              }
              // [PATCH v2.44] Assistent-Tab Init
              if (tabId === 'assistent') {
                setTimeout(function() {
                  if (typeof vxInitAssistentRufumleitung === 'function') vxInitAssistentRufumleitung();
                }, 50);
              }
            }
          });
      }
    }
  });
}

function toggleBrowserNotif() {
  if (!('Notification' in window)) { toast('Browser unterstützt keine Push-Benachrichtigungen.', 'warning'); return; }
  if (Notification.permission === 'denied') {
    toast('Benachrichtigungen sind im Browser blockiert. Bitte in den Browser-Einstellungen erlauben.', 'warning');
    return;
  }
  var off = localStorage.getItem('voxera_browser_notif_off') === '1';
  if (!off && Notification.permission === 'granted') {
    // Ausschalten
    vxSafeLocalStorageSet('voxera_browser_notif_off', '1');
    updateBrowserNotifToggleUI();
    return;
  }
  // Einschalten — Permission anfragen falls noch nicht
  ensureNotificationPermission().then(function(perm) {
    if (perm === 'granted') {
      vxSafeLocalStorageSet('voxera_browser_notif_off', '0');
      updateBrowserNotifToggleUI();
      toast('Browser-Benachrichtigungen aktiviert.');
    } else {
      toast('Berechtigung nicht erteilt. Bitte im Browser erlauben.', 'warning');
      updateBrowserNotifToggleUI();
    }
  });
}

function showIncomingBanner(count, newRecords) {
  const el = document.getElementById('incoming-banner');
  const textEl = document.getElementById('incoming-banner-text');
  const subEl = document.getElementById('incoming-banner-sub');
  if (!el) return;

  // Title
  const label = count === 1 ? 'Neuer Anruf' : (count + ' neue Anrufe');
  if (textEl) textEl.textContent = label;

  // Sub-line: show caller + duration for single call, count for multiple
  if (subEl) {
    if (count === 1 && newRecords && newRecords[0]) {
      var f = newRecords[0].fields || {};
      var caller = getCallDisplayName(f) || f.phone_number || f.caller_phone || 'Unbekannte Nummer';
      var dur = f.duration_seconds ? formatDurationForDisplay(f.duration_seconds) : null;
      subEl.textContent = dur ? (caller + ' · ' + dur) : caller;
    } else {
      subEl.textContent = 'Zum Anzeigen tippen';
    }
  }

  el.classList.add('show');
  if (incomingBannerTimer) clearTimeout(incomingBannerTimer);
  incomingBannerTimer = setTimeout(function() { el.classList.remove('show'); }, 8000);
  if (count === 1 && newRecords && newRecords[0]) vxMaybePlayLiveCallSound(newRecords[0], 'incoming-banner');
}

function handleIncomingBannerClick() {
  // Dismiss banner
  var el = document.getElementById('incoming-banner');
  if (el) el.classList.remove('show');
  if (incomingBannerTimer) { clearTimeout(incomingBannerTimer); incomingBannerTimer = null; }

  if (_incomingBannerRecordIds.length === 1) {
    // Single call: open detail directly
    var recordId = _incomingBannerRecordIds[0];
    var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-anrufe');
    showTab('anrufe', navEl);
    // Small delay to let tab render before opening detail
    setTimeout(function() { if(typeof vxOpenDetailV2==='function') vxOpenDetailV2(recordId); else openDetail(recordId); }, 120);
  } else {
    // Multiple: go to Anfragen tab
    var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-anrufe');
    showTab('anrufe', navEl);
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  LOAD DATA
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
async function loadData() {
  // ── Concurrency Guard ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Verhindert parallele loadData-Laeufe (Realtime + Polling + focus + confirm
  // koennen gleichzeitig feuern). Ohne Guard ueberschreibt der langsamere Lauf
  // customerMeta / candidateCall des schnelleren -> State Drift.
  // Wichtig: nicht hart verwerfen sondern "pending" merken und nach Abschluss
  // einmal wiederholen. Sonst werden Polling-Aufrufe waehrend pending_test
  // komplett verschluckt -> candidateCall wird nie gesetzt -> Auto-Confirm
  // laeuft nie durch.
  if (loadDataInFlight) {
    loadDataPending = true;
    return;
  }
  loadDataInFlight = true;
  loadDataPending = false;

  const btn = document.getElementById('refresh-btn');
  if (btn) { btn.textContent = '↻ Lädt...'; btn.style.opacity = '.6'; }

  try {
    await loadCustomerMeta().catch(()=>{});
    applyContractAccessStateToUI();
    await loadPlanConfigFromSupabase().catch(()=>{});
    const context = await resolveCustomerContext(true);
    setupDashboardRealtime((context && context.customerId) || '');

    // ─── ACCESS GUARD ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if (!(await ensureActiveCustomerOrLogout(context))) return;
