    // ─── END ACCESS GUARD ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const records = await loadCallRecords(context.customerId || '');
    const manualTasks = await loadManualTaskRecords(context.customerId || '');

    flagIncomingRecords(records);
    // Activation-Test-Calls aus der Dashboard-Anzeige ausschliessen.
    // activation_test_outbound und activation_test_inbound sind interne
    // System-Calls und sollen nicht in Anfragen/Verlauf/Dashboard erscheinen.
    // allRecordsRaw includes test calls for polling detection
    allRecordsRaw = records;
    allRecords = records.filter(function(r) {
      return !isActivationCallCategory((r.fields || {}).category);
    });
    allManualTaskRecords = manualTasks;
    window.allRecords = allRecords;
    window.allManualTaskRecords = allManualTaskRecords;
    _vxNotifDataReady = true;
    if (_vxNotifLoaded) {
      vxBellUpdateBadge();
      if (_vxBellOpen) { vxBellRender(); vxBellPageRender(); }
    }

    const titleEl = document.getElementById('greeting-main');
    if (titleEl) {
      var firstName = customerMeta.contactFirstName || (customerMeta.customerName ? customerMeta.customerName.split(' ')[0] : '') || '';
      titleEl.textContent = getTimeBasedGreeting() + (firstName ? ', ' + firstName : '');
      titleEl.dataset.custom = '1';
    }

    renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderAnrufe();
    renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderArchiv(allRecords);
    if (typeof updateLiveHero === 'function') updateLiveHero(allRecords);
    if (typeof renderAuswertung === 'function') renderAuswertung();
    applyContractAccessStateToUI();
    updateLastUpdatedLabel();

    // [legacy] Candidate-call activation polling removed — checkTestCall handles testing now

    // ── Atomarer Loader→Content Übergang ─────────────────────────────────────
    // dash-loading und dash-content werden in einer einzigen synchronen
    // Operation getauscht. Vorher: zwei separate Operationen mit einem Frame
    // Lücke dazwischen → leerer Screen / Flicker.
    // Zusätzlich: Content nur zeigen wenn customerMeta vollständig geladen
    // (customerId vorhanden) – verhindert '–' Flicker bei Voxera-Nummer/Tel.
    routeDashboardFirstSessionExperience();
    safeMountActivationV2({ reason: 'loadData', softGate: false });
    updateSidebarStatus();
    // Loader erst nach allen Render-Operationen verstecken – atomar
    var loadingEl = document.getElementById('dash-loading');
    if (loadingEl) loadingEl.style.display = 'none';

  } catch(e) {
    const rawMsg = String((e && e.message) || 'Unbekannter Fehler');
    const lower = rawMsg.toLowerCase();
    let uiMsg = rawMsg;

    if (lower.includes('airtable')) {
      uiMsg = 'Alte Version erkannt (Legacy Airtable Fehler). Bitte in Netlify "Clear cache and deploy site" ausführen und danach Hard-Reload (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+R).';
    }
    // Legacy compatibility: older cached deployments can still return stale backend error texts.
    else if (lower.includes('401') || lower.includes('unauthorized')) {
      uiMsg = 'Daten konnten nicht geladen werden. Bitte neu deployen und Seite mit Hard-Reload öffnen (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+R).';
    } else if (lower.includes('permission') || lower.includes('rls')) {
      uiMsg = 'Keine Berechtigung für diese Daten (RLS). Bitte Benutzer-Zuordnung in Supabase prüfen.';
    }

    const _loadingEl = document.getElementById('dash-loading');
    if (_loadingEl) _loadingEl.innerHTML =
      `<div class="error-banner">Fehler beim Laden: ${uiMsg}<br><small>Bitte Seite neu laden.</small></div>`;
  } finally {
    // Concurrency Guard: freigeben und pending-Run nachholen falls noetig
    loadDataInFlight = false;
    if (btn) { btn.textContent = '↻ Aktualisieren'; btn.style.opacity = '1'; }
    if (loadDataPending) {
      loadDataPending = false;
      // Minimalverzoegerung damit Browser-Event-Loop nicht blockiert wird
      setTimeout(function() { loadData().catch(function(){}); }, 50);
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  BENACHRICHTIGUNGSTON (Warm Bell)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
var _audioCtx = null;
window.vxPlayedLiveSoundIds = window.vxPlayedLiveSoundIds || new Set();
function getAudioCtx() {
  if (!_audioCtx) _audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
  return _audioCtx;
}

function playNotifSound() {
  if (localStorage.getItem('voxera_sound_off') === '1') return;
  var type = localStorage.getItem('voxera_sound_type') || 'warm';
  previewSound(type);
}

function vxMaybePlayLiveCallSound(record, source) {
  try {
    var stableId = vxGetCallStableId(record);
    if (!stableId) return false;
    if (!vxIsLiveCall(record)) return false;
    if (window.vxPlayedLiveSoundIds.has(stableId)) return false;
    window.vxPlayedLiveSoundIds.add(stableId);
    playNotifSound();
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[live-sound] failed', source || 'unknown', e);
    return false;
  }
}

function toggleNotifSound() {
  var off = localStorage.getItem('voxera_sound_off') === '1';
  var newOff = !off;
  vxSafeLocalStorageSet('voxera_sound_off', newOff ? '1' : '0');
  updateSoundToggleUI(newOff);
  if (!newOff) playNotifSound();
}

function updateSoundToggleUI(isOff) {
  var toggle = document.getElementById('sound-toggle');
  var knob = document.getElementById('sound-toggle-knob');
  if (!toggle || !knob) return;
  if (isOff) {
    toggle.style.background = '#D1D5DB';
    knob.style.left = '3px';
  } else {
    toggle.style.background = '#1A6FE8';
    knob.style.left = '22px';
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  HEUTE KPI MODEL — Launch cockpit
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, keys) {
  var r = record || {};
  var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
  for (var i = 0; i < (keys || []).length; i++) {
    var k = keys[i];
    if (r[k] !== undefined && r[k] !== null && r[k] !== '') return r[k];
    if (f[k] !== undefined && f[k] !== null && f[k] !== '') return f[k];
  }
  return '';
}
function vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, keys) {
  var v = vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, keys || ['created_at']);
  if (!v && typeof getRecordTimestamp === 'function') v = getRecordTimestamp(record);
  return v || '';
}
function vxHeuteParseDate(value) {
  if (!value) return null;
  var d;
  try { d = (value instanceof Date) ? value : new Date(value); } catch(e) { return null; }
  if (!d || Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  return d;
}
function vxHeuteIsToday(value) {
  var d = vxHeuteParseDate(value);
  if (!d) return false;
  var now = new Date();
  return d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() && d.getMonth() === now.getMonth() && d.getDate() === now.getDate();
}
function vxHeuteIsPastOrNow(value) {
  var d = vxHeuteParseDate(value);
  return !!(d && d.getTime() <= Date.now());
}
function vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(record) {
  var category = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['category']) || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  var direction = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['direction','call_direction']) || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (direction === 'outbound') return true;
  if (category === 'activation_test_outbound' || category === 'activation_test_inbound') return true;
  if (typeof isActivationCallCategory === 'function' && isActivationCallCategory(category)) return true;
  return false;
}
function vxHeuteIsManualTask(record) {
  try { if (typeof isManualTaskRecord === 'function' && isManualTaskRecord(record)) return true; } catch(e) {}
  var type = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['type','record_type','source']) || '').toLowerCase();
  return type.indexOf('task') !== -1 || !!vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['due_at','owner','assignee','task_type']);
}
function vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(record) {
  var status = vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['dashboard_status','status','state']);
  if (typeof isClosedStatus === 'function' && isClosedStatus(status)) return true;
  var norm = (typeof vxNormalizeDashboardStatus === 'function') ? vxNormalizeDashboardStatus(status) : String(status||'').toLowerCase();
  return ['done','completed','complete','erledigt','abgeschlossen','archived','archiviert','closed','resolved'].indexOf(norm) !== -1;
}
function vxHeuteRecordCreatedToday(record) {
  return vxHeuteIsToday(vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['created_at','inserted_at']));
}
function vxHeuteRecordCompletedToday(record) {
  if (!vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(record)) return false;
  // Primary: real completion timestamps.
  var explicit = vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['completed_at','closed_at','done_at','archived_at','resolved_at']);
  if (explicit) return vxHeuteIsToday(explicit);
  // Fallback for legacy rows without completion timestamp:
  // only count updated_at/created_at if the item is already in a done status.
  // This mirrors the visible "Heute" grouping for completed calls/tasks more reliably.
  var updated = vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['updated_at']);
  if (updated && vxHeuteIsToday(updated)) return true;
  var created = vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['created_at','inserted_at']);
  return !!created && vxHeuteIsToday(created);
}
function vxHeuteGetDueValue(record) {
  var f = (record && record.fields) || {};
  if (typeof resolveTaskDueAt === 'function') {
    try {
      var resolved = resolveTaskDueAt(f);
      if (resolved) return resolved;
    } catch(e) {}
  }
  return vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['due_at','follow_up_at','followup_at','callback_at','planned_at','scheduled_at']);
}
function vxHeuteIsCallbackOpen(record) {
  if (!record || vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(record) || vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(record)) return false;
  var callbackRequested = vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['callback_requested']);
  var category = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['category','task_type','type','title']) || '').toLowerCase();
  var due = vxHeuteGetDueValue(record);
  if (callbackRequested === true || String(callbackRequested).toLowerCase() === 'true') return true;
  if (vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['callback_at'])) return true;
  if ((category.indexOf('rückruf') !== -1 || category.indexOf('rueckruf') !== -1 || category.indexOf('callback') !== -1 || category.indexOf('follow') !== -1 || category.indexOf('nachfass') !== -1) && due) return true;
  return false;
}
function vxHeuteIsImportantNowRecord(record) {
  if (!record || vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(record) || vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(record)) return false;
  var lead = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['lead_quality','leadQuality']) || '').toLowerCase();
  var priority = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['priority','urgency']) || '').toLowerCase();
  var due = vxHeuteGetDueValue(record);
  if (lead.indexOf('hot') !== -1 || lead.indexOf('warm') !== -1) return true;
  if (['high','critical','urgent','dringend','hoch'].indexOf(priority) !== -1) return true;
  if (vxHeuteIsCallbackOpen(record)) return true;
  if (due && vxHeuteIsPastOrNow(due)) return true;
  return false;
}
function vxHeuteDedupeByStableId(records) {
  var liveIds = (typeof vxGetLiveCallIdSet === 'function') ? vxGetLiveCallIdSet(records || []) : new Set();
  var seen = new Set();
  return (records || []).filter(function(record) {
    if (!record) return false;
    if (vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(record)) return false;
    var id = (typeof vxGetCallStableId === 'function') ? vxGetCallStableId(record) : String(record.id || vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['id','call_id']) || '');
    if (id && liveIds && liveIds.has(id) && typeof vxIsLiveCall === 'function' && !vxIsLiveCall(record)) return false;
    if (id && seen.has(id)) return false;
    if (id) seen.add(id);
    return true;
  });
}
function vxGetHeuteKpiCounts(records, manualTasks) {
  var callRecords = vxHeuteDedupeByStableId(records || []).filter(function(r){
    return !vxHeuteIsManualTask(r) && (typeof isCustomerVisibleCall !== 'function' || isCustomerVisibleCall(r));
  });
  var tasks = (manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []).filter(Boolean);
  var combined = callRecords.concat(tasks);
  var counts = {
    callsToday: callRecords.filter(vxHeuteRecordCreatedToday).length,
    callbacksOpen: combined.filter(vxHeuteIsCallbackOpen).length,
    importantNow: combined.filter(vxHeuteIsImportantNowRecord).length,
    doneToday: combined.filter(vxHeuteRecordCompletedToday).length
  };
  if (typeof vxDebugLog === 'function') {
    vxDebugLog('[heute:kpis]', {
      total: combined.length,
      counts: counts,
      sampleShape: combined.slice(0, 3).map(function(r){
        return {
          id: String((r && (r.id || vxHeuteGetRecordValue(r, ['id','call_id']))) || ''),
          hasFields: !!(r && r.fields),
          status: vxHeuteGetRecordValue(r, ['dashboard_status','status']),
          created_at: !!vxHeuteGetTimestamp(r, ['created_at']),
          due: !!vxHeuteGetDueValue(r)
        };
      })
    });
  }
  return counts;
}
function vxSetHeuteKpiCell(cell, interactive, handlerName) {
  if (!cell) return;
  cell.classList.toggle('is-interactive', !!interactive);
  cell.classList.toggle('is-static', !interactive);
  cell.style.cursor = interactive ? 'pointer' : 'default';
  cell.onclick = interactive && handlerName ? function(ev) {
    if (ev) { ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation(); }
    if (typeof window[handlerName] === 'function') window[handlerName]();
  } : null;
}
function vxGoToAnfragenFilter(filter, subfilter) {
  window._anrufeSearchQuery = '';
  window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly = false;
  if (typeof vxCloseFloatingRowMenu === 'function') vxCloseFloatingRowMenu();
  if (typeof showTab === 'function') showTab('anrufe', document.getElementById('nav-anrufe'), { historyMode: 'push' });
  setTimeout(function(){
    _anrufeFilter = filter || 'alle';
    if (subfilter) _anrufeInboxSubfilter = subfilter;
    var search = document.getElementById('fb-anrufe-input');
    if (search) search.value = '';
    var filterBtns = document.querySelectorAll('#anrufe-split-left .vx-split-filters button');
    filterBtns.forEach(function(b){
      var active = b.getAttribute('data-filter') === _anrufeFilter;
      b.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', active);
      b.classList.toggle('vxfb-chip--active', active);
    });
    var subWrap = document.getElementById('anrufe-inbox-subfilters');
    if (subWrap) {
      subWrap.style.display = _anrufeFilter === 'offen' ? '' : 'none';
      subWrap.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(b){
        var active = b.getAttribute('data-inbox-subfilter') === _anrufeInboxSubfilter;
        b.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', active);
      });
    }
    if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
  }, 0);
}
function vxHeuteScrollToSection(sectionId) {
  window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly = false;
  if (typeof vxCloseFloatingRowMenu === 'function') vxCloseFloatingRowMenu();
  var navEl = document.getElementById('nav-dashboard') || document.getElementById('mnav-dashboard');
  if (typeof showTab === 'function') showTab('dashboard', navEl, { historyMode: 'push' });
  setTimeout(function(){
    var target = document.getElementById(sectionId);
    if (target && target.scrollIntoView) target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
  }, 0);
}
// KPI 1: Anrufe heute → Anfragen Tab, alle heutigen Anrufe
function vxHeuteKpiOpenTodayCalls() {
  window._anrufeSearchQuery = '';
  if (typeof vxCloseFloatingRowMenu === 'function') vxCloseFloatingRowMenu();
  if (typeof showTab === 'function') showTab('anrufe', document.getElementById('nav-anrufe'), { historyMode: 'push' });
  setTimeout(function(){
    window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly = true;
    _anrufeFilter = 'offen';
    var filterBtns = document.querySelectorAll('#anrufe-split-left .vx-split-filters button');
    filterBtns.forEach(function(b){ b.classList.remove('vx-chip--active','vxfb-chip--active'); });
    var offenBtn = document.querySelector('#anrufe-split-left button[data-filter="offen"]');
    if (offenBtn) { offenBtn.classList.add('vx-chip--active','vxfb-chip--active'); }
    if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
  }, 0);
}
// KPI 2: Offene Anfragen → Anfragen Tab, Filter Offen
function vxHeuteKpiOpenPlanned() { vxGoToAnfragenFilter('offen'); }
// KPI 3: Wichtig jetzt → bleibt auf Heute, scrollt zu Aufmerksamkeit
function vxHeuteKpiScrollImportant() { vxHeuteScrollToSection('dash-priority-section'); }
// KPI 4: Erledigt heute → Anfragen Tab, Filter Abgeschlossen
function vxHeuteKpiOpenDone() { vxGoToAnfragenFilter('abgeschlossen', 'all'); }
// Compat aliases
function vxHeuteKpiOpenInbox() { vxGoToAnfragenFilter('offen'); }

function vxHeuteFormatRowTime(record) {
  var ts = vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['completed_at','closed_at','done_at','archived_at','resolved_at','updated_at','created_at','inserted_at']);
  var d = vxHeuteParseDate(ts);
  if (!d) return '';
  try { return 'Heute, ' + TIME_FORMAT_CH.format(d); } catch(e) {
    return 'Heute, ' + String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  }
}
function vxHeuteRecordTitle(record) {
  try { if (typeof vxDashResolveWorkTitle === 'function') return vxDashResolveWorkTitle(record); } catch(e) {}
  return vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['caller_name','name','company_name','title','phone','caller_phone','phone_number']) || 'Unbekannter Eintrag';
}
function vxHeuteRecordSummary(record) {
  var raw = vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['call_summary','summary','note','notes','transcript']) || '';
  raw = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return '';
  return raw.length > 72 ? raw.slice(0, 69).trim() + '…' : raw;
}
function vxHeuteRecordTypeLabel(record) {
  if (vxHeuteIsManualTask(record)) return 'Aufgabe';
  if (vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['callback_requested']) === true || String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['callback_requested'])).toLowerCase() === 'true') return 'Rückruf';
  return 'Anruf';
}

function vxHeuteActivityTimestamp(record) {
  return vxHeuteGetTimestamp(record, ['completed_at','closed_at','done_at','archived_at','resolved_at','updated_at','created_at','inserted_at']);
}
function vxHeuteActivityTime(record) {
  var d = vxHeuteParseDate(vxHeuteActivityTimestamp(record));
  if (!d) return '';
  try { return TIME_FORMAT_CH.format(d); } catch(e) { return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2,'0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0'); }
}
function vxHeuteActivityLabel(record) {
  var done = typeof vxInquiryMatchesFilter === 'function' ? vxInquiryMatchesFilter(record, 'abgeschlossen') : vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(record);
  if (done) return 'Erledigt';
  if (vxHeuteIsManualTask(record)) return 'Aufgabe aktualisiert';
  var cb = vxHeuteGetRecordValue(record, ['callback_requested']);
  if (cb === true || String(cb).toLowerCase() === 'true') return 'Rückruf erkannt';
  return 'Anruf eingegangen';
}
function vxHeuteRenderActivityList(records) {
  var section = document.getElementById('dash-activity-section');
  var list = document.getElementById('dash-activity-list');
  var countEl = document.getElementById('dash-activity-count');
  if (!section || !list) return;
  var rows = vxHeuteDedupeByStableId(records || []).filter(Boolean).sort(function(a,b){
    return (new Date(vxHeuteActivityTimestamp(b) || 0).getTime() || 0) - (new Date(vxHeuteActivityTimestamp(a) || 0).getTime() || 0);
  }).slice(0, 25);
  section.style.display = '';
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = String(rows.length || 0);
  if (!rows.length) {
    vxSetHtmlIfChanged(list, '<div class="dash-empty">Heute gibt es noch keinen Verlauf.</div>');
    return;
  }
  var html = rows.map(function(rec){
    var id = String((rec && rec.id) || vxHeuteGetRecordValue(rec, ['id','call_id']) || '');
    var label = vxHeuteActivityLabel(rec);
    var title = vxHeuteRecordTitle(rec);
    var summary = vxHeuteRecordSummary(rec);
    var time = vxHeuteActivityTime(rec);
    var isDone = label === 'Erledigt';
    var icon = isDone ? 'check-circle' : (label.indexOf('Rückruf') >= 0 ? 'bell-ringing' : (vxHeuteIsManualTask(rec) ? 'calendar-check' : 'phone-incoming'));
    var iconClass = isDone ? 'vx-activity-icon is-done' : (!vxHeuteIsManualTask(rec) ? 'vx-activity-icon is-call' : 'vx-activity-icon');
    return '<div class="vx-activity-row" data-id="'+_esc(id)+'" data-record-id="'+_esc(id)+'">'
      + '<div class="vx-activity-time">'+_esc(time || '–')+'</div>'
      + '<div style="min-width:0;"><div class="vx-activity-title">'+_esc(title)+'</div>'
      + '<div class="vx-activity-sub">'+_esc(label)+(summary ? ' · '+_esc(summary) : '')+'</div></div>'
      + '<div class="'+iconClass+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-'+icon+'"></i></div>'
      + '</div>';
  }).join('');
  vxSetHtmlIfChanged(list, html);
  list.querySelectorAll('.vx-activity-row').forEach(function(row){
    row.addEventListener('click', function(){
      var recId = row.dataset.recordId || row.dataset.id;
      var rec = typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function' ? vxResolveDprRecord(recId) : null;
      if (rec && isManualTaskRecord(rec)) { if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(recId); }
      else if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(recId);
    });
  });
  refreshLucideIcons();
}

function vxGetHeuteTodayCallRecords(sourceRecords) {
  var source = Array.isArray(sourceRecords) ? sourceRecords : [];
  return vxHeuteDedupeByStableId(source).filter(function(r) {
    return !vxHeuteIsManualTask(r)
      && (typeof isCustomerVisibleCall !== 'function' || isCustomerVisibleCall(r))
      && vxHeuteRecordCreatedToday(r)
      && !vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(r);
  });
}

function vxHeuteRenderScopedList(listId, sectionId, countId, records, emptyText) {
  var section = document.getElementById(sectionId);
  var list = document.getElementById(listId);
  var countEl = document.getElementById(countId);
  if (!section || !list) return;
  var rows = (records || []).filter(Boolean);
  section.style.display = rows.length ? '' : 'none';
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = String(rows.length || 0);
  if (!rows.length) {
    vxSetHtmlIfChanged(list, '<div class="dash-empty">' + _esc(emptyText || 'Keine Einträge.') + '</div>');
    return;
  }
  var scopedHtml = rows.map(function(rec){
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    var id = String((rec && rec.id) || vxHeuteGetRecordValue(rec, ['id','call_id']) || '');
    var phone = String(vxHeuteGetRecordValue(rec, ['caller_phone','phone_number','phone']) || '').trim();
    var label = vxHeuteRecordTypeLabel(rec);
    var title = vxHeuteRecordTitle(rec);
    var time = vxHeuteFormatRowTime(rec);
    var summary = vxHeuteRecordSummary(rec);
    var icon = vxHeuteIsManualTask(rec) ? 'check-circle' : 'phone-incoming';
    var iconClass = vxHeuteIsManualTask(rec) ? 'vx-row-icon' : 'vx-row-icon is-call';
    var callBtn = phone ? '<button type="button" class="vx-ibtn is-call" data-action="call" data-rec-id="'+_esc(id)+'" data-phone="'+_esc(phone)+'" title="Anrufen"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone-call"></i></button>' : '';
    return '<div class="dpr-card" data-id="'+_esc(id)+'" data-phone="'+_esc(phone)+'">'
      + '<div class="vx-heute-row rail-today" data-id="'+_esc(id)+'" data-record-id="'+_esc(id)+'">'
      + '<div class="'+iconClass+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-'+icon+'"></i></div>'
      + '<div class="vx-row-body"><div class="vx-row-name">'+_esc(title)+'<span class="vx-row-pill">'+_esc(label)+'</span></div>'
      + (time ? '<div class="vx-row-time">'+_esc(time)+'</div>' : '')
      + (summary ? '<div class="vx-row-summary">'+_esc(summary)+'</div>' : '')
      + '</div><div class="vx-row-btns">'+callBtn+'<button type="button" class="vx-ibtn" data-action="detail" data-rec-id="'+_esc(id)+'" title="Details öffnen"><i class="ph-bold ph-caret-right"></i></button></div>'
      + '</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  vxSetHtmlIfChanged(list, scopedHtml);
  list.querySelectorAll('.dpr-card').forEach(function(card){
    card.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
      var recId = card.dataset.id;
      var rec = typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function' ? vxResolveDprRecord(recId) : null;
      if (rec && isManualTaskRecord(rec)) { if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(recId); }
      else if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(recId);
    });
  });
  refreshLucideIcons();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  RENDER DASHBOARD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function renderDashboard(records, manualTasks) {
  var buckets = deriveDashboardCallBuckets(records || []);
  var autoTasks = getUnifiedOpenTasks(records || []);
  var manualOpenTasks = (typeof vxGetOpenManualTasks === 'function') ? vxGetOpenManualTasks() : (manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []).filter(function(r) {
    var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
    return !vxIsClosedManualTaskStatus(f.status || f.dashboard_status);
  });
  var unifiedTasks = getUnifiedOpenTasks([].concat(autoTasks, manualOpenTasks));
  var doneToday = (records || []).filter(function(r) {
    return isClosedStatus(r.fields && r.fields.dashboard_status) && isToday(getRecordTimestamp(r));
  });
  var offen = unifiedTasks;
  var sortedOpenCallbacks = unifiedTasks;
  var allOpen = buckets.open;
  updateActivationStatusCard(records);

  // [BUG FIX v2.52b] KPI "Neue Anfragen" muss dieselbe Logik wie Chip "Neu" verwenden:
  // Records mit status=new die bereits auf Heute sind (callback/urgency/followup)
  // werden im Chip "Neu" ausgeblendet — der KPI muss das ebenfalls tun.
  var newOpenRequestsRaw = (typeof vxGetNewRequestCalls === 'function') ? vxGetNewRequestCalls(records || []) : allOpen.filter(function(r){
    var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
    return isCustomerVisibleCall(r) && String(f.live_status || '').trim().toLowerCase() === 'completed' && !f.read_at;
  });
  var newOpenRequests = newOpenRequestsRaw;
  var anfragenOps = (typeof vxGetAnfragenOperationalCounts === 'function') ? vxGetAnfragenOperationalCounts(records || [], manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []) : { inbox: newOpenRequests.length, planned: 0, done: 0, doneToday: 0 };

  // ── KPI: neue Anfragen / offene Aufgaben / erledigt heute / überfällig ─────
  var kpiHeute = document.getElementById('kpi-heute');
  if (kpiHeute) kpiHeute.textContent = allOpen.length;

  var kpiCallbacks = document.getElementById('kpi-callbacks');
  if (kpiCallbacks) kpiCallbacks.textContent = offen.length;

  var kpiFollowups = document.getElementById('kpi-followups');
  if (kpiFollowups) kpiFollowups.textContent = doneToday.length;

  // ── Hero + Fokuskarte (nur UI-Mapping, bestehende Datenquelle) ────────────
  var statusHero = document.getElementById('dash-status-hero');
  var statusTitle = document.getElementById('dash-status-title');
  var statusText = document.getElementById('dash-status-text');
  var statusChipPrimary = document.getElementById('dash-status-chip-primary');
  var statusChipSecondary = document.getElementById('dash-status-chip-secondary');
  if (statusHero && statusTitle && statusText && statusChipPrimary && statusChipSecondary) {
    statusChipPrimary.className = 'dash-status-chip';
    statusChipSecondary.className = 'dash-status-chip note';
    if (isContractRestrictedState()) {
      statusTitle.textContent = 'Vertrag derzeit nicht aktiv';
      statusText.textContent = 'Ihr Zugang ist eingeschränkt. Produktive Änderungen sind erst mit aktivem Vertrag wieder möglich.';
      statusChipPrimary.textContent = 'Eingeschränkter Modus';
      statusChipPrimary.classList.add('alert');
      statusChipSecondary.textContent = 'Vertragsstatus: ' + getContractStatusLabel(customerMeta.contractStatus);
    } else if (offen.length > 0) {
      statusTitle.textContent = 'Anfragen im Fokus';
      statusText.textContent = 'Sie haben aktuell ' + offen.length + ' offene Anfragen.';
      statusChipPrimary.textContent = offen.length + ' offen';
      statusChipPrimary.classList.add('alert');
      statusChipSecondary.textContent = anfragenOps.inbox + ' Inbox · ' + anfragenOps.planned + ' geplant';
    } else {
      statusTitle.textContent = 'Alles im Griff';
      statusText.textContent = 'Aktuell gibt es keine offenen Anfragen.';
      statusChipPrimary.textContent = 'Keine offenen Anfragen';
      statusChipPrimary.classList.add('ok');
      statusChipSecondary.textContent = anfragenOps.inbox + ' Inbox · ' + anfragenOps.planned + ' geplant';
    }
  }

  var focusNumberEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-number');
  var focusHintEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-hint');
  var focusTextEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-text');
  var focusNextEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-next');
  var focusStatusEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-status');
  var focusDateTimeEl = document.getElementById('dash-focus-datetime');
  if (focusDateTimeEl) {
    focusDateTimeEl.textContent = formatDashboardFocusMetaNow(new Date());
  }
  if (focusNumberEl && focusHintEl && focusTextEl && focusNextEl && focusStatusEl) {
    focusNumberEl.textContent = offen.length;
    var overdue = offen.filter(function(r){ return getTaskPriorityBucket(r) === 0; }).length;
    var dueToday = offen.filter(function(r){ return getTaskPriorityBucket(r) === 1; }).length;
    var upcoming = offen.filter(function(r){ return getTaskPriorityBucket(r) === 2; }).length;
    var nextFollowUp = offen.find(function(r){
      return !!resolveTaskDueAt((r && r.fields) || {});
    });
    var nextFollowUpLabel = nextFollowUp ? fmtFollowUpDateTime(resolveTaskDueAt(nextFollowUp.fields || {})) : '';
    if (offen.length > 0 && nextFollowUpLabel) {
      focusHintEl.textContent = 'Nächste Aufgabe';
      focusHintEl.className = 'dash-focus-hint badge badge-gold';
      focusTextEl.textContent = overdue > 0
        ? overdue + ' überfällig · sofort priorisieren.'
        : 'Offene Aufgaben strukturiert abarbeiten.';
      focusNextEl.textContent = nextFollowUpLabel;
      focusStatusEl.textContent = overdue > 0 ? 'Akut' : 'Geplant';
    } else if (offen.length > 0) {
      focusHintEl.textContent = 'Jetzt bearbeiten';
      focusHintEl.className = 'dash-focus-hint badge badge-blue';
      focusTextEl.textContent = dueToday > 0
        ? dueToday + ' Aufgabe(n) heute geplant.'
        : 'Offene Aufgaben strukturiert abarbeiten.';
      focusNextEl.textContent = dueToday > 0 ? 'Heute fällig: ' + dueToday : 'Nächster Termin offen';
      focusStatusEl.textContent = overdue > 0 ? 'Akut' : (upcoming > 0 ? 'In Planung' : 'Offen');
    } else {
      focusHintEl.textContent = 'Keine offenen Aufgaben';
      focusHintEl.className = 'dash-focus-hint badge badge-green';
      focusTextEl.textContent = 'Momentan gibt es keine offenen Aufgaben.';
      focusNextEl.textContent = 'Keine Aufgabe offen';
      focusStatusEl.textContent = 'Bereit';
    }
  }

  // KPI: semantisch sauber halten (Überfällig nur bei vorhandener Fälligkeit)
  var kpiLeads = document.getElementById('kpi-leads');
  var kpiLeadsLabel = document.getElementById('kpi-leads-label');
  var kpiLeadsNote = document.getElementById('kpi-leads-note');
  var openTasksWithDueDate = offen.filter(function(r){
    var due = parseFollowUpDate(resolveTaskDueAt((r && r.fields) || {}));
    return !!(due && !Number.isNaN(due.getTime()));
  });
  var overdueCount = openTasksWithDueDate.filter(function(r){ return getTaskPriorityBucket(r) === 0; }).length;
  var inProgressCount = [].concat(records || [], manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []).filter(function(r){
    var fields = (r && r.fields) || {};
    if (isClosedStatus(fields.dashboard_status)) return false;
    return normalizeStatus(fields.dashboard_status) === CALL_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS;
  }).length;
  var canUseOverdueMetric = openTasksWithDueDate.length > 0;
  if (kpiLeadsLabel) {
    kpiLeadsLabel.textContent = canUseOverdueMetric ? 'Überfällig' : 'In Bearbeitung';
  }
  if (kpiLeadsNote) {
    kpiLeadsNote.textContent = canUseOverdueMetric
      ? 'Offene Anfragen mit überschrittener Fälligkeit'
      : 'Offene Anfragen im Status In Bearbeitung';
  }
  if (kpiLeads) {
    kpiLeads.textContent = canUseOverdueMetric ? overdueCount : inProgressCount;
  }

  // ── NEW DASHBOARD SECTIONS ──────────────────────────────────────────

  // Greeting + datetime
  var greetingEl = document.getElementById('dash-greeting-title');
  var greetingSubEl = document.getElementById('dash-greeting-sub');
  var dtEl = document.getElementById('dash-datetime-label');
  var now2 = new Date();
  var hour = now2.getHours();
  var greeting = hour < 12 ? 'Guten Morgen' : hour < 18 ? 'Guten Nachmittag' : 'Guten Abend';
  var customerName = (customerMeta && customerMeta.displayName) ? customerMeta.displayName : (customerMeta && customerMeta.customerName) ? customerMeta.customerName : (customerContext && customerContext.displayName) ? customerContext.displayName : '';
  if (greetingEl) greetingEl.textContent = greeting + (customerName ? ', ' + customerName : '');
  if (greetingSubEl) {
    // [PATCH 2026-05-13 v2 — Aufgaben-Zählung Konsistenz]
    // Vorher: eigene Inline-Filterung über manualTasks → driftete mit topbar und tagesbericht
    // Jetzt: zentrale getOpenTaskCount()-Funktion, dieselbe wie topbar verwendet
    // [BUG FIX v2.54] Gleicher Filter wie KPI: isOnToday-Records ausschliessen
    var parts = [];
    if (anfragenOps.inbox > 0) parts.push(anfragenOps.inbox + ' Inbox');
    if (anfragenOps.planned > 0) parts.push(anfragenOps.planned + ' geplant');
    var heuteCountsForSub = (typeof vxGetHeuteKpiCounts === 'function') ? vxGetHeuteKpiCounts(records || [], manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []) : null;
    if (heuteCountsForSub && heuteCountsForSub.importantNow > 0) parts.push(heuteCountsForSub.importantNow + ' wichtig');
    greetingSubEl.textContent = parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' · ') : 'Keine offenen Inbox- oder Follow-up-Einträge';
  }
  if (dtEl) {
    var days = ['So','Mo','Di','Mi','Do','Fr','Sa'];
    var months = ['Jan','Feb','Mär','Apr','Mai','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Okt','Nov','Dez'];
    dtEl.textContent = days[now2.getDay()] + ', ' + now2.getDate() + '. ' + months[now2.getMonth()] + ' · ' + String(now2.getHours()).padStart(2,'0') + ':' + String(now2.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  }

  // Tagesbericht deaktiviert: Heute arbeitet jetzt mit Aufmerksamkeit + Verlauf.
  var dailyReportEl = document.getElementById('dash-daily-report');
  if (dailyReportEl) dailyReportEl.style.display = 'none';

  // KPI Strip
  var kpiCb = document.getElementById('kpi-callbacks-new');
  var kpiTd = document.getElementById('kpi-today-new');
  var kpiDn = document.getElementById('kpi-done-new');
  var kpiReachVal = document.getElementById('kpi-reach-val');
  var kpiReachLabel = document.getElementById('kpi-reach-label');
  var kpiCallbacksLabel = document.getElementById('kpi-callbacks-label');
  var kpiCallbacksNote = document.getElementById('kpi-callbacks-note');
  var kpiTodayLabel = document.getElementById('kpi-today-label');
  var kpiTodayNote = document.getElementById('kpi-today-note');
  var kpiDoneLabel = document.getElementById('kpi-done-label');
  var kpiDoneNote = document.getElementById('kpi-done-note');
  var kpiDueBar = document.getElementById('kpi-due-bar-new');
  var kpiCellCallbacks = document.getElementById('kpi-cell-callbacks');
  var kpiCellToday = document.getElementById('kpi-cell-today');
  var kpiCellDue = document.getElementById('kpi-cell-due');
  var kpiReachBarNew = document.getElementById('kpi-reach-bar-new');

  // Launch KPI cockpit:
  // 1) Anrufe heute = eingegangene Calls seit Tagesbeginn
  // 2) Follow-ups offen = offene Rückrufe + geplante Aufgaben
  // 3) Wichtig jetzt = priorisiert oder überfällig
  // 4) Erledigt heute = nur heute abgeschlossene Calls/Tasks
  var heuteKpis = (typeof vxGetHeuteKpiCounts === 'function') ? vxGetHeuteKpiCounts(records || [], manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []) : {
    callsToday: 0,
    callbacksOpen: offen.length,
    importantNow: (buckets.open || []).length,
    doneToday: doneToday.length
  };
  // Central today view-model: the KPIs and the visible Heute sections use the same arrays.
  var heuteViewSource = (typeof vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords === 'function') ? vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() : [].concat(records || [], manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []);
  var heuteTodayCallRecords = (typeof vxGetHeuteTodayCallRecords === 'function')
    ? vxGetHeuteTodayCallRecords(heuteViewSource)
    : [];
  var heuteDoneRecords = heuteViewSource.filter(function(r) {
    var isDone = typeof vxInquiryMatchesFilter === 'function' ? vxInquiryMatchesFilter(r, 'abgeschlossen') : vxHeuteIsDoneRecord(r);
    return isDone && vxHeuteRecordCompletedToday(r);
  });
  var kpiCellCompleted = kpiReachVal ? kpiReachVal.closest('.vx-kpi-cell') : null;

  if (kpiCallbacksLabel) kpiCallbacksLabel.textContent = 'Anrufe heute';
  if (kpiCallbacksNote) kpiCallbacksNote.textContent = 'Eingänge seit Tagesbeginn';
  if (kpiCellCallbacks) kpiCellCallbacks.style.borderTop = heuteTodayCallRecords.length > 0 ? '2px solid var(--vx-blue)' : '2px solid rgba(148,163,184,.45)';
  if (kpiCb) { kpiCb.textContent = String(heuteTodayCallRecords.length || 0); kpiCb.style.color = heuteTodayCallRecords.length > 0 ? '#1A6FE8' : '#94A3B8'; }
  if (typeof vxSetHeuteKpiCell === 'function') vxSetHeuteKpiCell(kpiCellCallbacks, true, 'vxHeuteKpiOpenTodayCalls');

  if (kpiTodayLabel) kpiTodayLabel.textContent = 'Offene Anfragen';
  if (kpiTodayNote) kpiTodayNote.textContent = 'Nicht bearbeitet';
  if (kpiCellToday) kpiCellToday.style.borderTop = heuteKpis.callbacksOpen > 0 ? '2px solid var(--vx-red)' : '2px solid rgba(148,163,184,.45)';
  if (kpiTd) { kpiTd.textContent = String(heuteKpis.callbacksOpen || 0); kpiTd.style.color = heuteKpis.callbacksOpen > 0 ? '#DC2626' : '#94A3B8'; }
  if (typeof vxSetHeuteKpiCell === 'function') vxSetHeuteKpiCell(kpiCellToday, true, 'vxHeuteKpiOpenPlanned');

  if (kpiDoneLabel) kpiDoneLabel.textContent = 'Wichtig jetzt';
  if (kpiDoneNote) kpiDoneNote.textContent = 'Priorisiert oder überfällig';
  if (kpiCellDue) kpiCellDue.style.borderTop = heuteKpis.importantNow > 0 ? '2px solid var(--vx-gold)' : '2px solid rgba(148,163,184,.45)';
  if (kpiDueBar) kpiDueBar.style.background = heuteKpis.importantNow > 0 ? 'linear-gradient(90deg,#FCD34D,#D97706)' : 'linear-gradient(90deg,#CBD5E1,#94A3B8)';
  if (kpiDn) { kpiDn.textContent = String(heuteKpis.importantNow || 0); kpiDn.style.color = heuteKpis.importantNow > 0 ? '#D97706' : '#94A3B8'; }
  if (typeof vxSetHeuteKpiCell === 'function') vxSetHeuteKpiCell(kpiCellDue, true, 'vxHeuteKpiScrollImportant');

  var kpiCompletedLabel = kpiCellCompleted ? kpiCellCompleted.querySelector('.vx-kpi-label') : null;
  if (kpiCompletedLabel) kpiCompletedLabel.textContent = 'Erledigt heute';
  if (kpiReachVal) {
    kpiReachVal.textContent = String(heuteDoneRecords.length || 0);
    kpiReachVal.style.fontSize = '';
    kpiReachVal.style.marginTop = '';
    kpiReachVal.style.color = heuteDoneRecords.length > 0 ? '#059669' : '#94A3B8';
  }
  if (kpiReachLabel) kpiReachLabel.textContent = 'Heute abgeschlossen';
  if (kpiCellCompleted) kpiCellCompleted.style.borderTop = heuteDoneRecords.length > 0 ? '2px solid #059669' : '2px solid rgba(148,163,184,.45)';
  if (kpiReachBarNew) kpiReachBarNew.style.background = heuteDoneRecords.length > 0 ? 'linear-gradient(90deg,#34D399,#059669)' : 'linear-gradient(90deg,#CBD5E1,#94A3B8)';
  if (typeof vxSetHeuteKpiCell === 'function') vxSetHeuteKpiCell(kpiCellCompleted, heuteDoneRecords.length > 0, 'vxHeuteKpiOpenDone');

  var heuteActivityRecords = [].concat(heuteTodayCallRecords || [], heuteDoneRecords || []);
  if (typeof vxHeuteRenderActivityList === 'function') vxHeuteRenderActivityList(heuteActivityRecords);
  if (typeof vxHeuteRenderScopedList === 'function') {
    vxHeuteRenderScopedList('dash-today-calls-list','dash-today-calls-section','dash-today-calls-count', heuteTodayCallRecords, 'Heute gab es noch keine Anrufe.');
    vxHeuteRenderScopedList('dash-today-done-list','dash-today-done-section','dash-today-done-count', heuteDoneRecords, 'Heute wurde noch nichts erledigt.');
  }

  var reach = getReachabilityStatus(records);

  // Reachability warning banner
  var warnBanner = document.getElementById('dash-reach-warning');
  if (!warnBanner) {
    warnBanner = document.createElement('div');
    warnBanner.id = 'dash-reach-warning';
    warnBanner.style.cssText = 'display:none;background:#FEF3C7;border:1px solid #FDE68A;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 14px;margin-bottom:12px;font-size:12px;color:#92400E;';
    var greetBlock = document.getElementById('dash-greeting-block');
    if (greetBlock && greetBlock.nextSibling) greetBlock.parentNode.insertBefore(warnBanner, greetBlock.nextSibling);
  }
  if (reach.daysSince !== null && reach.daysSince >= 7) {
    warnBanner.style.display = '';
    warnBanner.innerHTML = '⚠ Seit ' + reach.daysSince + ' Tagen kein Anruf eingegangen — ist die Weiterleitung noch aktiv?';
  } else {
    warnBanner.style.display = 'none';
  }

  // Sparkline — last 7 days call volume
  var sparkEl = document.getElementById('dash-sparkline');
  var sparkLabels = document.getElementById('dash-sparkline-labels');
  var sparkBlock = document.getElementById('dash-sparkline-block');
  if (sparkEl && records && records.length > 0) {
    var dayLabels = ['Mo','Di','Mi','Do','Fr','Sa','So'];
    var today3 = new Date();
    var weekData = [];
    for (var d = 6; d >= 0; d--) {
      var day = new Date(today3);
      day.setDate(today3.getDate() - d);
      var dayStart = new Date(day); dayStart.setHours(0,0,0,0);
      var dayEnd = new Date(day); dayEnd.setHours(23,59,59,999);
      var count = (records||[]).filter(function(r) {
        var ts = new Date(getRecordTimestamp(r));
        return ts >= dayStart && ts <= dayEnd && !isActivationCallCategory((r.fields||{}).category);
      }).length;
      var isToday4 = d === 0;
      weekData.push({ count: count, label: dayLabels[day.getDay() === 0 ? 6 : day.getDay() - 1], isToday: isToday4 });
    }
    var maxCount = Math.max.apply(null, weekData.map(function(d){return d.count;})) || 1;
    sparkEl.innerHTML = weekData.map(function(d) {
      var pct = Math.max(15, Math.round((d.count / maxCount) * 100));
      var bg = d.isToday ? '#1A6FE8' : d.count > 0 ? 'rgba(26,111,232,0.35)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.06)';
      return '<div style="flex:1;height:' + pct + '%;background:' + bg + ';border-radius:3px;min-height:3px;" title="' + d.count + ' Anrufe"></div>';
    }).join('');
    if (sparkLabels) sparkLabels.innerHTML = weekData.map(function(d) {
      return '<div style="flex:1;text-align:center;font-size:9px;color:' + (d.isToday ? '#1A6FE8' : '#94A3B8') + ';font-weight:' + (d.isToday?'700':'400') + ';">' + d.label + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    if (sparkBlock) sparkBlock.style.display = '';
  var sparkSec = document.getElementById('dash-sparkline-section'); if (sparkSec) sparkSec.style.display = '';
  }

  // ── Recent Calls (dedupliziert, nur primärer Arbeitskorb) ────────────
  var allOpenCalls = buckets.recent;
  // ── Priorisierte Einheitsliste "Jetzt wichtig" ───────────────────
  // Nutzt buckets.open (alle nicht-erledigten) statt nur buckets.recent
  var liveRecord = pickLiveCall(records || []);
  var isLiveMatch = buildLiveCallMatcher(liveRecord);
  var priorityNow = new Date();
  var todayStart = new Date(priorityNow.getFullYear(), priorityNow.getMonth(), priorityNow.getDate());
  var todayEnd = new Date(todayStart.getTime() + 86400000);

  var allTasks = (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : [];
  var workItems = buildDashboardWorkItems(buckets.open || [], allTasks);
  var manualNow = workItems.filter(function(w) {
    if (w.source !== 'manual_task') return false;
    var meta = vxGetDueSortMeta(w.record);
    return meta.hasDue && meta.sortMs <= todayEnd.getTime();
  }).map(function(w){ return w.record; });
  var manualPlanned = workItems.filter(function(w) {
    if (w.source !== 'manual_task') return false;
    var meta = vxGetDueSortMeta(w.record);
    return meta.hasDue && meta.sortMs > todayEnd.getTime();
  }).map(function(w){ return w.record; });
  var priorityRecords = buckets.open
    .filter(function(r){ return !isLiveMatch || !isLiveMatch(r); })
    .filter(function(r){
      var state = getCallDisplayState(r);
      if (state === 'short_abandoned') return false;
      var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
      var isCallback = getFollowUpTaskType(r) === 'callback';
      var badgeState = getFollowUpBadgeState(r, priorityNow);
      var hasDatedFollowUp = !!formatFollowUpDueLabel(r, priorityNow);
      var isUrgent = getPriority(r).key === 'urgent';
      if (hasDatedFollowUp && badgeState === 'planned') return false;
      if (state === 'processing') return isCallback || isUrgent || badgeState === 'overdue' || badgeState === 'today';
      return isCallback || isUrgent || badgeState === 'overdue' || badgeState === 'today' || normalizeStatus(f.dashboard_status) === CALL_STATUS.NEW;
    })
    .filter(function(r){ return !isClosedStatus(((r && r.fields) || {}).dashboard_status); })
    .sort(function(a, b) {
      var aFields = (a && a.fields) || {};
      var bFields = (b && b.fields) || {};
      var aBadge = getFollowUpBadgeState(a, priorityNow);
      var bBadge = getFollowUpBadgeState(b, priorityNow);
      var aCallback = getFollowUpTaskType(a) === 'callback';
      var bCallback = getFollowUpTaskType(b) === 'callback';
      var aUrgent = getPriority(a).key === 'urgent';
      var bUrgent = getPriority(b).key === 'urgent';
      function rank(badge, callback, urgent) {
        if (badge === 'overdue') return 0;
        if (badge === 'today') return 1;
        if (callback && badge === 'generic') return 2;
        if (urgent) return 3;
        return 4;
      }
      var ar = rank(aBadge, aCallback, aUrgent);
      var br = rank(bBadge, bCallback, bUrgent);
      if (ar !== br) return ar - br;
      if (ar <= 1) return compareFollowUpPriority(a, b, priorityNow);
      return new Date(getRecordTimestamp(b) || 0).getTime() - new Date(getRecordTimestamp(a) || 0).getTime();
    });
  // [PATCH 2026-05-13 v2 — Globale Sortierung Jetzt-wichtig]
  // Vorher: concat(manualNow, priorityRecords, manualPlanned) — Reihenfolge fest, aber Sortierung NICHT global.
  //   Folge: Silvia Müller (10.05) erschien VOR Rückruf (heute 17:53) weil sie in priorityRecords war,
  //   während der Rückruf in manualPlanned hinten landete.
  // Jetzt: Alles in ein Array, globale Sortierung nach Priorität:
  //   Rank 0: überfällig
  //   Rank 1: heute fällig
  //   Rank 2: zukünftig geplant
  //   Rank 3: ohne Datum (Rückruf-Wünsche von Anrufen ohne Nachfassen)
  var combinedAll = [].concat(manualNow, priorityRecords, manualPlanned);
  // V3.2: Heute nutzt ein bewusstes ViewModel.
  // Relevante Calls + offene customer_tasks kommen in Aufmerksamkeit;
  // irrelevante Calls bleiben im Verlauf "Heute passiert".
  if (typeof vxBuildTodayAttentionRecords === 'function') {
    _dashPriorityAllRecords = vxBuildTodayAttentionRecords(records || [], allTasks || []);
  } else {
    // Fallback auf alte Logik, falls Helper nicht geladen ist.
    var seenIds = new Set();
    var uniqueAll = combinedAll.filter(function(rec){
      if (!rec || !rec.id) return true;
      if (seenIds.has(rec.id)) return false;
      seenIds.add(rec.id);
      return true;
    });
    _dashPriorityAllRecords = uniqueAll.sort(function(a, b) {
      return vxCompareDashboardPriorityRecords(a, b, priorityNow);
    });
  }
  // Der KPI "Wichtig jetzt" muss exakt dieselbe Anzahl zeigen wie die sichtbare Liste unten.
  var visibleImportantCount = _dashPriorityAllRecords.length;
  if (kpiDn) {
    kpiDn.textContent = String(visibleImportantCount || 0);
    kpiDn.style.color = visibleImportantCount > 0 ? '#D97706' : '#94A3B8';
  }
  if (kpiCellDue) kpiCellDue.style.borderTop = visibleImportantCount > 0 ? '2px solid var(--vx-gold)' : '2px solid rgba(148,163,184,.45)';
  if (kpiDueBar) kpiDueBar.style.background = visibleImportantCount > 0 ? 'linear-gradient(90deg,#FCD34D,#D97706)' : 'linear-gradient(90deg,#CBD5E1,#94A3B8)';
  if (typeof vxSetHeuteKpiCell === 'function') vxSetHeuteKpiCell(kpiCellDue, visibleImportantCount > 0, 'vxHeuteKpiScrollImportant');
  renderDashPriorityList();
  // [v2.70] Focus-Card entfernt
  var moreBtn = document.getElementById('dash-recent-more');
  if (moreBtn) {
    if (!dashPriorityExpanded && _dashPriorityAllRecords.length > 5) {
      moreBtn.style.display = 'block';
      moreBtn.textContent = 'Alle ' + _dashPriorityAllRecords.length + ' offenen Punkte anzeigen';
    } else {
      moreBtn.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }

  // Sparkline section visibility
  var sparkSection = document.getElementById('dash-sparkline-section');
  if (sparkSection && document.getElementById('dash-sparkline') && document.getElementById('dash-sparkline').children.length > 0) {
    sparkSection.style.display = '';
  }

  // ── Badges (Sidebar + Mobile Nav) ────────────────────
  var badgeA = document.getElementById('badge-anrufe');
  var badgeR = document.getElementById('badge-rueckrufe');
  var mb1    = document.getElementById('mnav-badge-anrufe');
  var mb2    = document.getElementById('mnav-badge-rueckrufe');
  // Badge = nur neue, noch nicht gesehene Anrufe
  var seenCalls = getSeenCallIdsFromStorage();
  var newCount = allOpen.filter(function(r) {
    return normalizeStatus((r.fields||{}).dashboard_status) === CALL_STATUS.NEW && !seenCalls.has(String(r.id));
  }).length;
  if (badgeA) { badgeA.textContent = newCount; badgeA.style.display = newCount > 0 ? '' : 'none'; }
  if (badgeR) { badgeR.style.display = 'none'; }
  if (mb1) { mb1.textContent = formatMobileBadgeCount(newCount); mb1.style.display = newCount > 0 ? 'block' : 'none'; }
  if (mb2) { mb2.textContent = formatMobileBadgeCount(unifiedTasks.length);   mb2.style.display = unifiedTasks.length > 0   ? 'block' : 'none'; }
  if (typeof vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges === 'function') vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges();

  // Callback list lives on dedicated Rückrufe tab only

  // Kompatibilität: renderUsage und updateSystemStatus bleiben unverändert
  renderUsage(records);
  updateSystemStatus(records);
}

function updateActivationStatusCard(records) {
  var card = document.getElementById('dash-activation-card');
  var titleEl = document.getElementById('dash-activation-title');
  var textEl = document.getElementById('dash-activation-text');
  var primaryBtn = document.getElementById('dash-activation-primary');
  if (!card || !titleEl || !textEl) return;

  var setupCompleted = isForwardingSetupCompleted();
  card.classList.remove('info', 'success');
  if (!setupCompleted) {
    titleEl.textContent = '! Rufumleitung noch nicht eingerichtet';
    textEl.textContent = 'Leiten Sie Ihre Anrufe auf Voxera weiter, damit keine Anfrage verloren geht.';
    if (primaryBtn) {
      primaryBtn.textContent = 'Weiter zur Einrichtung';
      primaryBtn.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');
      primaryBtn.style.opacity = '';
      primaryBtn.style.pointerEvents = '';
    }
    card.classList.add('show');
    return;
  }

  titleEl.textContent = 'Rufumleitung eingerichtet';
  textEl.textContent = 'Testen Sie jetzt, ob alles funktioniert.';
  if (primaryBtn) {
    var hasBusinessNumber = !!((customerMeta && customerMeta.telNr) ? String(customerMeta.telNr).trim() : '');
    primaryBtn.textContent = 'So testen Sie es';
    primaryBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', hasBusinessNumber ? 'false' : 'true');
    primaryBtn.style.opacity = hasBusinessNumber ? '' : '.55';
    primaryBtn.style.pointerEvents = hasBusinessNumber ? '' : 'none';
  }
  card.classList.add('show', 'info');
}

function formatMobileBadgeCount(value) {
  var count = Number(value) || 0;
  if (count <= 0) return '';
  if (count > 999) return '999+';
  return String(count);
}

function handleDashboardActivationPrimaryCta(event){
  if(event) event.preventDefault();
  if (!isForwardingSetupCompleted()) {
    openSetupWizardFromCta(event, 's1');
    return;
  }
  var hasBusinessNumber = !!((customerMeta && customerMeta.telNr) ? String(customerMeta.telNr).trim() : '');
  if (!hasBusinessNumber) {
    toast('Ihre Geschäftsnummer ist noch nicht hinterlegt.');
    return;
  }
  openSetupHelpOverlay(event, 'testcall');
}

function markCbDone(recordId) {
  const row = document.getElementById('cb-row-' + recordId);
  confirmTaskDone(recordId, row, 'legacy-cb-row');
}

function vxIsManualTaskRecordId(recordId) {
  var resolved = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId) : null;
  if (resolved && typeof isManualTaskRecord === 'function' && isManualTaskRecord(resolved)) return true;
  return String(recordId || '').startsWith('case:');
}


// [PATCH 2026-05-17 — Status/Action Guard]
// Ein abgeschlossener Eintrag ist read-only: Er darf nicht nochmals als erledigt markiert werden.
function vxResolveActionRecord(recordId) {
  var id = String(recordId || '').trim();
  if (!id) return null;
  if (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') {
    var resolved = vxResolveDprRecord(id);
    if (resolved) return resolved;
  }
  var pools = [];
  if (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) pools.push(allRecords);
  if (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) pools.push(allManualTaskRecords);
  for (var p = 0; p < pools.length; p++) {
    var found = (pools[p] || []).find(function(r){
      var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
      return String((r && r.id) || '') === id ||
        String(f.task_entity_id || '') === id.replace(/^case:/,'') ||
        ('case:' + String(f.task_entity_id || '')) === id;
    });
    if (found) return found;
  }
  return null;
}
function vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord(record) {
  if (!record) return false;
  var f = (record && record.fields) || record || {};
  var statusRaw = f.dashboard_status || f.status || f.call_status || '';
  var normalized = (typeof normalizeStatus === 'function') ? normalizeStatus(statusRaw) : String(statusRaw || '').toLowerCase();
  if (typeof isClosedStatus === 'function' && isClosedStatus(normalized)) return true;
  var raw = String(statusRaw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['closed','done','completed','resolved','erledigt','abgeschlossen','archived','archiviert','deleted','cancelled'].includes(raw)) return true;
  if (f.completed_at || f.archived_at) return true;
  return false;
}
function vxGuardDoneAction(recordId, source) {
  var rec = vxResolveActionRecord(recordId);
  if (!vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord(rec)) return false;
  if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Dieser Eintrag ist bereits erledigt.', 'info');
  else if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('Dieser Eintrag ist bereits erledigt.');
  console.info('[Voxera Action Guard] blocked duplicate done action', { id: String(recordId || ''), source: source || 'unknown' });
  return true;
}
window.vxResolveActionRecord = vxResolveActionRecord;
window.vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord = vxIsReadonlyDoneRecord;
window.vxGuardDoneAction = vxGuardDoneAction;

function confirmTaskDone(recordId, rowElement, triggerSource, onSuccess, options) {
  options = options || {};
  const manual = vxIsManualTaskRecordId(recordId);
  if (typeof vxGuardDoneAction === 'function' && vxGuardDoneAction(recordId, triggerSource || 'confirmTaskDone')) return;
  // Guard: Anruf darf nicht als erledigt markiert werden wenn er noch läuft
  if (!manual) {
    const rec = (allRecords || []).find(r => String(r.id) === String(recordId));
    const liveStatus = String((rec?.fields || {}).live_status || '').toLowerCase();
    if (liveStatus === 'incoming' || liveStatus === 'active' || liveStatus === 'analyzing') {
      const liveMsg = liveStatus === 'analyzing'
        ? 'Der Anruf wird gerade noch ausgewertet. Bitte warte kurz bis die Zusammenfassung verfügbar ist.'
        : 'Dieser Anruf läuft noch. Er kann erst nach Abschluss als erledigt markiert werden.';
      openConfirmModal({
        title: 'Anruf noch nicht beendet',
        text: liveMsg,
        confirmLabel: 'OK',
        tone: 'primary',
        onConfirm: async function() { return true; }
      });
      return;
    }
  }
  if (manual) {
    logManualTaskDoneFlow('confirm.open', {
      triggerSource: triggerSource || 'unknown',
      recordId: String(recordId || ''),
      isManualTask: true
    });
  }
  realtimeSuppressUntil = Date.now() + 5000; // Verhindert Focus-Event Reload während Modal offen ist
  openConfirmModal({
    title: manual ? 'Aufgabe als erledigt markieren?' : 'Anfrage als erledigt markieren?',
    text: manual
      ? 'Diese Aufgabe wird als erledigt markiert und aus der offenen Liste entfernt.'
      : 'Diese Anfrage wird als erledigt markiert. Sie bleibt unter Erledigt sichtbar, sofern sie nicht archiviert wird.',
    checkboxLabel: manual ? '' : 'Nach Erledigung archivieren',
    checkboxDefault: manual ? false : !!options.archiveAfterClose,
    confirmLabel: 'Ja, erledigen',
    loadingLabel: 'Speichere...',
    tone: 'primary',
    onConfirm: async function(confirmState) {
      realtimeSuppressUntil = Date.now() + 3000;
      const shouldArchive = !!(confirmState && confirmState.archiveAfterClose);
      const targetStatus = shouldArchive ? CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED : CALL_STATUS.CLOSED;

      var result = await doStatusChange(recordId, targetStatus, rowElement || null);

      if (result !== false) {
        // doStatusChange is the authoritative lifecycle handler: it updates UI, shows toast and closes detail where appropriate.
        // Avoid duplicate toast/close cycles here.
        if (typeof onSuccess === 'function') onSuccess();
      }
      return result;
    }
  });
}

// Wrapper für Focus Mode: ruft confirmTaskDone auf und navigiert danach zur nächsten Karte.
// Der onSuccess-Callback wird NACH erfolgreichem DB-Schreiben ausgeführt (nicht sofort nach Klick),
// damit der Focus Mode erst weiterspringt wenn das Erledigen auch wirklich gespeichert wurde.
function markTaskDoneDirectly(id, name) {
  confirmTaskDone(id, null, 'list-quick-action');
}

function confirmArchiveInquiry(recordId, rowElement) {
  openConfirmModal({
    title: 'Anfrage archivieren?',
    text: 'Diese Anfrage wird aus der normalen Ansicht ausgeblendet und bleibt im Archiv auffindbar.',
    confirmLabel: 'Archivieren',
    loadingLabel: 'Archiviere...',
    tone: 'primary',
    onConfirm: async function() {
      return await doStatusChange(recordId, CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED, rowElement || null);
    }
  });
}
window.confirmArchiveInquiry = confirmArchiveInquiry;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  CONTEXT MENU (v2.65)
//  Rechtsklick + Long-Press auf dpr-cards
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════

(function() {
  var _menu = null;
  var _longPressTimer = null;
  var _longPressMoved = false;
  var LONG_PRESS_MS = 500;

  // ── Menu bauen ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _createMenuEl() {
    var el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'vx-ctx-menu';
    el.setAttribute('role', 'menu');
    el.style.cssText = [
      'position:fixed',
      'z-index:99999',
      'min-width:200px',
      'background:#ffffff',
      'border:0.5px solid #E4E8F0',
      'border-radius:10px',
      'box-shadow:0 4px 24px rgba(13,31,60,.13)',
      'padding:4px',
      'font-family:inherit',
      'font-size:13px',
      'display:none',
      'user-select:none',
    ].join(';');
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  }

  function _getMenu() {
    return document.getElementById('vx-ctx-menu') || _createMenuEl();
  }

  function _menuItem(label, icon, action, danger) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.setAttribute('role', 'menuitem');
    btn.style.cssText = [
      'display:flex',
      'align-items:center',
      'gap:9px',
      'width:100%',
      'padding:8px 12px',
      'border:none',
      'background:transparent',
      'border-radius:7px',
      'cursor:pointer',
      'font-size:13px',
      'font-family:inherit',
      'font-weight:400',
      'color:' + (danger ? '#DC2626' : '#1A1A2E'),
      'text-align:left',
      'line-height:1',
    ].join(';');
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-bold ph-' + icon + '" style="font-size:14px;flex-shrink:0;color:' + (danger ? '#DC2626' : '#7A8599') + '"></i>' +
      '<span>' + label + '</span>';
    btn.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
      this.style.background = danger ? '#FEF2F2' : '#F7F8FA';
    });
    btn.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
      this.style.background = 'transparent';
    });
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      _close();
      action();
    });
    return btn;
  }

  function _divider() {
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.style.cssText = 'height:0.5px;background:#E4E8F0;margin:3px 8px;';
    return d;
  }

  // ── Menu anzeigen ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _show(x, y, items) {
    var menu = _getMenu();
    menu.innerHTML = '';
    items.forEach(function(item) {
      if (item === 'divider') {
        menu.appendChild(_divider());
      } else {
        menu.appendChild(_menuItem(item.label, item.icon, item.action, item.danger));
      }
    });

    menu.style.display = 'block';
    menu.style.left = '0px';
    menu.style.top = '0px';

    // Position so das Menu nicht aus dem Viewport fällt
    var menuW = menu.offsetWidth;
    var menuH = menu.offsetHeight;
    var vw = window.innerWidth;
    var vh = window.innerHeight;
    var left = (x + menuW > vw - 8) ? Math.max(8, vw - menuW - 8) : x;
    var top  = (y + menuH > vh - 8) ? Math.max(8, y - menuH) : y;
    menu.style.left = left + 'px';
    menu.style.top  = top  + 'px';

    _menu = menu;
  }

  function _close() {
    var m = document.getElementById('vx-ctx-menu');
    if (m) m.style.display = 'none';
    _menu = null;
  }

  // ── Record aus Card auflösen ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _resolveRecord(card) {
    var id = card && card.dataset && card.dataset.id;
    if (!id) return null;
    return (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(id) : null;
  }

  // ── Items für Call ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _ctxResolveById(id) {
    if (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') return vxResolveDprRecord(id);
    var calls = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
    var tasks = (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : (window.allManualTaskRecords || []);
    return calls.find(function(r){ return String((r && r.id) || '') === String(id); })
      || tasks.find(function(r){ return String((r && r.id) || '') === String(id); });
  }

  function _ctxRecordPhone(rec) {
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    return String(f.caller_phone || f.phone_number || f.phone || f.tel_link || '').trim();
  }

  // ── Items für Call ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _callItems(id) {
    var rec = _ctxResolveById(id);
    var lifecycle = (typeof vxRowActionLifecycle === 'function') ? vxRowActionLifecycle(rec) : '';
    var isDone = lifecycle === 'done';
    var isArchived = lifecycle === 'archived';
    var phone = _ctxRecordPhone(rec);
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    var currentStatus = rec && rec.fields ? normalizeStatus(rec.fields.dashboard_status) : CALL_STATUS.NEW;
    var isRead = currentStatus !== CALL_STATUS.NEW;
    var items = [];

    if (phone) {
      items.push({
        label: 'Anrufen',
        icon: 'phone-call',
        action: function() { if (typeof dprAction === 'function') dprAction('call', id, phone); }
      });
    }

    if (!isDone && !isArchived) {
      items.push({
        label: 'Als erledigt markieren',
        icon: 'check-circle',
        action: function() { if (typeof confirmTaskDone === 'function') confirmTaskDone(id, null, 'context-menu'); }
      });
    }

    items.push({
      label: 'Rückruf planen',
      icon: 'phone-outgoing',
      action: function() { if (typeof openFollowupDirect === 'function') openFollowupDirect(id); }
    });

    items.push({
      label: 'Details öffnen',
      icon: 'arrow-square-out',
      action: function() { if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(id); else if (typeof openDetail === 'function') openDetail(id); }
    });

    if (typeof vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord !== 'function' || vxIsUnreadEligibleRecord(rec)) {
      items.push('divider');
      items.push({
        label: isRead ? 'Als ungelesen markieren' : 'Als gelesen markieren',
        icon: isRead ? 'envelope' : 'envelope-open',
        action: function() {
          var card = document.querySelector('[data-id="' + id + '"]');
          if (!card) return;
          if (isRead) {
            if (typeof vxMarkRowAsUnread === 'function') vxMarkRowAsUnread(card);
          } else {
            if (typeof vxMarkRowAsRead === 'function') vxMarkRowAsRead(card);
          }
        }
      });
    }

    if (!isArchived) {
      items.push('divider');
      items.push({
        label: 'Archivieren',
        icon: 'archive',
        action: function() { if (typeof confirmArchiveInquiry === 'function') confirmArchiveInquiry(id, null); },
        danger: false
      });
    }
    return items;
  }

  // ── Items für Task ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _taskItems(id, rec) {
    rec = rec || _ctxResolveById(id);
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    var lifecycle = (typeof vxRowActionLifecycle === 'function') ? vxRowActionLifecycle(rec) : '';
    var isDone = lifecycle === 'done';
    var isArchived = lifecycle === 'archived';
    var hasDue = !!(f.follow_up_at || f.due_at);
    var phone = _ctxRecordPhone(rec);
    var items = [];

    if (phone) {
      items.push({
        label: 'Anrufen',
        icon: 'phone-call',
        action: function() { if (typeof dprAction === 'function') dprAction('call', id, phone); }
      });
    }

    if (isDone || isArchived) {
      items.push({
        label: 'Rückruf planen',
        icon: 'phone-outgoing',
        action: function() { if (typeof openFollowupDirect === 'function') openFollowupDirect(id); }
      });
      items.push({
        label: 'Details öffnen',
        icon: 'arrow-square-out',
        action: function() { if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(id); }
      });
      if (!isArchived) {
        items.push('divider');
        items.push({
          label: 'Archivieren',
          icon: 'archive',
          action: function() { if (typeof dprAction === 'function') dprAction('archive', id); },
          danger: false
        });
      }
      return items;
    }

    items.push({
      label: 'Als erledigt markieren',
      icon: 'check-circle',
      action: function() { if (typeof confirmTaskDone === 'function') confirmTaskDone(id, null, 'context-menu'); }
    });
    items.push({
      label: hasDue ? 'Fälligkeit ändern' : 'Fälligkeit setzen',
      icon: 'calendar-plus',
      action: function() { if (typeof openFollowupDirect === 'function') openFollowupDirect(id); }
    });
    items.push({
      label: 'Details öffnen',
      icon: 'arrow-square-out',
      action: function() { if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(id); }
    });
    items.push('divider');
    items.push({
      label: 'Archivieren',
      icon: 'archive',
      action: function() { if (typeof dprAction === 'function') dprAction('archive', id); },
      danger: false
    });
    return items;
  }

  // ── Guard: prüft ob ein Modal/Overlay/Detail-Page offen ist ─────────────
  function _anyOverlayOpen() {
    var overlayIds = [
      'detail-overlay', 'followup-overlay',
      'confirm-overlay', 'vx-detail-v2', 'vx-onboarding-overlay',
      'vx-preview-modal', 'vx-upgrade-modal', 'vx-minutes-modal'
    ];
    for (var i = 0; i < overlayIds.length; i++) {
      var el = document.getElementById(overlayIds[i]);
      if (!el) continue;
      var s = el.style;
      if (s.display === 'block' || s.display === 'flex') return true;
      if (el.classList.contains('open')) return true;
      if (el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') === 'false') return true;
    }
    // call-detail-page: nur als Overlay werten wenn Fullscreen (nicht Split-Panel)
    var cdp = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    if (cdp && cdp.style.display === 'block' && cdp.classList.contains('is-fullscreen')) return true;
    return false;
  }

  // ── Unified Row Menu Bridge ──────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Source of truth for row actions is the same hidden .vx-row-menu that powers
  // the visible three-dot overflow button. Long-press and right-click must not
  // build their own action list, otherwise status-specific actions drift.
  function _openUnifiedRowPortalMenu(card, x, y) {
    if (!card || typeof vxOpenFloatingRowMenuAtPoint !== 'function') return false;

    var row = card.closest && card.closest('.vx-heute-row');
    if (!row && card.querySelector) row = card.querySelector('.vx-heute-row');
    if (!row) return false;

    var rowMenu = row.querySelector && row.querySelector('.vx-row-menu');
    if (!rowMenu && card.querySelector) rowMenu = card.querySelector('.vx-row-menu');
    if (!rowMenu) return false;

    var recId = (row.dataset && (row.dataset.recordId || row.dataset.id)) ||
      (card.dataset && (card.dataset.recordId || card.dataset.id)) || '';
    var phone = (row.dataset && row.dataset.phone) ||
      (card.dataset && card.dataset.phone) || '';

    var source = { dataset: { recId: recId, id: recId, recordId: recId, phone: phone } };
    vxOpenFloatingRowMenuAtPoint(source, rowMenu, x, y);
    return true;
  }

  // ── Öffnen (gemeinsam für Rechtsklick + Long-Press) ───────────────────────
  function _openForCard(card, x, y) {
    if (_anyOverlayOpen()) return; // kein Menü wenn Overlay/Modal offen

    // Prefer the operational row menu used by the three-dot button. This keeps
    // long-press/right-click exactly aligned with the visible overflow menu.
    if (_openUnifiedRowPortalMenu(card, x, y)) return;

    // Legacy fallback for older dpr-cards without a .vx-row-menu.
    var rec = _resolveRecord(card);
    if (!rec) return;
    var id = card.dataset.id;
    var isTask = (typeof isManualTaskRecord === 'function') && isManualTaskRecord(rec);
    var items = isTask ? _taskItems(id, rec) : _callItems(id);
    _show(x, y, items);
  }

  // ── Rechtsklick ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function _onContextMenu(e) {
    var card = e.target.closest('.dpr-card');
    if (!card) return;

    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    _openForCard(card, e.clientX + 4, e.clientY + 4);
  }

  // ── Long-Press (Touch) ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  var _longPressDidFire = false; // verhindert Card-Expand nach Long-Press
  window._vxLongPressActive = false; // global lesbar für dprToggleTouchEnd

  function _onTouchStart(e) {
    _longPressMoved = false;
    _longPressDidFire = false;
    var card = e.target.closest('.dpr-card');
    if (!card) return;
    // [v2.71] Kein Long-Press wenn auf Button/Link geklickt
    if (e.target.closest('button, a, input, select, textarea, [role="button"]')) return;
    var touch = e.touches[0];
    var x = touch.clientX;
    var y = touch.clientY;
    _longPressTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      if (!_longPressMoved) {
        _longPressDidFire = true;
        window._vxLongPressActive = true;
        if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(30);
        _openForCard(card, x + 4, y + 4);
      }
    }, LONG_PRESS_MS);
  }

  function _onTouchMove(e) {
    _longPressMoved = true;
    if (_longPressTimer) { clearTimeout(_longPressTimer); _longPressTimer = null; }
  }

  function _onTouchEnd(e) {
    if (_longPressTimer) { clearTimeout(_longPressTimer); _longPressTimer = null; }
    // [v2.65 FIX] Card-Expand nach Long-Press unterdrücken
    if (_longPressDidFire) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      _longPressDidFire = false;
      // Flag bleibt kurz aktiv damit dprToggleTouchEnd es noch lesen kann
      setTimeout(function() { window._vxLongPressActive = false; }, 600);
    }
  }

  // ── Schliessen ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // [v2.65 FIX] touchstart statt mousedown: auf Mobile kommt mousedown NACH
  // touchend — würde Menü-Item-Tap annullieren bevor click feuert.
  // Auf Desktop bleibt mousedown aktiv.
  var _isTouchDevice = false;
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', function() { _isTouchDevice = true; }, { once: true, passive: true });

  document.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
    if (_isTouchDevice) return; // Touch-Gerät: touchstart-Handler übernimmt
    if (_menu && !_menu.contains(e.target)) _close();
  });
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
    if (_menu && !_menu.contains(e.target)) {
      // Kurze Verzögerung: erst schliessen nachdem tap-Event auf Menü-Item
      // verarbeitet wurde (click feuert ~300ms nach touchend)
      setTimeout(_close, 350);
    }
  }, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') _close();
  });
  document.addEventListener('scroll', _close, true);

  // ── Listener auf Listen-Container delegieren ──────────────────────────────
  function _attachToContainer(containerId) {
    var el = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if (!el) return;
    el.addEventListener('contextmenu', _onContextMenu);
    el.addEventListener('touchstart', _onTouchStart, { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchmove',  _onTouchMove,  { passive: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchend',   _onTouchEnd,   { passive: true });
  }

  // Sofort attachen falls Container bereits existiert, sonst nach loadData
  function _init() {
    _attachToContainer('dash-priority-list');
    _attachToContainer('anrufe-list');
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', _init);
  } else {
    _init();
  }

  // Re-attach nach jedem loadData (Container wird neu gerendert)
  var _origLoadData = window.loadData;
  if (typeof _origLoadData === 'function') {
    window.loadData = function() {
      var result = _origLoadData.apply(this, arguments);
      if (result && typeof result.then === 'function') {
        result.then(function() { setTimeout(_init, 200); });
      }
      return result;
    };
  }

  window.vxContextMenu = { open: _openForCard, close: _close };
})();



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  SYSTEM STATUS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function getLatestCallDate(records) {
  if (!records || !records.length) return null;
  let maxTime = 0;
  let latestDate = null;
  records.forEach(function(r) {
    const raw = getRecordTimestamp(r);
    if (!raw) return;
    const d = new Date(raw);
    if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) && d.getTime() > maxTime) {
      maxTime = d.getTime();
      latestDate = d;
    }
  });
  return latestDate;
}

function updateSystemStatus(records) {
  var el = document.getElementById('system-status');
  var txt = document.getElementById('system-status-text');
  var dot = el ? el.querySelector('.status-dot') : null;

  var latest = getLatestCallDate(records);
  var hasRecent = false;
  var now = new Date();
  if (latest) {
    var hoursSince = (now - latest) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    hasRecent = hoursSince < 168;
  }

  if (el && txt) {
    if (hasRecent) {
      txt.textContent = 'System aktiv';
      el.style.background = 'var(--green-light)';
      el.style.borderColor = 'var(--green-mid)';
      el.style.color = 'var(--green)';
      if (dot) dot.style.background = 'var(--green)';
    } else if (records.length > 0) {
      txt.textContent = 'Letzter Anruf vor über 7 Tagen';
      el.style.background = 'var(--amber-light)';
      el.style.borderColor = 'var(--amber-mid)';
      el.style.color = 'var(--amber)';
      if (dot) dot.style.background = 'var(--amber)';
    } else {
      txt.textContent = 'Warte auf ersten Anruf';
      el.style.background = 'var(--surface2)';
      el.style.borderColor = 'var(--line)';
      el.style.color = 'var(--slate2)';
      if (dot) dot.style.background = 'var(--gray400)';
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  RENDER LIST
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function renderList(containerId, records, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  records = (records || []).filter(function(rec) {
    return isManualTaskRecord(rec) || isCustomerVisibleCall(rec);
  });
  const el = document.getElementById(containerId);
  const isMobileList = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)').matches;
  const isTaskList = containerId === 'rueckrufe-list' || containerId === 'dash-callback-list';
  // Preserve expanded state across re-renders
  const expandedIds = new Set();
  if (el) {
    el.querySelectorAll('.call-row.expanded').forEach(function(r) {
      var id = r.dataset && r.dataset.id;
      if (id) expandedIds.add(id);
    });
  }
  if (!records.length) {
    var emptyIcon = containerId === 'archiv-list' ? 'archive'
                  : containerId === 'rueckrufe-list' ? 'calendar'
                  : 'phone';
    var emptyTitle = containerId === 'archiv-list' ? 'Noch kein Verlauf'
                   : containerId === 'rueckrufe-list' ? 'Keine offenen Aufgaben'
                   : 'Keine Anfragen vorhanden';
    var emptySub = containerId === 'archiv-list' ? 'Erledigte Anrufe erscheinen hier im Verlauf.'
                 : containerId === 'rueckrufe-list' ? 'Sobald ein Rückruf oder Nachfassen ansteht, erscheint er hier.'
                 : (getAssistantName()) + ' nimmt Anrufe für Sie entgegen — neue Anrufe erscheinen hier.';
    el.innerHTML = renderEmptyState(emptyTitle, emptySub, emptyIcon);
    return;
  }
  const ordered = opts.skipSort ? [...records] : sortRecordsOpenFirst(records);
  const limited = typeof opts.limit === 'number' ? ordered.slice(0, opts.limit) : ordered;
  el.innerHTML = limited.map(r => {
    const f = r.fields;
    const q = qualKey(f.lead_quality);
    const done = isClosedStatus(f.dashboard_status);
    const callerDisplayName = getCallDisplayName(f);
    const phoneLink = (f.tel_link || '').trim();
    const phoneRaw = (f.phone_number || f.caller_phone || '').trim();
    const displayPhone = phoneRaw || phoneLink.replace(/^tel:/i,'');
    const callTarget = phoneLink || phoneRaw.replace(/\s/g,'');
    const notizText = (f.notes_customer_voxera || '').trim();
    const showActions = opts.showActions !== false;
    const summary = (f.call_summary || fallbackCallSummary(f) || '').trim();
    const visibleSummary = isDashLikePlaceholder(summary) ? '' : summary;
    const categoryLabel = getCallCategoryDisplayLabel(r);
    const callSummary = opts.dashboardView ? visibleSummary.substring(0, 140) : visibleSummary;

    const incoming = recentIncomingIds.has(r.id);
    const displayState = getCallDisplayState(r);
    const isLiveCall = displayState === 'live';
    const isProcessingCall = displayState === 'processing';
    const isShortAbandonedCall = displayState === 'short_abandoned';
    const liveBadgeHtml = (isLiveCall || isProcessingCall || isShortAbandonedCall) ? (() => {
      if (isLiveCall) return `<span class="live-call-badge state-incoming"><span class="live-call-badge-dot"></span>LIVE</span>`;
      if (isProcessingCall) return `<span class="live-call-badge state-analyzing"><span class="live-call-badge-dot"></span>Wird verarbeitet</span>`;
      return `<span class="live-call-badge state-analyzing"><span class="live-call-badge-dot"></span>Kurzer Anruf</span>`;
    })() : '';
    const priority = getPriority(r);
