    // Nachfassen meta line (action/date/time) in short unified format
    const followUpAt = resolveTaskDueAt(f);
    const followUpMetaText = buildFollowUpMetaText(r, new Date());
    const taskDueBucket = getTaskPriorityBucket(r);
    var taskDueLabel = '';
    var taskDueClass = 'open';
    if (!done && isTaskList) {
      if (taskDueBucket === 0) { taskDueLabel = 'Überfällig'; taskDueClass = 'overdue'; }
      else if (taskDueBucket === 1) { taskDueLabel = 'Heute fällig'; taskDueClass = 'today'; }
      else if (taskDueBucket === 2) { taskDueLabel = 'Geplant'; taskDueClass = 'planned'; }
      else { taskDueLabel = 'Offene Aufgabe'; taskDueClass = 'open'; }
    }
    const closedAt = resolveClosedAt(f);
    const callDateTime = fmtHumanDateTime(getRecordTimestamp(r)) || fmtExactDateTime(getRecordTimestamp(r));
    const durationLabel = !isManualTaskRecord(r) ? formatDurationForDisplay(f.duration_seconds) : null;
    const callEndInfo = !isManualTaskRecord(r) ? resolveCallEndLabel(f) : null;

    const badgeHtml = isMobileList ? callCardMobileBadgeHtml(r) : callCardBadgeHtml(r);

    // ── Inbox mobile vars ──
    const ibxUrgent  = !done && (priority.key === 'urgent' || !!f.callback_requested);
    const ibxNew     = !done && normalizeStatus(f.dashboard_status) === 'new';
    const isUnread   = (typeof vxIsUnreadRecord === 'function') ? vxIsUnreadRecord(r) : (!isManualTaskRecord(r) && !f.read_at);
    const ibxDotCls  = ibxUrgent ? 'ibx-dot-urgent' : ibxNew ? 'ibx-dot-new' : 'ibx-dot-empty';
    const ibxAvCls   = ibxUrgent ? 'ibx-av-red' : done ? 'ibx-av-gray' : 'ibx-av-blue';
    const ibxNameCls = done ? 'ibx-name-done' : '';
    // Subject: live state first, then priority, then category
    const ibxSubj = isLiveCall ? 'Lara nimmt entgegen…'
                  : isProcessingCall ? 'Zusammenfassung wird erstellt…'
                  : isShortAbandonedCall ? 'Der Anrufer hat aufgelegt, bevor eine Anfrage aufgenommen wurde.'
                  : f.callback_requested && !done ? 'Rückruf gewünscht'
                  : ibxUrgent ? 'Dringend'
                  : (categoryLabel && !isDashLikePlaceholder(categoryLabel)) ? categoryLabel
                  : durationLabel && durationLabel !== '–' ? durationLabel
                  : 'Inbound';
    const ibxSubjMuted = !ibxUrgent && !f.callback_requested && !isLiveCall;
    // Preview: phone · company · duration
    const ibxPreviewParts = [displayPhone, f.company_name, durationLabel && durationLabel !== '–' ? durationLabel : ''].filter(Boolean);
    const ibxPreview = ibxPreviewParts.join(' · ') || (callSummary ? callSummary.substring(0, 70) : '');
    // Call button SVG
    const ibxCallSvg = '<svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M6.6 10.8c1.4 2.8 3.8 5.1 6.6 6.6l2.2-2.2c.3-.3.7-.4 1-.2 1.1.4 2.3.6 3.6.6.6 0 1 .4 1 1V20c0 .6-.4 1-1 1C10.6 21 3 13.4 3 4c0-.6.4-1 1-1h3.5c.6 0 1 .4 1 1 0 1.3.2 2.5.6 3.6.1.3 0 .7-.2 1L6.6 10.8z"/></svg>';

    // ─── Nachfassen Badge ───
    const openFollowup = getOpenFollowupForRecord(r);
    let followupBadgeHtml = '';
    if (openFollowup && !done) {
      const ft = openFollowup.fields || {};
      const ftTypeMap = { callback: 'Rückruf', offerte: 'Offerte', information: 'Offerte', appointment: 'Termin', termin: 'Termin', task: 'Aufgabe', general: 'Aufgabe', follow_up: 'Nachfassen' };
      const ftLabel = ftTypeMap[ft.type] || 'Nachfassen';
      const ftDue = ft.due_at ? new Date(ft.due_at) : null;
      const ftOverdue = ftDue && ftDue < new Date();
      const ftDateStr = ftDue ? (fmtDate(ftDue) + (fmtTime(ftDue) ? ' · ' + fmtTime(ftDue) : '')) : '';
      followupBadgeHtml = `<span class="followup-badge${ftOverdue?' overdue':''}">${escHtml(ftLabel)}${ftDateStr?' · '+escHtml(ftDateStr):''}</span>`;
    }

    return `<div class="call-row${done?' done':''}${incoming?' incoming':''}${opts.dashboardView?' dashboard-call':''}" data-qual="${q}" data-done="${done?'1':'0'}" data-id="${r.id}" data-open="${done?'0':'1'}" data-priority="${priority.key}" data-status="${escHtml(normalizeStatus(f.dashboard_status))}" onclick="toggleCallRow(event,this)">

      <!-- ── MOBILE: INBOX ROW ── -->
      <div class="ibx-dot-col"><div class="ibx-dot ${ibxDotCls}" style="${isUnread?'background:#1A6FE8;':''}"></div></div>
      <div class="ibx-av-col"><div class="ibx-av ${ibxAvCls}">${initials(callerDisplayName)}</div></div>
      <div class="ibx-body">
        <div class="ibx-top">
          <span class="ibx-name ${ibxNameCls}" style="${isUnread?'font-weight:700;':''}">${escHtml(callerDisplayName)}${f.company_name && !done?` <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:400;color:var(--slate2);">${escHtml(f.company_name)}</span>`:''}</span>
          <span class="ibx-time">${callDateTime}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="ibx-subject${ibxSubjMuted?' ibx-subject-muted':''}">${escHtml(ibxSubj)}</div>
        ${followupBadgeHtml}
        ${ibxPreview?`<div class="ibx-preview">${escHtml(ibxPreview)}</div>`:''}
      </div>
      <div class="ibx-chevron">›</div>

      <!-- ── MOBILE: EXPANDED DETAIL ── -->
      <div class="ibx-exp">
        ${callSummary?`<div class="ibx-exp-summary">${escHtml(callSummary)}</div>`:''}
        ${ibxPreview?`<div class="ibx-exp-meta">${escHtml(ibxPreviewParts.join(' · '))}</div>`:''}
        ${badgeHtml?`<div class="ibx-exp-badges">${badgeHtml}</div>`:''}
        ${showActions && !done && !isLiveCall?`<div class="ibx-exp-actions">
          ${callTarget?`<a class="btn btn--primary" href="tel:${callTarget}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();directCall('${r.id}','${encodeURIComponent(callTarget)}')" style="text-decoration:none;">Anrufen</a>`:''}
          <button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();confirmTaskDone('${r.id}',this.closest('.call-row'),'list-quick-action')">Erledigt</button>
          ${!isManualTaskRecord(r)?`<button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openFollowupDirect('${r.id}')">Nachfassen</button>`:''}
          ${!isManualTaskRecord(r)?`<button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxEmailForward('${r.id}')">E-Mail</button>`:''}
          ${!isManualTaskRecord(r)?`<button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openDetail('${r.id}')">Details</button>`:''}
        </div>`:''}
        ${showActions&&done?`<div class="ibx-exp-actions">
          <button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openDetail('${r.id}')">Details anzeigen</button>
        </div>`:''}
      </div>

      <!-- ── DESKTOP: dark header + white body (hidden on mobile via CSS) ── -->
      <div class="cr-head">
        <div class="av ${avClass(f.lead_quality, done)}">${initials(callerDisplayName)}</div>
        <div class="cr-head-info">
          <div class="cr-head-name">${liveBadgeHtml}${escHtml(callerDisplayName)}${f.company_name?`<span class="cr-head-co">${escHtml(f.company_name)}</span>`:''}</div>
        </div>
        <span class="cr-head-date">${callDateTime}</span>
        ${showActions && !done && callTarget ? `<a class="cr-call-btn" href="tel:${callTarget}" onclick="event.stopPropagation();directCall('${r.id}','${encodeURIComponent(callTarget)}')" title="Anrufen" aria-label="Anrufen"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="white"><path d="M6.6 10.8c1.4 2.8 3.8 5.1 6.6 6.6l2.2-2.2c.3-.3.7-.4 1-.2 1.1.4 2.3.6 3.6.6.6 0 1 .4 1 1V20c0 .6-.4 1-1 1C10.6 21 3 13.4 3 4c0-.6.4-1 1-1h3.5c.6 0 1 .4 1 1 0 1.3.2 2.5.6 3.6.1.3 0 .7-.2 1L6.6 10.8z"/></svg></a>` : ''}
        ${showActions && !done ? `<button class="cr-done-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();confirmTaskDone('${r.id}',this.closest('.call-row'),'list-quick-action')" title="${isLiveCall?'Anruf läuft…':'Erledigt'}" ${isLiveCall?'disabled':''}>
          <i class="ph-bold ph-check" style="stroke-width:2.5;"></i>
        </button>` : ''}
        <span class="cr-chevron" style="margin-left:2px;">›</span>
      </div>

      <!-- BODY: Badges, Meta, Summary -->
      <div class="cr-body">
        <div class="call-secondary" style="margin-bottom:6px;">${badgeHtml}</div>
        <div class="call-meta">
          ${displayPhone?`<span class="call-meta-item call-meta-phone">${escHtml(displayPhone)}</span>`:''}
          ${(categoryLabel && !isDashLikePlaceholder(categoryLabel))?`<span class="call-meta-item">${escHtml(categoryLabel)}</span>`:''}
          ${durationLabel && durationLabel !== '–' ? `<span class="call-meta-item">⏱ ${escHtml(durationLabel)}</span>` : ''}
          ${(!isManualTaskRecord(r) && customerMeta && customerMeta.smsCallerAddon) ? '<span class="call-meta-item" style="color:#059669;font-weight:600;">✓ SMS</span>' : ''}
        </div>
        ${taskDueLabel?`<div class="task-due-pill ${taskDueClass}" style="margin-top:6px;">${escHtml(taskDueLabel)}</div>`:''}
        ${(isLiveCall || isProcessingCall || isShortAbandonedCall) && !callSummary ? `<div class="call-summary" style="color:#94A3B8;font-style:italic;">${isLiveCall ? 'Lara nimmt entgegen…' : (isProcessingCall ? 'Zusammenfassung wird erstellt…' : 'Der Anrufer hat aufgelegt, bevor eine Anfrage aufgenommen wurde.')}</div>` : (callSummary?`<div class="call-summary">${escHtml(callSummary)}</div>`:'')}
        ${followUpMetaText?`<div class="followup-meta-line">${escHtml(followUpMetaText)}</div>`:''}
        ${done && closedAt ? `<div class="followup-meta-line">Erledigt am ${escHtml(fmtExactDateTime(closedAt) || fmtDate(closedAt))}</div>` : ''}
        ${callEndInfo && !opts.dashboardView ? `<div style="margin-top:4px;"><span class="chip ${callEndInfo.cls}" style="font-size:11px;">${callEndInfo.label}</span></div>` : ''}
        ${notizText && !opts.dashboardView?`<div class="call-notiz">${escHtml(notizText)}</div>`:''}

        <!-- Expanded Actions -->
        ${showActions && !done?`<div class="call-actions">
          <button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();${isManualTaskRecord(r) ? `openDetail('${r.id}')` : `openFollowupDirect('${r.id}')`}">${isManualTaskRecord(r) ? 'Details' : 'Nachfassen setzen'}</button>
          ${!isManualTaskRecord(r) ? `<button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxEmailForward('${r.id}')">E-Mail</button>` : ''}
          ${isManualTaskRecord(r) ? '' : `<button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openDetail('${r.id}')">Details</button>`}
        </div>`:''}
        ${showActions && done?`<div class="call-actions">
          <button class="btn btn--secondary" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openDetail('${r.id}')">Details</button>
        </div>`:''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
  // Restore expanded state
  if (expandedIds.size > 0) {
    el.querySelectorAll('.call-row[data-id]').forEach(function(r) {
      if (expandedIds.has(r.dataset.id)) r.classList.add('expanded');
    });
  }
  refreshLucideIcons();
}


function vxSplitUpdateActionBar(callId) {
  var splitRight = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right');
  if (!splitRight) return;
  // [B1-FIX] Nur rendern wenn call-detail-page wirklich in split-right und sichtbar
  var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  var existing = document.getElementById('split-action-bar');
  if (!page || !splitRight.contains(page) || page.style.display === 'none') {
    if (existing) existing.remove();
    return;
  }
  if (existing) existing.remove();

  var rec = vxResolveDprRecord(callId);
  if (!rec) return;
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  var phone = f.phone || f.caller_phone || f.tel_link || '';
  var isCallback = f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true';
  var isDone = vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec);
  var isArchived = vxInquiryIsArchived(rec);
  var id = _esc(String(rec.id));
  var ph = _esc(phone);

  // [v2.66] split-action-bar deaktiviert — CTAs sind jetzt im Header (vx-cd-qa)
  // Keine separate Bar mehr nötig
}

function vxRenderRequestsDetailV2(record, options) {
  options = options || {};
  var host = options.hostEl || document.getElementById('requests-detail-v2');
  if (!host) return;

  var f        = (record && record.fields) ? record.fields : {};
  var name     = String(f.caller_name || '').trim();
  var phone    = String(f.phone || f.caller_phone || f.tel_link || '').trim();
  var rawDate  = f.created_at || f.received_at || '';
  var date     = rawDate ? fmtDate(rawDate) : '';
  var status   = String(f.status || f.dashboard_status || '').trim();
  var catRaw   = String(f.category || '').trim();
  var category = (function(c) {
    var cl = c.toLowerCase();
    if (cl.indexOf('termin best\u00e4tigt') !== -1 || cl.indexOf('termin best\u00e4tigen') !== -1) return 'Terminwunsch erkannt';
    if (cl.indexOf('termin geplant') !== -1) return 'Terminabkl\u00e4rung offen';
    return c;
  })(catRaw);
  var leadQual = String(f.lead_quality || '').trim();
  var source   = String(f.source || '').trim();
  var durSec   = f.duration_seconds;
  var duration = (durSec != null && durSec !== '' && !isNaN(Number(durSec)) && Number(durSec) > 0)
                 ? _formatDuration(durSec) : '';
  var summary  = (typeof vxGetCallSummary === 'function')
                 ? vxGetCallSummary(record)
                 : String(f.call_summary || f.summary || '').trim();
  var note     = String(f.notes_customer_voxera || '').trim();
  var isDone   = (typeof vxInquiryIsCompleted === 'function') && vxInquiryIsCompleted(record);
  var isPlanned = (typeof vxInquiryIsPlanned === 'function') && vxInquiryIsPlanned(record);

  // Konsistente Werte — immer gesetzt, auch bei fehlenden Daten
  var displayName = name || phone || 'Unbekannte Nummer';
  var displayPhone = phone || '';
  var displayMeta = [date, duration].filter(Boolean).join(' \u00b7 ') || '\u2014';
  var initials = name
    ? name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 2).map(function(w) { return w[0] || ''; }).join('').toUpperCase()
    : (phone ? phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-2) : '?');

  // Chips — immer mit vxLabel, immer gleiche Reihenfolge: Status / Kategorie / Lead
  var chipBase = 'display:inline-block;padding:3px 9px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;font-weight:500;margin:0;';
  var chips = [];
  if (status) chips.push('<span style="'+chipBase+'background:var(--vx-bg);color:var(--vx-text2);border:0.5px solid var(--vx-border-md);">'+_esc(vxLabel(status))+'</span>');
  if (category) chips.push('<span style="'+chipBase+'background:var(--vx-blue-light);color:var(--vx-blue);border:0.5px solid var(--vx-blue-bd);">'+_esc(vxLabel(category))+'</span>');
  if (leadQual) {
    var lqk = leadQual.toLowerCase();
    var lqBg = lqk==='hot'?'var(--vx-red-light)':lqk==='warm'?'var(--vx-gold-light)':'var(--vx-bg)';
    var lqFg = lqk==='hot'?'var(--vx-red)':lqk==='warm'?'#B45309':'var(--vx-text3)';
    var lqBd = lqk==='hot'?'var(--vx-red-bd)':lqk==='warm'?'var(--vx-gold-bd)':'var(--vx-border-md)';
    chips.push('<span style="'+chipBase+'background:'+lqBg+';color:'+lqFg+';border:0.5px solid '+lqBd+';">'+_esc(vxLabel(leadQual))+'</span>');
  }
  var chipRowHtml = '<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:8px;">'+chips.join('')+'</div>';

  // Header — immer gleiche Struktur
  var headerHtml =
    '<div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;padding:16px 16px 12px;border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--vx-border);">' +
      '<div style="flex-shrink:0;width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--vx-night);color:#fff;' +
           'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:500;font-size:15px;">' +
        _esc(initials) +
      '</div>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
        '<div style="font-weight:500;font-size:15px;color:var(--vx-text);white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">' + _esc(displayName) + '</div>' +
        (displayPhone ? '<div style="color:var(--vx-blue);font-size:13px;margin-top:1px;">' + _esc(displayPhone) + '</div>' : '') +
        '<div style="color:var(--vx-text3);font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;">' + _esc(displayMeta) + '</div>' +
        chipRowHtml +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>';

  // Action-Bar — immer vollständig, isDone-State beeinflusst nur Erledigt-Button
  var btnPrimary = 'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:7px 14px;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;';
  var btnSecondary = btnPrimary + 'background:var(--vx-bg);color:var(--vx-text);border:0.5px solid var(--vx-border-md);';
  var btnDone = btnPrimary + 'background:var(--vx-green-light);color:var(--vx-green);border:0.5px solid var(--vx-green-bd);';
  var actionsHtml = '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--vx-bg);border-bottom:0.5px solid var(--vx-border);flex-wrap:wrap;">';
  if (displayPhone) {
    actionsHtml += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailAnrufen()" style="'+btnPrimary+'background:var(--vx-night);color:#fff;"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone" style="font-size:13px;"></i>Anrufen</button>';
  }
  actionsHtml += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailFaelligkeit()" style="'+btnSecondary+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-plus" style="font-size:13px;"></i>R\u00fcckruf planen</button>';
  if (!isDone) {
    actionsHtml += '<button onclick="vxCallDetailErledigt()" style="'+btnDone+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" style="font-size:13px;"></i>Erledigt</button>';
  }
  actionsHtml += '</div>';

  // Card-Wrapper
  var cardStyle = 'background:var(--vx-surface);border-radius:12px;border:0.5px solid var(--vx-border);padding:14px;';
  var labelStyle = 'color:var(--vx-text3);font-size:12px;margin-bottom:2px;';
  var valueStyle = 'font-size:13px;color:var(--vx-text);';

  // Anliegen-Karte — immer anzeigen, auch leer
  var anliegenHtml =
    '<div style="'+cardStyle+'">' +
      '<div style="font-weight:500;font-size:13px;color:var(--vx-text);margin-bottom:10px;">Anliegen</div>';
  if (summary) {
    anliegenHtml +=
      '<div style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--vx-text2);margin-bottom:8px;">' + _esc(summary) + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--vx-text3);">automatisch erstellt</div>';
  } else {
    anliegenHtml +=
      '<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--vx-text3);font-style:italic;">Keine Zusammenfassung verf\u00fcgbar.</div>';
  }
  if (category) {
    anliegenHtml += '<div style="margin-top:10px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Kategorie</div><div style="'+valueStyle+'">'+_esc(vxLabel(category))+'</div></div>';
  }
  if (leadQual) {
    anliegenHtml += '<div style="margin-top:6px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Lead-Qualit\u00e4t</div><div style="'+valueStyle+'">'+_esc(vxLabel(leadQual))+'</div></div>';
  }
  anliegenHtml += '</div>';

  // Kontakt-Karte — immer anzeigen
  var kontaktHtml =
    '<div style="'+cardStyle+'">' +
      '<div style="font-weight:500;font-size:13px;color:var(--vx-text);margin-bottom:10px;">Kontakt</div>';
  if (name) kontaktHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:6px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Name</div><div style="'+valueStyle+'">'+_esc(name)+'</div></div>';
  if (displayPhone) kontaktHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:6px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Telefon</div><div style="font-size:13px;"><a href="tel:'+_esc(displayPhone)+'" style="color:var(--vx-blue);text-decoration:none;">'+_esc(displayPhone)+'</a></div></div>';
  if (date) kontaktHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:6px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Eingang</div><div style="'+valueStyle+'">'+_esc(date)+'</div></div>';
  if (source) kontaktHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:6px;"><div style="'+labelStyle+'">Quelle</div><div style="'+valueStyle+'">'+_esc(source)+'</div></div>';
  kontaktHtml += '</div>';

  // Notizen-Karte
  var notizenHtml =
    '<div style="'+cardStyle+'">' +
      '<div style="font-weight:500;font-size:13px;color:var(--vx-text);margin-bottom:10px;">Notizen</div>' +
      '<textarea id="vx-dv2-note" style="width:100%;min-height:80px;padding:8px 10px;border:0.5px solid var(--vx-border-md);' +
        'border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;resize:vertical;box-sizing:border-box;font-family:inherit;background:var(--vx-bg);color:var(--vx-text);">' +
        _esc(note) +
      '</textarea>' +
      '<button id="vx-dv2-save-btn" onclick="vxDv2SaveNote()" style="'+btnPrimary+'background:var(--vx-night);color:#fff;margin-top:8px;">Speichern</button>' +
    '</div>';

  host.innerHTML =
    '<div style="overflow-y:auto;height:100%;background:var(--vx-bg);">' +
      headerHtml + actionsHtml +
      '<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;padding:14px;">' +
        anliegenHtml + kontaktHtml + notizenHtml +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>';
}

// [v2.64] Header-Zähler für Unified Inbox — zählt Calls UND Tasks
// [PR-C] Archiv-Row Click-Handler
function vxArchivRowClick(e, cardEl) {
  if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
  var recId = cardEl && cardEl.dataset && cardEl.dataset.id;
  if (!recId) return;

  var isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;

  if (isMobile) {
    // Mobile: Vollbild wie Anfragen
    if (typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(recId);
    if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(recId);
    return;
  }

  // Desktop: in archiv-split-right einbetten
  var splitRight = document.getElementById('archiv-split-right');
  var splitEmpty = document.getElementById('archiv-split-empty');
  var detailHost = document.getElementById('archiv-detail-v2');

  if (!splitRight || !detailHost) {
    // Fallback
    if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(recId);
    return;
  }

  // Active row markieren
  if (typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(recId);
  window._vxActiveRecordId = recId;
  window._vxCurrentCallId = recId;

  // Record auflösen
  var rec = null;
  if (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') rec = vxResolveDprRecord(recId);
  if (!rec && Array.isArray(allRecords)) {
    rec = allRecords.find(function(r) { return String((r && r.id) || '') === String(recId); }) || null;
  }

  if (!rec) {
    detailHost.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:2rem;color:var(--slate2);font-size:13px;">Wird geladen…</div>';
    if (splitEmpty) splitEmpty.style.display = 'none';
    detailHost.style.display = 'flex';
    return;
  }

  if (splitEmpty) splitEmpty.style.display = 'none';
  detailHost.style.display = 'block';

  // Detail rendern — nutzt denselben Renderer wie Anfragen
  if (typeof vxRenderRequestsDetailV2 === 'function') {
    vxRenderRequestsDetailV2(rec, { hostEl: detailHost });
  } else if (typeof renderCallDetailPage === 'function') {
    detailHost.innerHTML = '';
    renderCallDetailPage(rec.fields || rec, []);
  }
}

function vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() {
  var calls = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
  var tasks = (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : (window.allManualTaskRecords || []);
  return [].concat(calls || [], tasks || []).filter(Boolean).filter(function(r) {
    return isManualTaskRecord(r) || isCustomerVisibleCall(r);
  });
}

// [v3.4] Anfragen-Nav-Badge folgt dem echten Ungelesen-State (read_at), nicht nur dem alten Seen-Storage.
function vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges(countsOverride) {
  var source = (typeof vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords === 'function') ? vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() : [];
  var counts = countsOverride || ((typeof vxGetEntryCounts === 'function') ? vxGetEntryCounts(source) : null) || {};
  var unreadCount = Number(counts.unreadNew || 0);
  var badgeA = document.getElementById('badge-anrufe');
  var mb1 = document.getElementById('mnav-badge-anrufe');
  var label = unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : String(unreadCount || 0);
  if (badgeA) { badgeA.textContent = label; badgeA.style.display = unreadCount > 0 ? '' : 'none'; }
  if (mb1) { mb1.textContent = label; mb1.style.display = unreadCount > 0 ? 'block' : 'none'; }
}

// [v2.64] Header-Zähler für Unified Inbox — zählt Calls UND Tasks
function vxUpdateAnfragenCount() {
  var source = (typeof vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords === 'function') ? vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() : [];
  var counts = (typeof vxGetEntryCounts === 'function') ? vxGetEntryCounts(source) : { new:0, planned:0, done:0, unreadNew:0, total:0 };

  vxDebugLog('[anfragen:entry-count-source]', {
    sourceLength: source.length,
    counts: counts,
    sample: source.slice(0, 3).map(function(r) {
      var vm = (typeof vxBuildEntryViewModel === 'function') ? vxBuildEntryViewModel(r) : null;
      return vm ? { id: vm.id, recordType: vm.recordType, workBucket: vm.workBucket, isUnread: vm.isUnread, typeLabel: vm.typeLabel } : null;
    }).filter(Boolean)
  });

  var countEl = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-count');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = counts.new + ' offen · ' + counts.planned + ' geplant · ' + counts.done + ' erledigt';

  var mainWrap = document.querySelector('#anrufe-split-left .vx-split-filters');
  if (mainWrap) {
    var inboxBtn = mainWrap.querySelector('[data-filter="offen"]');
    var plannedBtn = mainWrap.querySelector('[data-filter="geplant"]');
    var doneBtn = mainWrap.querySelector('[data-filter="abgeschlossen"]');
    var allBtn = mainWrap.querySelector('[data-filter="alle"]');
    if (inboxBtn) inboxBtn.textContent = 'Offen (' + counts.new + ')';
    if (plannedBtn) plannedBtn.textContent = 'Geplant (' + counts.planned + ')';
    if (doneBtn) doneBtn.textContent = 'Erledigt (' + counts.done + ')';
    if (allBtn) allBtn.textContent = 'Alle (' + (counts.all || counts.total) + ')';
  }

  var subWrap = document.getElementById('anrufe-inbox-subfilters');
  if (subWrap) {
    subWrap.querySelectorAll('[data-inbox-subfilter]').forEach(function(btn) {
      if (btn.getAttribute('data-inbox-subfilter') === 'unread') {
        btn.textContent = 'Nur ungelesene' + (counts.unreadNew ? ' (' + counts.unreadNew + ')' : '');
        btn.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', _anrufeInboxSubfilter === 'unread');
      }
    });
  }
  if (typeof vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges === 'function') vxUpdateAnfragenNavBadges(counts);
}

function renderAnrufe() {
  var filter = vxGetAnfragenActiveFilter();
  vxSyncAnfragenFilterChips(filter);
  // [v2.65] Unified Inbox: immer intern aus allRecords + allManualTaskRecords lesen
  // Parameter wird ignoriert — verhindert dass Task-Array beim Aufruf wegfällt
  var source = (typeof vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords === 'function') ? vxGetAnfragenSourceRecords() : [];
  var filtered;
  if (window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly) {
    filtered = source.filter(function(r) {
      return !isManualTaskRecord(r) && isCustomerVisibleCall(r) && vxHeuteRecordCreatedToday(r) && !vxHeuteIsOutboundOrTestRecord(r);
    });
  } else {
    filtered = source.filter(function(r) { return vxInquiryMatchesFilter(r, filter); });
  }
  if (!window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly && filter === 'offen' && _anrufeInboxSubfilter === 'unread') {
    filtered = filtered.filter(function(r) { return vxIsUnreadRecord(r); });
  }
  var _searchQ = String(window._anrufeSearchQuery || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (_searchQ) {
    filtered = filtered.filter(function(r) {
      var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
      var haystack = [
        r && r.name, r && r.caller_name, r && r.phone, r && r.caller_phone, r && r.phone_number,
        r && r.summary, r && r.transcript, r && r.category, r && r.lead_quality, r && r.dashboard_status,
        f.name, f.caller_name, f.phone, f.caller_phone, f.phone_number,
        f.call_summary, f.summary, f.transcript, f.category, f.lead_quality, f.dashboard_status
      ].filter(function(v){ return v != null; }).map(function(v){ return String(v).toLowerCase(); }).join(' ');
      return haystack.indexOf(_searchQ) !== -1;
    });
  }
  if (typeof vxUpdateAnfragenCount === 'function') vxUpdateAnfragenCount();
  var sorted = applySortToRecords(filtered, _anrufeSort);
  if (filter === 'offen') {
    var rank = { live:0, processing:1, checking:2, ready:3 };
    sorted = sorted.slice().sort(function(a,b){
      var ar = rank[(vxGetCallDisplayState(a)||{}).state] ?? 3;
      var br = rank[(vxGetCallDisplayState(b)||{}).state] ?? 3;
      if (ar !== br) return ar - br;
      return 0;
    });
  }
  renderAnrufeInbox('anrufe-list', sorted, filter);
  if (window._vxActiveRecordId && typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(window._vxActiveRecordId);
  if (window._vxAnfragenTodayOnly) {
    var countEl = document.getElementById('anrufe-split-count');
    if (countEl) countEl.textContent = sorted.length + ' Anrufe heute';
  }
}

function vxGetCallStableId(record) {
  if (!record) return '';
  var f = record.fields || {};
  return String(record.id || f.id || record.call_id || f.call_id || '').trim();
}

function vxIsLiveCall(record) {
  if (!record || isManualTaskRecord(record)) return false;
  var f = record.fields || {};
  var liveStatus = String((f.live_status !== undefined ? f.live_status : record.live_status) || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return ['live', 'ringing', 'in_progress', 'processing', 'incoming', 'active'].indexOf(liveStatus) !== -1;
}

function vxGetLiveCallIdSet(records) {
  var ids = new Set();
  (records || []).forEach(function(rec) {
    if (!rec || !isCustomerVisibleCall(rec) || !vxIsLiveCall(rec)) return;
    var stableId = vxGetCallStableId(rec);
    if (stableId) ids.add(stableId);
  });
  return ids;
}

function vxIsExcludedNormalCall(rec, liveCallIds) {
  if (!rec || !rec.fields || isManualTaskRecord(rec)) return false;
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  var category = String(f.category || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  var direction = String(f.direction || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (category === 'activation_test_outbound') return true;
  if (direction === 'outbound') return true;
  if (vxIsLiveCall(rec)) return true;
  var stableId = vxGetCallStableId(rec);
  return !!(stableId && liveCallIds && liveCallIds.has(stableId));
}

function renderAnrufeInbox(containerId, records, activeFilter) {

  var el = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!el) return;
  el.classList.add('dpr-command-center');
  var filter = vxGetAnfragenActiveFilter(activeFilter);
  var _selectionMode = false;

  if (!records || records.length === 0) {
    var emptyLabel = vxInquiryFilterEmptyText(filter);
    el.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:24px 20px;text-align:center;color:var(--slate2);font-size:13px;background:#fff;border-radius:0 0 14px 14px;">' +
      '<i class="ph-bold ph-tray" style="color:#1A6FE8;margin-bottom:8px;display:block;margin-left:auto;margin-right:auto;"></i>' +
      _esc(emptyLabel) + '</div>';
    vxRenderInboxSelectionToolbar();
    refreshLucideIcons();
    return;
  }

  var liveCallIds = vxGetLiveCallIdSet(allRecords || []);
  var visibleRecords = (records || []).filter(function(rec) {
    return !vxIsExcludedNormalCall(rec, liveCallIds);
  });

  var groups = {};
  var groupOrder = [];
  var _rtz = 'Europe/Zurich';
  var _rfd = function(d) { return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-CH',{timeZone:_rtz,year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date(d)); };
  var _rtdk = _rfd(new Date()); var _ryek = _rfd(new Date(Date.now()-86400000));

  visibleRecords.forEach(function(rec) {
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    var isDone = vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec) || vxInquiryIsArchived(rec);
    var ts = (isDone && (f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at)) ? (f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at) : getRecordTimestamp(rec);
    var d = ts ? parseCallTimestamp(ts) : null;
    var label;
    if (!d) { label = 'Unbekannt'; }
    else {
      var dk = _rfd(d);
      if (dk === _rtdk) label = 'Heute';
      else if (dk === _ryek) label = 'Gestern';
      else label = d.toLocaleDateString('de-CH', {day:'numeric',month:'short',year:'numeric',timeZone:_rtz});
    }
    if (!groups[label]) { groups[label] = []; groupOrder.push(label); }
    groups[label].push(rec);
  });

  function buildInquiryDueMeta(rec, linkedTasks) {
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    if (vxInquiryIsArchived(rec)) {
      var tsArchived = f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at || f.archived_at || getRecordTimestamp(rec);
      return { label: 'Erledigt', value: tsArchived ? fmtExactDateTime(tsArchived) : 'erledigt', state: 'incoming', group: 'calls' };
    }
    if (vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec)) {
      var tsClosed = f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at || getRecordTimestamp(rec);
      return { label: 'Erledigt', value: tsClosed ? fmtExactDateTime(tsClosed) : 'erledigt', state: 'incoming', group: 'calls' };
    }
    if (vxInquiryIsPlanned(rec)) {
      var due = f.follow_up_at || f.followup_at || f.followUpAt || '';
      return { label: 'Geplant', value: due ? fmtExactDateTime(due) : 'geplant', state: 'planned', group: 'calls' };
    }
    if (vxInquiryIsNew(rec)) {
      var tsNew = getRecordTimestamp(rec);
      return { label: 'Offen', value: tsNew ? _formatRelativeTime(tsNew) : 'offen', state: 'today', group: 'calls' };
    }
    var ts = getRecordTimestamp(rec);
    return { label: 'Eingegangen', value: ts ? _formatRelativeTime(ts) : '', state: 'incoming', group: 'calls' };
  }

  function buildInquiryDescription(rec, linkedTasks, category, summary) {
    var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
    // Primär: Kategorie oder next_action — das ist der aussagekräftigste Inhalt
    var mainContent = '';
    if (f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true') {
      mainContent = 'Rückruf erbeten';
    } else if (f.next_action && !vxDashIsGenericLabel(f.next_action)) {
      mainContent = String(f.next_action).trim();
    } else if (category && !isDashLikePlaceholder(category)) {
      mainContent = category;
    } else if (summary) {
      mainContent = String(summary).slice(0, 60).trim() + (summary.length > 60 ? '…' : '');
    } else {
      mainContent = 'Eingehender Anruf';
    }
    // Aufgaben-Hinweis kompakt
    var taskHint = '';
    if (linkedTasks.length === 1) taskHint = '· 1 Aufgabe offen';
    else if (linkedTasks.length > 1) taskHint = '· ' + linkedTasks.length + ' Aufgaben offen';
    return mainContent + (taskHint ? ' ' + taskHint : '');
  }

  function buildInquiryMeta(record, linkedTasks) {
    var status = vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus(record);
    var out = [];
    if (status === 'archived') out.push('Archiviert');
    else if (status === 'done') out.push('Erledigt');
    else if (status === 'planned') out.push('Geplant');
    else out.push('Offen');
    if (linkedTasks.length > 0) out.push('mit Aufgabe');
    return out.join(' · ');
  }

  var html = '';
  groupOrder.forEach(function(label) {
    html += '<div class="vx-date-sep"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-dots" aria-hidden="true"></i>' + _esc(label) + '</div>';
    groups[label].forEach(function(rec) {
      var f = rec.fields || {};
      var phone = String(f.caller_phone || f.phone || f.tel_link || '').trim();
      var urgencyVal = String(f.urgency || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      var urgencyBadge = '';
      if (urgencyVal === 'sofort') {
        urgencyBadge = '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;background:#FEF2F2;color:#DC2626;border:0.5px solid #FECACA;margin-left:4px;"><i class=\"ph-bold ph-warning-circle\" style=\"font-size:10px;\"></i>Sofort</span>';
      } else if (urgencyVal === 'heute') {
        urgencyBadge = '<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:20px;background:#FFFBEB;color:#B45309;border:0.5px solid #E8C547;margin-left:4px;"><i class=\"ph-bold ph-clock\" style=\"font-size:10px;\"></i>Heute</span>';
      }
      var linkedTasks = vxFindOpenManualTasksForPhone(phone);
      var dueMeta = buildInquiryDueMeta(rec, linkedTasks);
      var name = vxDashResolveWorkTitle(rec);
      var company = (f.company_name && f.caller_name && f.company_name !== f.caller_name) ? f.company_name : '';
      // [v2.69] Einheitliche Beschreibung via vxDashGetTaskDescription
      var _typeMeta2 = vxDashGetCommandTypeMeta(rec);
      var description = vxDashGetTaskDescription(rec, _typeMeta2, name);
      // Meta-Zeile: nur Telefon
      var metaLine = (phone && name !== phone) ? _esc(phone) : '';
      var action = 'detail';
      var actionLabel = vxInquiryIsNew(rec) ? 'Prüfen' : 'Details';
      var actionIcon = vxInquiryIsNew(rec) ? 'check-circle' : 'caret-right';
      var actionClass = 'is-soft-blue';
      if ((f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true') && phone && linkedTasks.length === 0 && !vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec)) {
        action = 'call'; actionLabel = 'Jetzt anrufen'; actionIcon = 'phone-call'; actionClass = 'is-call';
      }
      // [v2.67] Typ-Pill statt Status-Pill — einheitlich mit Heute-Tab
      var _typeMeta = vxDashGetCommandTypeMeta(rec);
      var _stateMeta = (typeof vxGetCallDisplayState === 'function' ? vxGetCallDisplayState(rec) : null) || vxGetDefaultCallDisplayState();
      var iconClass = _typeMeta.pillClass;
      var iconName = _typeMeta.icon;
      var _archOrDone = vxInquiryIsArchived(rec) || vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec);
      var _isOnToday = typeof vxCallIsOnToday === 'function' && vxCallIsOnToday(rec);
      var _isCallback2 = f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true';
      var _railAnfragen = _archOrDone ? 'done'
        : urgencyVal === 'sofort' ? 'urgent'
        : (_isOnToday || vxInquiryIsPlanned(rec)) ? 'planned'
        : _isCallback2 ? 'callback'
        : 'new';
      // Beschreibung: KI-Zusammenfassung / Notiz via einheitliche Funktion
      var _unifiedDesc = vxDashGetTaskDescription(rec, _typeMeta, name);
      // Pill-Label: für erledigte/archivierte Records Status zeigen, sonst Typ
      var _pillLabel = vxInquiryIsArchived(rec) ? 'Archiviert'
        : vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec) ? 'Erledigt'
        : (_stateMeta.state === 'live' ? 'LIVE' : (_stateMeta.state === 'processing' ? 'Wird verarbeitet' : (_stateMeta.state === 'checking' ? 'Wird geprüft' : _typeMeta.label)));
      var _pillClass = vxInquiryIsArchived(rec) ? 'dpr-kind-default'
        : vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec) ? 'dpr-kind-done'
        : _typeMeta.pillClass;
      // Meta-Zeile: nur Telefon
      var _metaSimple = (phone && name !== phone) ? _esc(phone) : '';
      // [v3.0] Outlook-Pattern mit vx-* Klassen
      var _isUnread = (typeof vxIsUnreadRecord === 'function') ? vxIsUnreadRecord(rec) : (!isManualTaskRecord(rec) && !f.read_at);
      var _isDoneOrArch = vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec) || vxInquiryIsArchived(rec);
      var _isCallIcon = _typeMeta.kind === 'callback' || _typeMeta.kind === 'incoming';
      var _rowOpacity = '';

      // Status-Pill
      var _statusPillClass = 'vx-anf-pill';
      var _statusLabel = dueMeta.label;
      if (_isDoneOrArch) _statusPillClass += ' is-done';
      else if (vxInquiryIsPlanned(rec)) _statusPillClass += ' is-planned';
      else if (_isUnread) _statusPillClass += ' is-new';

      // Lead quality pill
      var _leadQ = String((rec.fields||{}).lead_quality||'').toLowerCase();
      var _leadPill = '';
      if (_leadQ === 'hot') _leadPill = '<span class="vx-anf-pill is-hot">Hot</span>';
      else if (_leadQ === 'warm') _leadPill = '<span class="vx-anf-pill" style="background:var(--vx-gold-light);border-color:var(--vx-gold-bd);color:#B45309;">Warm</span>';

      // Archiv: Lösch-Warnung (90-Tage-Regel)
      var _expiryPill = '';
      if (vxInquiryIsArchived(rec) && f.updated_at) {
        var _archivedAt = parseCallTimestamp(f.updated_at);
        if (_archivedAt) {
          var _daysLeft = Math.ceil(((_archivedAt.getTime() + 90*86400000) - Date.now()) / 86400000);
          if (_daysLeft <= 30 && _daysLeft > 0) {
            var _expiryClass = _daysLeft <= 7 ? 'vx-archive-expiry' : 'vx-archive-expiry is-near';
            _expiryPill = '<span class="'+_expiryClass+'">⏱ '+_daysLeft+'d</span>';
          } else if (_daysLeft <= 0) {
            _expiryPill = '<span class="vx-archive-expiry">Läuft ab</span>';
          }
        }
      }

      // Preview text
      var _preview = '';
      if (_stateMeta.state === 'live') _preview = 'Lara nimmt den Anruf gerade entgegen.';
      else if (_stateMeta.state === 'processing') _preview = 'Zusammenfassung wird erstellt.';
      else if (_stateMeta.state === 'checking') _preview = 'Zusammenfassung wird erstellt.';
      else { _preview = String(f.call_summary || f.summary || f.note || '').trim(); if (_preview.length > 65) _preview = _preview.slice(0,62) + '…'; }

      var _activeRenderId = String(window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId || '');
      var _activeClass = (_activeRenderId && String(rec.id) === _activeRenderId) ? ' sp-active' : '';
      var _isSelected = vxInboxIsSelected(rec.id);
      html += '<div class="dpr-card'+_activeClass+'" data-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'" data-phone="'+_esc(phone)+'" data-kind="inquiry" data-rail="'+_railAnfragen+'" data-filter-new="'+(_isUnread?'1':'0')+'" data-filter-task="'+(linkedTasks.length>0?'1':'0')+'" data-filter-planned="'+((_isOnToday||vxInquiryIsPlanned(rec))?'1':'0')+'" data-filter-checked="'+(_isDoneOrArch?'1':'0')+'" data-filter-archived="'+(vxInquiryIsArchived(rec)?'1':'0')+'">';
      var _rowRailClass = _railAnfragen === 'urgent' ? 'rail-overdue' : (_railAnfragen === 'planned' ? 'rail-planned' : 'rail-today');
      html += '<div class="vx-heute-row '+_rowRailClass+(_isUnread?' is-unread':'')+(_selectionMode?' is-selection-mode':'')+(_isSelected?' is-selected':'')+'"'+_rowOpacity+' onclick="dprToggleClick(event,this)" ontouchstart="dprToggleTouchStart(event,this)" ontouchmove="dprToggleTouchMove(event)" ontouchend="dprToggleTouchEnd(event,this)" data-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'" data-record-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'">';
      if (_selectionMode) html += '<span class="vx-inbox-select-dot" aria-hidden="true"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" style="font-size:10px;"></i></span>';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-icon'+(_isCallIcon?' is-call':'')+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-'+iconName+'" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-body">';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-name" style="'+(_isUnread?'font-weight:700;':'')+'">'+(_isUnread?'<span style="display:inline-block;width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:#1A6FE8;margin-right:7px;vertical-align:middle;"></span>':'')+_esc(name)+(company?'<span class="dpr-co">'+_esc(company)+'</span>':'')+(_isUnread?'<span class="vx-row-pill" style="background:#DBEAFE;color:#1A6FE8;border-color:#BFDBFE;">Neu</span>':'')+'<span class="vx-row-pill">'+_esc(_pillLabel)+'</span></div>';
      if (dueMeta && dueMeta.value) html += '<div class="vx-row-time">'+_esc(dueMeta.value)+'</div>';
      if (_preview) html += '<div class="vx-row-summary">'+_esc(_preview)+'</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      // [v3.3] Anfragen-Aktionslogik: gleiche Hierarchie wie Heute.
      // Sichtbar bleibt nur eine Hauptaktion + Overflow; Status-, Planungs- und Archivaktionen liegen im Menü.
      var _primaryAnfrage = (typeof vxTodayGetPrimaryAction === 'function')
        ? vxTodayGetPrimaryAction(rec, _typeMeta, phone)
        : { action:(phone ? 'call' : 'open'), title:(phone ? 'Anrufen' : 'Öffnen'), icon:(phone ? 'phone-call' : 'arrow-square-out') };
      var _primaryActionAnfrage = _primaryAnfrage.action;
      var _primaryTitleAnfrage = _primaryAnfrage.title;
      var _primaryIconAnfrage = _primaryAnfrage.icon;
      var _primaryClassAnfrage = 'vx-ibtn vx-ibtn-main ' + (_primaryActionAnfrage === 'done' ? 'is-open' : (_primaryActionAnfrage === 'call' ? 'is-ghost-call' : 'is-open'));
      html += '<div class="vx-row-btns vx-row-btns--single">';
      html += '<button type="button" class="'+_primaryClassAnfrage+'" data-action="'+_primaryActionAnfrage+'" data-rec-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'" data-phone="'+_esc(phone)+'" title="'+_esc(_primaryTitleAnfrage)+'" aria-label="'+_esc(_primaryTitleAnfrage)+'"><i class="ph-bold ph-'+_primaryIconAnfrage+'" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>';
      html += '<button type="button" class="vx-ibtn is-overflow" data-action="overflow" data-rec-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'" data-phone="'+_esc(phone)+'" title="Mehr"><i class="ph-bold ph-dots-three"></i></button>';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-menu" id="vx-row-menu-'+_esc(rec.id)+'">';
      html += (typeof vxAnfragenBuildRowMenuHtml === 'function')
        ? vxAnfragenBuildRowMenuHtml(rec, phone, _typeMeta)
        : '<button type="button" data-action="fulldetail" data-rec-id="'+_esc(rec.id)+'">Details öffnen</button>';
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      // Expanded Panel bleibt für Touch-Long-Press (sekundär)
      var isCallback = (f.callback_requested === true || f.callback_requested === 'true');
      var isDone = vxInquiryIsCompleted(rec);
      var isArchived = vxInquiryIsArchived(rec);
      html += '</div>'; // /dpr-card
    });
  });
  el.innerHTML = html;

  // Aktive Row wiederherstellen nach Re-Render
  var _activeId = window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxCurrentCallId || window._vxCurrentTaskId;
  if (_activeId) {
    el.querySelectorAll('.dpr-card').forEach(function(c) {
      c.classList.toggle('sp-active', String(c.dataset.id) === String(_activeId));
    });
  }

  if (!el.dataset.boundClick) {
    el.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var rowEl = e.target.closest('.vx-heute-row');
      if (window._vxInboxSelectionMode && rowEl) {
        var rowId = vxInboxResolveRowRecordId(e.target) || vxInboxResolveRowRecordId(rowEl);
        if (rowId) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          vxInboxToggleSelected(rowId);
          renderAnrufe();
          return;
        }
        var blockedActionBtn = e.target.closest('button, [data-action]');
        if (blockedActionBtn) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          return;
        }
      }
      var overflowBtn = e.target.closest('[data-action="overflow"]');
      if (overflowBtn) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        var menu = overflowBtn.parentElement && overflowBtn.parentElement.querySelector('.vx-row-menu');
        if (menu) vxOpenFloatingRowMenu(overflowBtn, menu);
        return;
      }
      var card = e.target.closest('.dpr-card');
      if (!card || e.target.closest('.dpr-btn, .va-exp-panel, button')) return;
      var recId = card.dataset.id;
      if (!recId) return;
      if (typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(recId);
      var rec = typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function' ? vxResolveDprRecord(recId) : null;
      if (rec && typeof isManualTaskRecord === 'function' && isManualTaskRecord(rec)) { if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(recId); }
      else if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(recId);
    });
    el.dataset.boundClick = '1';
  }

  vxRenderInboxSelectionToolbar();
  vxUpdateAnfragenCount();
  /* phosphor: no init needed */
  dprRestoreOpen('anrufe-list');
  if (window._vxActiveRecordId && typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(window._vxActiveRecordId);
  vxApplyPendingRowHighlight();
}

function renderRueckrufe(records, manualTasks) {
  const autoTasks = getUnifiedOpenTasks(records || []);
  const manualOpenTasks = (typeof vxGetOpenManualTasks === 'function') ? vxGetOpenManualTasks() : (manualTasks || allManualTaskRecords || []).filter(function(r) {
    var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
    return !vxIsClosedManualTaskStatus(f.status || f.dashboard_status);
  });
  const unifiedTasks = getUnifiedOpenTasks([].concat(autoTasks, manualOpenTasks));

  // Aufgaben-Tab: immer nach Prioritäts-Bucket sortieren (Überfällig → Heute → Geplant → Ohne Termin).
  // Innerhalb des gleichen Buckets: neueste zuerst.
  // Eine "nach Datum"-Umsortierung würde die Bucket-Gruppen durchmischen und die
  // Gruppen-Header unbrauchbar machen — deshalb kein applySortToRecords hier.
  const sorted = [...unifiedTasks].sort(function(a, b) {
    var ba = getTaskPriorityBucket(a);
    var bb = getTaskPriorityBucket(b);
    if (ba !== bb) return ba - bb;
    // Buckets 0/1/2 (überfällig/heute/geplant): nach Fälligkeitsdatum aufsteigend
    if (ba <= 2) {
      var da = parseFollowUpDate(resolveTaskDueAt((a && a.fields) || {}));
      var db = parseFollowUpDate(resolveTaskDueAt((b && b.fields) || {}));
      var ta = da && !Number.isNaN(da.getTime()) ? da.getTime() : Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
      var tb = db && !Number.isNaN(db.getTime()) ? db.getTime() : Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
      if (ta !== tb) return ta - tb;
    }
    // Bucket 3 (ohne Termin): neueste zuerst
    return new Date(getRecordTimestamp(b) || 0) - new Date(getRecordTimestamp(a) || 0);
  });

  // [v2.56 FIX] rueckrufe-list Container wurde in v2.55 entfernt — renderList überspringen
  var _rlEl = document.getElementById('rueckrufe-list');
  if (_rlEl) renderList('rueckrufe-list', sorted, {showActions:true, primaryCallback:true, skipSort:true});
  if (typeof injectTaskGroupHeaders === 'function') injectTaskGroupHeaders('rueckrufe-list');



  // Gespeicherten Filter wiederherstellen
  if (_rueckrufeFilter && _rueckrufeFilter !== 'alle') {
    var listEl = document.getElementById('rueckrufe-list');
    if (listEl) listEl.querySelectorAll('.task-section-header').forEach(function(h) { h.remove(); });
    document.querySelectorAll('#rueckrufe-list .call-row').forEach(function(row) {
      if (_rueckrufeFilter === 'dringend') row.classList.toggle('hidden', row.dataset.priority !== 'urgent');
      else if (_rueckrufeFilter === 'inbearbeitung') row.classList.toggle('hidden', !['in_progress','follow_up_scheduled'].includes(row.dataset.status));
    });
  }
}

// FOCUS MODE — Single-task focus view für Aufgaben-Tab
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var _focusTasks = [];
var _focusIndex = 0;
var _focusModeActive = false;




var _archivFilter = 'all';
var _archivAllDone = [];
var _anrufeFilter = 'offen';
var _anrufeInboxSubfilter = 'all';
window._vxInboxSelectionMode = false;
window._vxInboxSelectedIds = new Set();
var _rueckrufeFilter = 'alle';
var _anrufeSort = 'datum-neu';
var _rueckrufeSort = 'datum-neu';
var _archivSort = 'datum-neu';

function vxInboxIsSelected(recordId) {
  var recId = String(recordId || '').trim();
  return !!(recId && window._vxInboxSelectedIds && window._vxInboxSelectedIds.has(recId));
}
function vxInboxClearSelection() {
  if (!window._vxInboxSelectedIds || typeof window._vxInboxSelectedIds.clear !== 'function') window._vxInboxSelectedIds = new Set();
  window._vxInboxSelectedIds.clear();
}
function vxInboxToggleSelected(recordId) {
  var recId = String(recordId || '').trim();
  if (!recId) return;
  if (!window._vxInboxSelectedIds || typeof window._vxInboxSelectedIds.has !== 'function') window._vxInboxSelectedIds = new Set();
  if (window._vxInboxSelectedIds.has(recId)) window._vxInboxSelectedIds.delete(recId);
  else window._vxInboxSelectedIds.add(recId);
}
function vxInboxResolveSelectedRecords() {
  var ids = Array.from((window._vxInboxSelectedIds && typeof window._vxInboxSelectedIds.values === 'function') ? window._vxInboxSelectedIds.values() : []);
  return ids.map(function(id) {
    var rec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(id) : null;
    if (!rec) rec = vxResolveActionRecord(id);
    return { id: String(id || '').trim(), record: rec || null };
  }).filter(function(entry) { return !!entry.id; });
}
function vxInboxCanBulkDone(record) {
  if (!record) return false;
  if (vxInquiryIsArchived(record) || vxInquiryIsCompleted(record)) return false;
  return vxInquiryIsNew(record) || vxInquiryIsPlanned(record);
}
function vxInboxCanBulkArchive(record) {
  if (!record) return false;
  if (vxInquiryIsArchived(record)) return false;
  var id = String((record && record.id) || '').trim();
  if (!id) return false;
  if (typeof vxCanPerformAction === 'function') return !!vxCanPerformAction(id, 'archive');
  return vxInquiryIsNew(record) || vxInquiryIsPlanned(record) || vxInquiryIsCompleted(record);
}
async function vxInboxRunBulkStatusChange(actionType) {
  var isArchive = actionType === 'archive';
  var targetStatus = isArchive ? CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED : CALL_STATUS.CLOSED;
  var selected = vxInboxResolveSelectedRecords();
  if (!selected.length) return;
  var eligibilityFn = isArchive ? vxInboxCanBulkArchive : vxInboxCanBulkDone;
  var eligible = selected.filter(function(entry) { return eligibilityFn(entry.record); });
  var skipped = selected.length - eligible.length;
  var success = 0;
  var failed = 0;

  for (var i = 0; i < eligible.length; i++) {
    var entry = eligible[i];
    try {
      var ok = await doStatusChange(entry.id, targetStatus, null);
      if (ok !== false) success++;
      else failed++;
    } catch (err) {
      failed++;
      console.warn('[inbox-bulk-action]', { action: actionType, id: entry.id, error: err });
    }
  }

  renderAnrufe(vxGetAnfragenActiveFilter());
  vxInboxExitSelectionMode();

  if (failed > 0) {
    if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Einige Einträge konnten nicht aktualisiert werden.', 'warning');
    else if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('Einige Einträge konnten nicht aktualisiert werden.');
  } else if (success > 0 && skipped > 0) {
    if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(success + ' von ' + selected.length + ' Einträgen ' + (isArchive ? 'archiviert' : 'erledigt') + '. ' + skipped + ' übersprungen.', 'info');
    else if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(success + ' von ' + selected.length + ' Einträgen ' + (isArchive ? 'archiviert' : 'erledigt') + '. ' + skipped + ' übersprungen.');
  } else if (success > 0) {
    if (typeof toast === 'function') toast(success + ' Einträge ' + (isArchive ? 'archiviert' : 'erledigt') + '.', 'success');
    else if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast(success + ' Einträge ' + (isArchive ? 'archiviert' : 'erledigt') + '.');
  } else if (selected.length > 0) {
    if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Keine passenden Einträge für diese Aktion ausgewählt.', 'info');
    else if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('Keine passenden Einträge für diese Aktion ausgewählt.');
  }
}
function vxInboxResolveRowRecordId(target) {
  if (!target || !target.closest) return '';
  var recNode = target.closest('[data-record-id]');
  if (!recNode) recNode = target.closest('[data-id]');
  if (!recNode) recNode = target.closest('.vx-heute-row, .dpr-card');
  if (!recNode) return '';
  return String(
    (recNode.getAttribute && recNode.getAttribute('data-record-id')) ||
    (recNode.getAttribute && recNode.getAttribute('data-id')) ||
    (recNode.dataset ? (recNode.dataset.recordId || recNode.dataset.id) : '') ||
    ''
  ).trim();
}
function vxRenderInboxSelectionToolbar() {
  var mount = document.getElementById('vx-inbox-selection-toolbar');
  var controls = document.getElementById('anrufe-selection-controls');
  if (controls) controls.style.display = 'none';
  if (mount) mount.innerHTML = '';
}
function vxInboxEnterSelectionMode() {
  window._vxInboxSelectionMode = false;
  vxInboxClearSelection();
  vxRenderInboxSelectionToolbar();
  return false;
}
function vxInboxExitSelectionMode() {
  window._vxInboxSelectionMode = false;
  vxInboxClearSelection();
  renderAnrufe(vxGetAnfragenActiveFilter());
}

function applySortToRecords(records, sortKey) {
  var sorted = [...records];
  var getTs = function(r) {
    var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
    // Für erledigte Einträge: Erledigungsdatum verwenden
    return f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at || f.updated_at || getRecordTimestamp(r) || 0;
  };
  if (sortKey === 'datum-neu') {
    sorted.sort(function(a, b) {
      return new Date(getTs(b)) - new Date(getTs(a));
    });
  } else if (sortKey === 'datum-alt') {
    sorted.sort(function(a, b) {
      return new Date(getTs(a)) - new Date(getTs(b));
    });
  } else if (sortKey === 'prioritaet') {
    sorted.sort(function(a, b) {
      var pa = getPriority(a).key === 'urgent' ? 0 : 1;
      var pb = getPriority(b).key === 'urgent' ? 0 : 1;
      if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb;
      return new Date(getTs(b)) - new Date(getTs(a));
    });
  } else if (sortKey === 'name') {
    sorted.sort(function(a, b) {
      var na = (getCallDisplayName(a.fields || {}) || '').toLowerCase();
      var nb = (getCallDisplayName(b.fields || {}) || '').toLowerCase();
      return na.localeCompare(nb, 'de');
    });
  }
  return sorted;
}

function closeSortMenus() {
  ['anrufe','rueckrufe','archiv'].forEach(function(k) {
    var m = document.getElementById(k + '-sort-menu');
    if (m) m.style.display = 'none';
  });
}
function toggleSortMenu(key) {
  var menu = document.getElementById(key + '-sort-menu');
  if (!menu) return;
  var isOpen = menu.style.display !== 'none';
  closeSortMenus();
  if (!isOpen) menu.style.display = 'block';
}
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  if (!e.target.closest('[id$="-sort-btn"],[id$="-sort-menu"]')) closeSortMenus();
});

function injectSortButtons() {
  var configs = [
    { key: 'anrufe', filterEl: document.querySelector('#tab-anrufe .filter-tabs') },
    { key: 'archiv', filterEl: null }
  ];
  configs.forEach(function(cfg) {
    if (!cfg.filterEl) return;
    if (document.getElementById(cfg.key + '-sort-btn')) return;

    // Wrap filter-tabs + sort button in a flex row
    var parent = cfg.filterEl.parentNode;
    var wrapper = document.createElement('div');
    wrapper.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;';

    // Move filter-tabs into wrapper
    parent.insertBefore(wrapper, cfg.filterEl);
    wrapper.appendChild(cfg.filterEl);
    cfg.filterEl.style.marginBottom = '0'; // Remove old margin

    // Create sort button + menu
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.style.cssText = 'position:relative;flex-shrink:0;';

    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.id = cfg.key + '-sort-btn';
    btn.textContent = 'Sortierung';
    btn.style.cssText = 'padding:6px 12px;border-radius:20px;border:1px solid var(--gray200,#E5E7EB);background:#fff;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;color:var(--gray600,#94A3B8);cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;white-space:nowrap;';
    btn.onclick = function() { toggleSortMenu(cfg.key); };

    var menu = document.createElement('div');
    menu.id = cfg.key + '-sort-menu';
    menu.style.cssText = 'display:none;position:absolute;right:0;top:110%;background:#fff;border:0.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.12);z-index:9999;min-width:160px;overflow:hidden;';

    [['datum-neu','Neueste zuerst'],['datum-alt','Älteste zuerst'],['prioritaet','Priorität'],['name','Name A–Z']].forEach(function(opt) {
      var item = document.createElement('button');
      item.textContent = opt[1];
      item.style.cssText = 'display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:10px 14px;border:none;background:none;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;';
      item.onmouseover = function() { this.style.background = '#F8FAFC'; };
      item.onmouseout = function() { this.style.background = 'none'; };
      var k = cfg.key, v = opt[0];
      item.onclick = function() { setSortAndRender(k, v); closeSortMenus(); };
      menu.appendChild(item);
    });

    wrap.appendChild(btn);
    wrap.appendChild(menu);
    wrapper.appendChild(wrap);
  });
}

function setSortAndRender(list, value) {
  if (list === 'anrufe') {
    _anrufeSort = value;
    renderAnrufe();
  } else if (list === 'rueckrufe') {
    _rueckrufeSort = value;
    renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
  } else if (list === 'archiv') {
    _archivSort = value;
    filterArchiv(_archivFilter, null);
  }
}

function renderArchivInbox(containerId, records) {
  var el = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!el) return;

  if (!records || records.length === 0) {
    var emptyMsg = (typeof vxGetInboxEmptyMessage === 'function')
      ? vxGetInboxEmptyMessage('archiv', 'all')
      : 'Noch keine archivierten Einträge.';
    el.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:40px 20px;text-align:center;color:var(--vx-text3);font-size:13px;">'
      + '<i class="ph-bold ph-archive-box" style="font-size:28px;opacity:.25;margin-bottom:8px;display:block;" aria-hidden="true"></i>'
      + '<div>' + _esc(emptyMsg) + '</div></div>';
    return;
  }

  // Datum-Gruppen — identisch zu Anfragen
  var _now = new Date();
  var _rfd2 = function(d) { return d.getFullYear()*10000 + (d.getMonth()+1)*100 + d.getDate(); };
  var _rtdk2 = _rfd2(_now);
  var _ryek2 = _rfd2(new Date(_now.getTime() - 86400000));
  var groups = {}, groupOrder = [];
  records.forEach(function(rec) {
    var ts = getRecordTimestamp(rec);
    var d = ts ? parseCallTimestamp(ts) : null;
    var label;
    if (!d) { label = 'Unbekannt'; }
    else {
      var dk2 = _rfd2(d);
      if (dk2 === _rtdk2) label = 'Heute';
      else if (dk2 === _ryek2) label = 'Gestern';
      else label = d.toLocaleDateString('de-CH', {day:'numeric', month:'short', year:'numeric', timeZone:'Europe/Zurich'});
    }
    if (!groups[label]) { groups[label] = []; groupOrder.push(label); }
    groups[label].push(rec);
  });

  var html = '';
  groupOrder.forEach(function(label) {
    html += '<div class="vx-date-sep"><i class="ph-bold ph-calendar-dots" aria-hidden="true"></i>' + _esc(label) + '</div>';
    groups[label].forEach(function(rec) {
      var f = rec.fields || {};
      var id = _esc(rec.id);
      var phone = String(f.caller_phone || f.phone_number || '').trim();
      var name = vxResolveDisplayName(f, 'Unbekannte Nummer');
      var company = (f.company_name && f.caller_name && f.company_name !== f.caller_name) ? f.company_name : '';
      var _typeMeta = (typeof vxDashGetCommandTypeMeta === 'function') ? vxDashGetCommandTypeMeta(rec) : {icon:'phone-incoming', label:'Anruf', pillClass:'dpr-kind-default', kind:'incoming'};
      var iconName = _typeMeta.icon || 'phone-incoming';
      var _isCallIcon = _typeMeta.kind === 'callback' || _typeMeta.kind === 'incoming';
      var _preview = String(f.call_summary || f.summary || f.note || '').trim();
      if (_preview.length > 65) _preview = _preview.slice(0, 62) + '…';
      var ts2 = getRecordTimestamp(rec);
      var _timeStr = ts2 ? _formatRelativeTime(ts2) : '';
      var _activeRenderId = String(window._vxActiveRecordId || window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId || window._vxCurrentCallId || '');
      var _activeClass = (_activeRenderId && String(rec.id) === _activeRenderId) ? ' sp-active' : '';

      html += '<div class="dpr-card' + _activeClass + '" data-id="' + id + '" data-phone="' + _esc(phone) + '" data-kind="inquiry" data-rail="done" onclick="vxArchivRowClick(event,this)">';
      html += '<div class="vx-heute-row rail-today" data-id="' + id + '" data-record-id="' + id + '">';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-icon' + (_isCallIcon ? ' is-call' : '') + '"><i class="ph-bold ph-' + iconName + '" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-body">';
      html += '<div class="vx-row-name">' + _esc(name) + (company ? '<span class="dpr-co">' + _esc(company) + '</span>' : '') + '<span class="vx-row-pill dpr-kind-done">Archiviert</span></div>';
      if (_timeStr) html += '<div class="vx-row-time">' + _esc(_timeStr) + '</div>';
      if (_preview) html += '<div class="vx-row-summary">' + _esc(_preview) + '</div>';
      html += '</div>';
      // Wiederherstellen — kompakter Button rechts
      html += '<div class="vx-row-btns vx-row-btns--single" onclick="event.stopPropagation();">';
      html += '<button type="button" class="vx-ibtn is-open" data-restore-id="' + id + '" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxArchivRestore(this.dataset.restoreId)" title="Wiederherstellen" aria-label="Wiederherstellen" style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;overflow:hidden;"><i class="ph-bold ph-arrow-u-up-left" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>';
      html += '</div>';
      html += '</div>'; // vx-heute-row
      html += '</div>'; // dpr-card
    });
  });

  el.innerHTML = html;
  if (window._vxActiveRecordId && typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') vxSetActiveRequestRow(window._vxActiveRecordId);
  vxApplyPendingRowHighlight();
}

function filterArchiv(period, btn) {
  _archivFilter = period;

  // Sync chip active state
  var archivChips = document.querySelectorAll('#tab-archiv .vx-chip');
  archivChips.forEach(function(c) { c.classList.remove('vx-chip--active'); });
  if (btn) btn.classList.add('vx-chip--active');
  else {
    var activeChip = document.querySelector('#tab-archiv .vx-chip[data-archiv-filter="'+period+'"]');
    if (activeChip) activeChip.classList.add('vx-chip--active');
  }

  // Always sync from allRecords — nur echte archived Records
  if (allRecords && allRecords.length) {
    _archivAllDone = allRecords.filter(function(r) {
      return normalizeStatus((r.fields || {}).dashboard_status) === CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED;
    });
  }
  var now = new Date();
  var filtered = _archivAllDone.slice();

  function _archivTs(r) {
    var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
    var raw = f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at || getRecordTimestamp(r);
    if (!raw) return null;
    var s = String(raw).trim().replace(' ', 'T');
    if (!/[Zz]$/.test(s) && !/[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2}$/.test(s)) s += 'Z';
    var d = new Date(s);
    return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
  }

  if (period === 'week') {
    var weekAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7*24*60*60*1000);
    filtered = _archivAllDone.filter(function(r) { var d = _archivTs(r); return d && d >= weekAgo; });
  } else if (period === 'month') {
    var monthAgo = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth()-1, now.getDate());
    filtered = _archivAllDone.filter(function(r) { var d = _archivTs(r); return d && d >= monthAgo; });
  } else if (period === 'today') {
    var todayStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
    filtered = _archivAllDone.filter(function(r) { var d = _archivTs(r); return d && d >= todayStart; });
  }
  renderArchivInbox('archiv-list', applySortToRecords(filtered, _archivSort));
}

function renderArchiv(records) {
  const done = records.filter(r => {
    var s = normalizeStatus((r.fields || {}).dashboard_status);
    return s === CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED;
  });
  _archivAllDone = done;

  const exportBtn = document.getElementById('archiv-export-btn');
  const statsSub = document.getElementById('archiv-stats-sub');

  if (done.length > 0) {
    if (exportBtn) exportBtn.style.display = 'flex';

    const totalSecs = done.reduce((sum,r) => sum + Number((r.fields||{}).duration_seconds||0), 0);
    const totalMins = Math.round(totalSecs / 60);
    const closedToday = done.filter(function(r){
      const f = (r && r.fields) || {};
      const closedTs = f.completed_at || f.closed_at || f.done_at || resolveClosedAt(f) || getRecordTimestamp(r);
      return !!closedTs && isToday(closedTs);
    }).length;

    const set = (id,v) => { const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.textContent=v; };
    set('archiv-stat-count', done.length);
    set('archiv-stat-time', totalMins);
    set('archiv-stat-followups', closedToday);
    if (statsSub) statsSub.textContent = done.length + ' Eintr\u00e4ge \u00b7 ' + totalMins + ' Min. Gespr\u00e4chsdauer';
    if (exportBtn) exportBtn.style.display = 'flex';
  } else {
    if (statsSub) statsSub.textContent = 'Noch keine archivierten Eintr\u00e4ge';
    if (exportBtn) exportBtn.style.display = 'none';
  }
  filterArchiv(_archivFilter, null);
}


async function vxArchivRestore(recordId) {
  if (!recordId) return;

  var btn = document.querySelector('[data-restore-id="'+recordId+'"]');
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-bold ph-spinner" style="font-size:11px;"></i>'; }

  try {
    // Muss über Netlify Function gehen — direkter Supabase-Call scheitert an RLS + DB-Constraint
    var result = await callDashboardFunction('call-update-status', {
      call_id: recordId,
      status: 'closed'
    });

    if (result && result.error) throw new Error(result.error);

    // Lokalen State aktualisieren
    var records = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : (window.allRecords || []);
    var rec = records.find(function(r) { return String((r && r.id) || '') === String(recordId); });
    if (rec && rec.fields) {
      rec.fields.dashboard_status = 'closed';
      rec.fields.completed_at = null;
    }

    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('✓ Eintrag wiederhergestellt');
    else if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('Eintrag wiederhergestellt', 'success');

    renderArchiv(records);
    if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
    if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') renderDashboard(records, []);

  } catch(e) {
    console.error('[vxArchivRestore]', e);
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') showToast('⚠ Fehler: ' + (e.message || 'Unbekannter Fehler'));
    var btn2 = document.querySelector('[data-restore-id="'+recordId+'"]');
    if (btn2) {
      btn2.disabled = false;
      btn2.innerHTML = '<i class="ph-bold ph-arrow-u-up-left" aria-hidden="true"></i>';
    }
  }
}
window.vxArchivRestore = vxArchivRestore;

function exportArchiv() {
  var done = _archivAllDone.length ? _archivAllDone :
    (allRecords||[]).filter(function(r){ return normalizeStatus((r.fields||{}).dashboard_status) === CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED; });
  if (!done.length) { toast('Keine Einträge zum Exportieren'); return; }
  var rows = [['Name','Telefon','Kategorie','Datum','Uhrzeit','Zusammenfassung','Nachfassen','Notiz']];
  done.forEach(function(r) {
    var f = r.fields || {};
    var ts = getRecordTimestamp(r) || '';
    var d = ts ? parseCallTimestamp(ts) : null;
    var date = d ? d.toLocaleDateString('de-CH', {timeZone:'Europe/Zurich'}) : '';
    var time = d ? d.toLocaleTimeString('de-CH',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',timeZone:'Europe/Zurich'}) : '';
    var followUp = resolveFollowUpAt(f) ? fmtFollowUpDateTime(resolveFollowUpAt(f)) : '';
    rows.push([
      f.caller_name||'',
      f.caller_phone||f.phone_number||'',
      f.category||'',
      date, time,
      (f.call_summary||'').replace(/\n/g,' '),
      followUp,
      (f.notes_customer_voxera||'').replace(/\n/g,' ')
    ]);
  });
  var csv = rows.map(function(row){
    return row.map(function(cell){
      var s = String(cell||'').replace(/"/g,'""');
      return (s.includes(',')||s.includes('"')||s.includes('\n')) ? '"'+s+'"' : s;
    }).join(',');
  }).join('\r\n');
  var blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
  var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  var a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = 'voxera-archiv-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.csv';
  document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  toast('CSV exportiert ('+done.length+' Einträge)');
}

function activateTab(name) {
  const preferred = document.getElementById('nav-' + name) || document.getElementById('mnav-' + name);
  if (preferred) {
    showTab(name, preferred);
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
//  ACTIONS

var confirmModalAction = null;
var confirmModalBusy = false;
var overlayFocusReturnMap = {};

function rememberOverlayFocusReturn(overlayId) {
  if (!overlayId) return;
  var active = document.activeElement;
  if (active && typeof active.focus === 'function') overlayFocusReturnMap[overlayId] = active;
}

function restoreOverlayFocus(overlayId) {
  var el = overlayFocusReturnMap[overlayId];
  delete overlayFocusReturnMap[overlayId];
  if (el && el.isConnected && !el.disabled && el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true') {
    try { el.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch(e) { try { el.focus(); } catch(_) {} }
  }
}

function setOverlayAriaHidden(overlayId, hidden) {
  var overlay = document.getElementById(overlayId);
  if (overlay) overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', hidden ? 'true' : 'false');
}


window.vxScrollLocks = window.vxScrollLocks || new Set();

function vxApplyScrollLock() {
  var locked = !!(window.vxScrollLocks && window.vxScrollLocks.size);
  var value = locked ? 'hidden' : '';
  if (document.body) document.body.style.overflow = value;
  if (document.documentElement) document.documentElement.style.overflow = value;
  if (window.VOXERA_DEBUG_SCROLL) {
    vxDebugLog('[scroll-lock] apply', locked, Array.from(window.vxScrollLocks || []));
  }
}

function vxSetScrollLock(reason, locked) {
  if (!window.vxScrollLocks) window.vxScrollLocks = new Set();
  if (!reason) reason = 'unknown';
  if (locked) window.vxScrollLocks.add(String(reason));
  else window.vxScrollLocks.delete(String(reason));
  vxApplyScrollLock();
}

function vxClearAllScrollLocks() {
  if (!window.vxScrollLocks) window.vxScrollLocks = new Set();
  window.vxScrollLocks.clear();
  vxApplyScrollLock();
}

function vxSyncScrollLock() {
  var hasOverlay = !!document.querySelector('.overlay.open, .detail-overlay.open, .account-overlay.open');
  var callDetail = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
  var callDetailOpen = !!(callDetail && callDetail.style.display === 'block');
  var detailV2 = document.getElementById('vx-detail-v2');
  var detailV2Open = !!(detailV2 && detailV2.style.display !== 'none');
  vxSetScrollLock('overlay', hasOverlay);
  vxSetScrollLock('call-detail-page', callDetailOpen);
  vxSetScrollLock('detail-v2', detailV2Open);
}
function syncOverlayScrollLock() {
  vxSyncScrollLock();
}

// [VX 2026-05-17] Interaction Reliability helpers.
// Small, local UI safeguards only: no schema changes, no business-logic changes.
function vxSetActionButtonLoading(button, isLoading, loadingLabel) {
  if (!button) return;
  if (isLoading) {
    if (!button.dataset.vxOriginalText) button.dataset.vxOriginalText = button.textContent || '';
    button.dataset.vxSaving = '1';
    button.disabled = true;
    button.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    button.textContent = loadingLabel || 'Speichern...';
  } else {
    button.dataset.vxSaving = '0';
    button.disabled = false;
    button.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'false');
    if (button.dataset.vxOriginalText) button.textContent = button.dataset.vxOriginalText;
    delete button.dataset.vxOriginalText;
  }
}

function vxIsActionButtonSaving(button) {
  return !!(button && button.dataset && button.dataset.vxSaving === '1');
}

function vxRefreshOperationalViews(records, manualRecords) {
  var callRecords = records || allRecords || [];
  var taskRecords = manualRecords || allManualTaskRecords || [];
  try { if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') renderDashboard(callRecords, taskRecords); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Voxera SaveFlow] renderDashboard skipped', e); }
  try { if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe(); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Voxera SaveFlow] renderAnrufe skipped', e); }
  try { if (typeof renderRueckrufe === 'function') renderRueckrufe(callRecords, taskRecords); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Voxera SaveFlow] renderRueckrufe skipped', e); }
  try { if (typeof renderDashPriorityList === 'function') renderDashPriorityList(callRecords, taskRecords); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Voxera SaveFlow] renderDashPriorityList skipped', e); }
}

function vxUpdateLocalCallRecord(recordId, fields) {
  var rec = (allRecords || []).find(function(r) { return r && String(r.id) === String(recordId); });
  if (rec && rec.fields && fields && typeof fields === 'object') Object.assign(rec.fields, fields);
  return rec || null;
}

function vxScheduleSingleDataRefresh(delayMs) {
  if (window.__vxSaveFlowReloadTimer) clearTimeout(window.__vxSaveFlowReloadTimer);
  window.__vxSaveFlowReloadTimer = setTimeout(function(){
    window.__vxSaveFlowReloadTimer = null;
    try { if (typeof loadData === 'function') loadData(); } catch(e) { console.warn('[Voxera SaveFlow] loadData skipped', e); }
  }, typeof delayMs === 'number' ? delayMs : 500);
}

// [v5.8.2] Modal lifecycle helpers.
// Centralizes safe closing after successful actions without changing business logic.
function vxCloseOverlayById(id) {
  var overlay = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!overlay) return false;
  overlay.classList.remove('open');
  overlay.style.display = overlay.classList.contains('vx-custom-overlay') ? 'none' : overlay.style.display;
  setOverlayAriaHidden(id, true);
  return true;
}

function vxCloseAllActionModals() {
  ['confirm-overlay','followup-overlay','manual-task-overlay','detail-overlay'].forEach(function(id) {
    vxCloseOverlayById(id);
  });
  try { if (typeof closeManualTaskModal === 'function') closeManualTaskModal(); } catch(e) {}
  try { if (typeof closeFollowup === 'function') closeFollowup(); } catch(e) {}
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion() {
  try { if (typeof hideCallDetail === 'function') hideCallDetail(); } catch(e) {}
  try { vxCloseAllActionModals(); } catch(e) {}
  try { vxClearAllScrollLocks(); } catch(e) { syncOverlayScrollLock(); }
}
window.vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion = vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion;

window._vxPendingHighlightRecordId = window._vxPendingHighlightRecordId || null;

function vxApplyPendingRowHighlight() {
  var targetId = String(window._vxPendingHighlightRecordId || '').trim();
  if (!targetId) return;
  var row = document.querySelector('.vx-heute-row[data-record-id="' + CSS.escape(targetId) + '"]')
    || document.querySelector('.vx-heute-row[data-id="' + CSS.escape(targetId) + '"]')
    || document.querySelector('.dpr-card[data-id="' + CSS.escape(targetId) + '"] .vx-heute-row');
  if (!row) return;
  row.classList.remove('vx-row-highlight');
  void row.offsetWidth;
  row.classList.add('vx-row-highlight');
  window._vxPendingHighlightRecordId = null;
  setTimeout(function() { row.classList.remove('vx-row-highlight'); }, 1600);
}

function vxRefreshActiveDetailAfterAction(options) {
  var opts = options || {};
  var activeType = String(window._vxCurrentDetailType || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  var currentActiveId = String(window._vxActiveRecordId || '').trim();
  var targetId = String(opts.recordId || '').trim();
  var createdId = String(opts.createdRecordId || '').trim();
  var primaryId = createdId || targetId;
  var detailId = '';

  if (activeType === 'task' && (primaryId || currentActiveId)) {
    detailId = primaryId || currentActiveId;
  } else if (activeType === 'call' && (primaryId || currentActiveId)) {
    detailId = primaryId || currentActiveId;
  } else if (!activeType && (primaryId || currentActiveId)) {
    detailId = primaryId || currentActiveId;
  }

  if (!detailId) return;

  var isTask = (typeof vxIsManualTaskRecordId === 'function')
    ? vxIsManualTaskRecordId(detailId)
    : String(detailId || '').indexOf('case:') === 0;

  if (isTask) {
    if (typeof showTaskDetail === 'function') showTaskDetail(detailId);
  } else {
    if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(detailId);
  }
}

function vxAfterActionSuccess(options) {
  var opts = options || {};
  var targetId = String(opts.createdRecordId || opts.recordId || '').trim();
  if (targetId) {
    window._vxActiveRecordId = targetId;
    window._vxPendingHighlightRecordId = targetId;
  }
  if (opts.closeModal !== false) {
    if (opts.modal === 'manual-task' && typeof closeManualTaskModal === 'function') closeManualTaskModal();
    else if (opts.modal === 'followup' && typeof closeFollowup === 'function') closeFollowup();
  }
  if (typeof vxRefreshOperationalViews === 'function') vxRefreshOperationalViews(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
  else {
    if (typeof renderDashboard === 'function') renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
    if (typeof renderRueckrufe === 'function') renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
  }
