  try { vxRefreshActiveDetailAfterAction({ recordId: opts.recordId || '', createdRecordId: opts.createdRecordId || '' }); } catch(e) {}
  if (targetId && typeof vxSetActiveRequestRow === 'function') {
    setTimeout(function(){ try { vxSetActiveRequestRow(targetId); } catch(e) {} }, 0);
  }
}


(function initOverlayScrollLock(){
  document.addEventListener('click', function(){ setTimeout(vxSyncScrollLock, 0); });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if (e.key === 'Escape') setTimeout(vxSyncScrollLock, 0); });
})();

function openConfirmModal(config) {
  rememberOverlayFocusReturn('confirm-overlay');
  confirmModalBusy = false;
  const titleEl = document.getElementById('confirm-title');
  const subEl = document.getElementById('confirm-sub');
  const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
  const modalEl = document.getElementById('confirm-modal');
  const checkboxWrap = document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox-wrap');
  const checkboxEl = document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox');
  const checkboxLabel = document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox-label');
  if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = (config && config.title) || 'Bitte bestätigen';
  if (subEl) subEl.textContent = (config && config.text) || 'Sind Sie sicher?';
  if (modalEl) {
    modalEl.classList.remove('confirm-tone-danger', 'confirm-tone-neutral', 'confirm-tone-primary');
    modalEl.classList.add('confirm-tone-' + ((config && config.tone) || 'primary'));
  }
  if (checkboxWrap && checkboxEl && checkboxLabel) {
    var hasCheckbox = !!(config && config.checkboxLabel);
    checkboxWrap.style.display = hasCheckbox ? 'flex' : 'none';
    checkboxEl.checked = !!(config && config.checkboxDefault);
    checkboxLabel.textContent = hasCheckbox ? String(config.checkboxLabel || '') : '';
  }
  if (okBtn) {
    okBtn.textContent = (config && config.confirmLabel) || 'Bestätigen';
    okBtn.classList.remove('danger', 'neutral', 'btn--primary', 'btn--danger', 'btn--neutral');
    if (config && config.tone === 'danger') okBtn.classList.add('btn--danger');
    else if (config && config.tone === 'neutral') okBtn.classList.add('btn--neutral');
    else okBtn.classList.add('btn--primary');
    okBtn.dataset.loadingLabel = (config && config.loadingLabel) || 'Bitte warten...';
    okBtn.disabled = false;
    okBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'false');
  }
  confirmModalAction = (config && typeof config.onConfirm === 'function') ? function() {
    var checked = !!(document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox') && document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox').checked);
    return config.onConfirm({ checkboxChecked: checked, archiveAfterClose: checked });
  } : null;
  const overlay = document.getElementById('confirm-overlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.classList.add('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('confirm-overlay', false);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function closeConfirmModal() {
  if (confirmModalBusy) return;
  const overlay = document.getElementById('confirm-overlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('confirm-overlay', true);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
  restoreOverlayFocus('confirm-overlay');
  confirmModalBusy = false;
  confirmModalAction = null;
  var checkboxWrap = document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox-wrap');
  var checkboxEl = document.getElementById('confirm-checkbox');
  if (checkboxWrap) checkboxWrap.style.display = 'none';
  if (checkboxEl) checkboxEl.checked = false;
  // [PATCH v2.40] is-loading auf Header-Buttons zurücksetzen beim Abbrechen
  var doneBtn = document.getElementById('vx-cd-qa-done');
  if (doneBtn && doneBtn.classList.contains('is-loading')) {
    doneBtn.classList.remove('is-loading');
  }
}

async function runConfirmModalAction() {
  if (confirmModalBusy) return;
  const fn = confirmModalAction;
  if (typeof fn !== 'function') {
    closeConfirmModal();
    return;
  }
  const okBtn = document.getElementById('confirm-ok-btn');
  const originalLabel = okBtn ? okBtn.textContent : '';
  if (okBtn) {
    okBtn.disabled = true;
    okBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    okBtn.textContent = okBtn.dataset.loadingLabel || 'Bitte warten...';
  }
  confirmModalBusy = true;
  try {
    logManualTaskDoneFlow('confirm.submit', { hasAction: true });
    const result = await fn();
    if (result !== false) {
      confirmModalBusy = false;
      closeConfirmModal();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    toast(' ' + toManualTaskUserMessage(e, 'Aktion konnte nicht gespeichert werden.'));
  } finally {
    confirmModalBusy = false;
    if (okBtn) {
      okBtn.disabled = false;
    okBtn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'false');
      okBtn.textContent = originalLabel || 'Bestätigen';
    }
  }
}

function confirmClose(recordId, newStatus, rowElement) {
  if (normalizeStatus(newStatus) === CALL_STATUS.CLOSED) {
    confirmTaskDone(recordId, rowElement, 'status-dropdown');
    return;
  }
  openConfirmModal({
    title: 'Status aktualisieren?',
    text: 'Der Status dieses Eintrags wird aktualisiert.',
    confirmLabel: 'Speichern',
    loadingLabel: 'Speichere...',
    tone: 'primary',
    onConfirm: async function() {
      return await doStatusChange(recordId, newStatus, rowElement || null);
    }
  });
}

// kept for backward compat (cb-row dashboard buttons still reference this name)
function confirmCloseWithDropdownPreservation(recordId, newStatus, rowElement) {
  confirmClose(recordId, newStatus, rowElement);
}

async function doStatusChange(recordId, newStatus, rowElement) {
  if (hasKnownInactiveContract()) {
    toast(getRestrictedContractMessage('Aufgaben'), 'warning');
    logManualTaskDoneFlow('guard.blocked.contract-inactive', {
      recordId: String(recordId || ''),
      contractState: customerContractState
    });
    return false;
  }
  const normalizedStatus = normalizeStatus(newStatus);
  const isManualTask = (typeof vxIsManualTaskRecordId === 'function') ? vxIsManualTaskRecordId(recordId) : String(recordId || '').startsWith('case:');
  if (isManualTask) {
    logManualTaskDoneFlow('status-change.start', {
      recordId: String(recordId || ''),
      requestedStatus: String(newStatus || ''),
      normalizedStatus,
      isManualTask: true
    });
  }
  const row = rowElement || null;
  const sourceRecords = isManualTask ? (allManualTaskRecords || []) : (allRecords || []);
  const cached = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function')
    ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId)
    : sourceRecords.find(r => String(r.id) === String(recordId));
  const currentStatus = normalizeStatus(cached && cached.fields ? (cached.fields.dashboard_status || cached.fields.status) : CALL_STATUS.NEW);

  if (currentStatus === normalizedStatus) {
    if (cached && cached.fields) cached.fields.dashboard_status = normalizedStatus;
    renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderAnrufe();
    renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderArchiv(allRecords);
    toast('Status bereits gesetzt');
    return true;
  }

  if (row) {
    row.style.transition = 'all .3s ease';
    if (isClosedStatus(normalizedStatus)) {
      row.style.opacity = '.5';
      row.style.background = '#F0FDF4';
      row.style.borderColor = '#BBF7D0';
    } else if ([CALL_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS, CALL_STATUS.FOLLOW_UP_SCHEDULED].includes(normalizedStatus)) {
      row.style.background = '#FFFBEB';
      row.style.borderColor = '#FDE68A';
    } else {
      row.style.opacity = '1';
      row.style.background = '';
      row.style.borderColor = '';
    }
    const secRow = row.querySelector('.call-secondary');
    if (secRow) {
      secRow.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(c => c.remove());
      const original = sourceRecords.find(r => String(r.id) === String(recordId));
      if (original && original.fields) {
        original.fields.dashboard_status = normalizedStatus;
        secRow.innerHTML = callCardBadgeHtml(original);
      }
    }
  }

  const icons = {
    [CALL_STATUS.NEW]:'Neu',
    [CALL_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS]:'In Bearbeitung',
    [CALL_STATUS.FOLLOW_UP_SCHEDULED]:'In Bearbeitung',
    [CALL_STATUS.CLOSED]:'Erledigt',
    [CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED]:'Archiviert'
  };
  try {
    realtimeSuppressUntil = Date.now() + 2000; // Realtime unterdrücken VOR API-Call
    if (isManualTask) {
      const caseStatus = mapDashboardStatusToManualTaskDbStatus(normalizedStatus);
      logManualTaskDoneFlow('status-change.manual-mapping', {
        recordId: String(recordId || ''),
        normalizedStatus,
        mappedCaseStatus: caseStatus
      });
      const updated = await updateManualTaskStatus(recordId, caseStatus);
      const updatedRawId = String((updated && updated.fields && updated.fields.task_entity_id) || '').trim();
      const idx = (allManualTaskRecords || []).findIndex(function(r) {
        var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
        return String(r.id) === String(recordId) || String(f.task_entity_id || '') === String(recordId).replace(/^case:/,'') || (updatedRawId && String(f.task_entity_id || '') === updatedRawId);
      });
      if (idx >= 0) allManualTaskRecords[idx] = updated;
      var taskLifecyclePatch = { updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
      if (caseStatus === 'closed') {
        taskLifecyclePatch.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
        taskLifecyclePatch.archived_at = null;
      } else if (caseStatus === 'archived') {
        taskLifecyclePatch.archived_at = new Date().toISOString();
      } else if (caseStatus === 'new') {
        taskLifecyclePatch.completed_at = null;
        taskLifecyclePatch.archived_at = null;
      }
      await vxBestEffortPatchTaskLifecycle(recordId, taskLifecyclePatch);
    } else {
      await applyStatusTransition(recordId, currentStatus, normalizedStatus);
      if (cached && cached.fields) {
        cached.fields.dashboard_status = normalizedStatus;
        if (isClosedStatus(normalizedStatus)) cached.fields.follow_up_at = null;
      }
    }
    renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderAnrufe();
    renderRueckrufe(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
    renderArchiv(allRecords);
    toast(icons[normalizedStatus] || 'Status aktualisiert', isClosedStatus(normalizedStatus) ? 'success' : 'info');
    if (isClosedStatus(normalizedStatus) || normalizedStatus === CALL_STATUS.ARCHIVED) {
      if (typeof vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion === 'function') setTimeout(vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion, 120);
    }
    return true;
  } catch(e) {
    // [PATCH v2.21 — illegal call transition explizit abfangen]
    // Das Backend lehnt bestimmte Status-Übergänge mit 422 / "illegal call transition" ab.
    // Statt generischem Fehlertext: klare Erklärung + Reload-Option anbieten.
    var errMsg = String(e && e.message ? e.message : '').toLowerCase();
    var isIllegalTransition = errMsg.includes('illegal') || errMsg.includes('transition') ||
                              errMsg.includes('422') || errMsg.includes('cannot archive') ||
                              errMsg.includes('invalid status');
    if (isIllegalTransition) {
      toast('Status kann nicht geändert werden — bitte Seite neu laden.', 'warning');
      // Lokalen Status zurücksetzen damit UI nicht inkonsistent bleibt
      if (cached && cached.fields) {
        cached.fields.dashboard_status = currentStatus;
      }
    } else {
      toast('⚠ ' + toManualTaskUserMessage(e, 'Fehler beim Status-Update'));
    }
    if (row) { row.style.opacity='1'; row.style.background=''; row.style.borderColor=''; }
    return false;
  }
}


function directCall(recordId, encodedTarget) {
  const raw = decodeURIComponent(encodedTarget || '').trim();
  if (!raw) {
    toast('Keine Telefonnummer vorhanden');
    return;
  }
  pendingFollowupRecordId = recordId;
  pendingFollowupStartedAt = Date.now();
  pendingFollowupStatus = '';
  const href = /^tel:/i.test(raw) ? raw : ('tel:' + raw.replace(/\s/g,''));
  window.location.href = href;
}

function maybeOpenFollowup() {
  if (!pendingFollowupRecordId) return;
  if (Date.now() - pendingFollowupStartedAt > 10 * 60 * 1000) {
    pendingFollowupRecordId = null;
    return;
  }
  const rec = allRecords.find(r => r.id === pendingFollowupRecordId);
  if (!rec) return;
  openFollowup(rec.id, (rec.fields.caller_name || '').trim(), (rec.fields.notes_customer_voxera || '').trim(), rec.fields.dashboard_status || '', rec.fields.next_action || '', rec.fields.follow_up_at || '');
  pendingFollowupRecordId = null;
}

function openFollowupDirect(recordId, options) {
  // [v2.65] Auch Tasks (case:-Prefix) auflösen
  var rec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function')
    ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId)
    : allRecords.find(function(r){ return r.id === recordId; });
  if (!rec) return;
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  openFollowup(recordId, (f.caller_name || f.title || '').trim(), (f.notes_customer_voxera || f.note || f.notes || '').trim(), f.dashboard_status || '', f.next_action || '', f.follow_up_at || '', options || null);
}

function openFollowup(recordId, callerName, existingNotiz, currentStatus, nextAction, followUpAt, options) {
  rememberOverlayFocusReturn('followup-overlay');
  notizRecordId = recordId;
  followupCallerName = String(callerName || '').trim();
  var opts = options || {};
  var mode = String(opts.mode || 'callback').toLowerCase();
  if (mode !== 'due') mode = 'callback';
  followupModalMode = mode;
  const done = isClosedStatus(currentStatus);
  var hasExistingDue = !!String(followUpAt || '').trim();
  var titleEl = document.getElementById('followup-title');
  if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = (mode === 'due') ? (hasExistingDue ? 'Fälligkeit ändern' : 'Fälligkeit setzen') : 'Nachfassen planen';
  var subtitleEl = document.getElementById('followup-sub');
  if (subtitleEl) {
    subtitleEl.textContent = (mode === 'due')
      ? 'Lege fest, wann dieser Eintrag wieder fällig ist.'
      : 'Plane einen Rückruf oder Folgekontakt.';
  }
  document.getElementById('followup-input').value = existingNotiz || '';
  document.getElementById('followup-next-action').value = (mode === 'due') ? '' : (nextAction || '');
  document.getElementById('followup-datetime').value = fmtDateTimeLocal(followUpAt || '');
  var nextActionField = document.getElementById('followup-next-action-field');
  if (nextActionField) nextActionField.style.display = (mode === 'due') ? 'none' : '';
  const markComplete = document.getElementById('followup-mark-complete');
  if (markComplete) {
    markComplete.checked = false;
    var markRow = document.getElementById('followup-mark-row') || markComplete.closest('.followup-mark-row');
    if (markRow) markRow.style.display = (mode === 'due' || done) ? 'none' : '';
  }
  const lockWarning = document.getElementById('followup-locked-warning');
  const editableSection = document.getElementById('followup-editable');
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById('followup-save-btn');
  if (done) {
    // Erledigte Einträge bleiben abgeschlossen; ein neuer Rückruf wird als separate Aufgabe geplant.
    if (lockWarning) {
      lockWarning.style.display = 'block';
      lockWarning.childNodes[0].nodeValue = mode === 'due'
        ? 'Dieser Eintrag bleibt erledigt. Die Fälligkeit wird als neue geplante Aufgabe erstellt. '
        : 'Dieser Eintrag bleibt erledigt. Der Rückruf wird als neue geplante Aufgabe erstellt. ';
    }
  } else if (lockWarning) {
    lockWarning.style.display = 'none';
  }
  if (editableSection) {
    editableSection.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea').forEach(function(el){ el.disabled = false; });
  }
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    if (mode === 'due') saveBtn.textContent = 'Fälligkeit speichern';
    else saveBtn.textContent = done ? 'Rückruf erstellen' : 'Nachfassen speichern';
  }
  document.getElementById('followup-overlay').classList.add('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('followup-overlay', false);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function _completedWarningDismissed(recordId) {
  try { return !!localStorage.getItem('voxera_warn_dismissed_' + recordId); } catch(e) { return false; }
}


function rerenderFollowupMetaForCallRow(recordId) {
  if (!recordId) return;
  var rec = (allRecords || []).find(function(r) { return r && r.id === recordId; });
  if (!rec || !rec.fields) return;
  var followUpMetaText = buildFollowUpMetaText(rec, new Date());
  var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.call-row[data-id]')).filter(function(row) {
    return row && row.dataset && row.dataset.id === recordId;
  });
  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    var body = row.querySelector('.cr-body');
    if (!body) return;
    var summaryEl = body.querySelector('.call-summary');
    var existingLines = body.querySelectorAll('.followup-meta-line');
    existingLines.forEach(function(el) {
      if (!el.textContent || el.textContent.indexOf('Erledigt am') !== 0) el.remove();
    });
    if (!followUpMetaText) return;
    var metaEl = document.createElement('div');
    metaEl.className = 'followup-meta-line';
    metaEl.textContent = followUpMetaText;
    if (summaryEl) {
      var summaryParent = summaryEl.parentNode;
      if (summaryParent === body && summaryEl.nextSibling && summaryEl.nextSibling.parentNode === body) body.insertBefore(metaEl, summaryEl.nextSibling);
      else if (summaryParent && summaryEl.nextSibling && summaryEl.nextSibling.parentNode === summaryParent) summaryParent.insertBefore(metaEl, summaryEl.nextSibling);
      else if (summaryParent) summaryParent.appendChild(metaEl);
      else if (body && body.firstChild && body.firstChild.parentNode === body) body.insertBefore(metaEl, body.firstChild);
      else if (body) body.appendChild(metaEl);
    } else if (body && body.firstChild && body.firstChild.parentNode === body) body.insertBefore(metaEl, body.firstChild);
    else if (body) body.appendChild(metaEl);
  });
}

function dismissCompletedWarning() {
  const w = document.getElementById('followup-locked-warning');
  if (w) w.style.display = 'none';
  if (notizRecordId) {
    try { vxSafeLocalStorageSet('voxera_warn_dismissed_' + notizRecordId, '1'); } catch(e) {}
  }
}

function closeFollowup() {
  vxSetActionButtonLoading(document.getElementById('followup-save-btn'), false);
  document.getElementById('followup-overlay').classList.remove('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('followup-overlay', true);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
  document.getElementById('followup-input').value = '';
  document.getElementById('followup-next-action').value = '';
  document.getElementById('followup-datetime').value = '';
  followupCallerName = '';
  followupModalMode = 'callback';
  const mc = document.getElementById('followup-mark-complete');
  if (mc) {
    mc.checked = false;
    var mcRow = document.getElementById('followup-mark-row') || mc.closest('.followup-mark-row');
    if (mcRow) mcRow.style.display = '';
  }
  var nextActionField = document.getElementById('followup-next-action-field');
  if (nextActionField) nextActionField.style.display = '';
  const editableSection = document.getElementById('followup-editable');
  if (editableSection) {
    editableSection.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea').forEach(function(el){ el.disabled = false; });
  }
  restoreOverlayFocus('followup-overlay');
}

async function saveFollowup() {
  if (!notizRecordId) return;
  const btn = document.getElementById('followup-save-btn');
  if (vxIsActionButtonSaving(btn)) return;
  const text = document.getElementById('followup-input').value.trim();
  const nextAction = document.getElementById('followup-next-action').value;
  const _followUpAtRaw = document.getElementById('followup-datetime').value;
  // [BUG FIX v2.52b] T00:00 bedeutet: Nutzer hat Datum gewählt aber keine Uhrzeit gesetzt.
  // Default-Zeit auf 09:00 setzen damit hasMeaningfulDueTime() die Uhrzeit erkennt
  // und in der Übersicht "Morgen, 09:00" statt nur "Morgen" anzeigt.
  const followUpAt = _followUpAtRaw && /T00:00$/.test(_followUpAtRaw)
    ? _followUpAtRaw.replace(/T00:00$/, 'T09:00')
    : _followUpAtRaw;
  const markComplete = document.getElementById('followup-mark-complete');
  vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, true, 'Speichern...');

  const fields = {
    notes_customer_voxera: text,
    next_action: nextAction || null,
    follow_up_at: serializeFollowUpLocal(followUpAt) || null,
    mark_complete: !!(markComplete && markComplete.checked)
  };
  if (fields.mark_complete) fields.follow_up_at = null;

  try {
    var recordId = notizRecordId;
    var sourceRec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId) : null;
    if (!sourceRec && Array.isArray(allRecords)) {
      sourceRec = allRecords.find(function(r){ return r && String(r.id) === String(recordId); });
    }
    var sourceFields = (sourceRec && sourceRec.fields) || {};
    var sourceLifecycle = (sourceRec && typeof vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus === 'function') ? vxGetEntryLifecycleStatus(sourceRec) : '';
    var sourceIsDone = sourceLifecycle === 'done' || sourceLifecycle === 'archived' || isClosedStatus(sourceFields.dashboard_status || sourceFields.status || currentStatus || '');
    if (sourceIsDone && followUpAt && !fields.mark_complete) {
      var dueAt = String(followUpAt || '').slice(0, 10);
      var dueTime = String(followUpAt || '').slice(11, 16) || '09:00';
      var caller = String(sourceFields.caller_name || sourceFields.company_name || followupCallerName || '').trim();
      var phone = String(sourceFields.caller_phone || sourceFields.phone_number || sourceFields.tel_link || '').trim();
      var createdTask = await createManualTask({
        title: caller ? ('Rückruf für ' + caller) : 'Rückruf planen',
        type: 'callback',
        due_at: dueAt,
        due_time: dueTime,
        status: 'new',
        note: text || nextAction || sourceFields.call_summary || sourceFields.notes_customer_voxera || null,
        phone: phone || null,
        source_type: 'call',
        source_call_id: String(recordId || '')
      });
      if (createdTask) {
        allManualTaskRecords = Array.isArray(allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : [];
        allManualTaskRecords.unshift(createdTask);
        window.allManualTaskRecords = allManualTaskRecords;
      }
      vxAfterActionSuccess({
        createdRecordId: createdTask && createdTask.id ? String(createdTask.id) : '',
        modal: 'followup'
      });
      notizRecordId = null;
      toast('Rückruf als neue Aufgabe geplant');
      vxScheduleSingleDataRefresh(500);
      return;
    }
    await patchCallRecord(recordId, fields);
    vxNotifMarkReadByRecord(recordId);

    var localFields = {
      notes_customer_voxera: fields.notes_customer_voxera,
      next_action: fields.next_action,
      follow_up_at: fields.follow_up_at
    };
    if (fields.mark_complete) localFields.follow_up_at = null;
    var localRec = vxUpdateLocalCallRecord(recordId, localFields);

    if (fields.mark_complete) {
      await applyStatusTransition(recordId, CALL_STATUS.NEW, CALL_STATUS.CLOSED);
      vxUpdateLocalCallRecord(recordId, {
        dashboard_status: CALL_STATUS.CLOSED,
        completed_at: (localRec && localRec.fields && localRec.fields.completed_at) || new Date().toISOString(),
        follow_up_at: null
      });
    }

    rerenderFollowupMetaForCallRow(recordId);
    vxAfterActionSuccess({ recordId: recordId, modal: 'followup' });
    notizRecordId = null;

    toast(fields.mark_complete ? 'Erledigt' : followUpAt ? 'Rückruf geplant' : 'Gespeichert');

    if (fields.mark_complete && typeof vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion === 'function') vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion();
    vxScheduleSingleDataRefresh(500);
  } catch(e) {
    const detail = e && e.message ? String(e.message) : '';
    toast(detail ? 'Speichern fehlgeschlagen: ' + detail : 'Speichern fehlgeschlagen');
    // Error contract: keep modal open and preserve inputs.
  } finally {
    vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, false);
  }
}

document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
  if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
    setTimeout(maybeOpenFollowup, 250);
    safeLoadData();
  }
});
window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
  setTimeout(maybeOpenFollowup, 250);
  safeLoadData();
});
window.addEventListener('online', () => safeLoadData());

// Session-safe data loader - checks session before loading, avoids false logouts
async function safeLoadData() {
  if (document.getElementById('app').style.display === 'none') return; // not logged in
  if (realtimeSuppressUntil > 0 && Date.now() < realtimeSuppressUntil) return; // Mutation läuft gerade
  const sb = getSupabaseAuthClient();
  if (!sb) return;
  try {
    var result = await sb.auth.getSession();
    if (!result.data.session) {
      // Session expired - try to recover silently
      vxDebugLog('Session check: no active session');
      return; // Don't force logout - Supabase will auto-refresh if possible
    }
    loadData();
  } catch(e) {
    vxDebugLog('Session check error:', e.message);
  }
}


function vxHasTemporaryCalls(records) {
  return (records || []).some(function(rec){ return (vxGetCallDisplayState(rec) || {}).isTemporary; });
}
function vxUpdateRefreshPolling(records) {
  var needsFast = vxHasTemporaryCalls(records || allRecords || []);
  var intervalMs = needsFast ? TEMP_CALL_POLL_INTERVAL_MS : 12000;
  if (!autoRefreshTimer || window._vxRefreshIntervalMs !== intervalMs) {
    if (autoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(autoRefreshTimer);
    autoRefreshTimer = setInterval(safeLoadData, intervalMs);
    window._vxRefreshIntervalMs = intervalMs;
  }
}


var _vxPendingManualTaskSource = null;
function openManualTaskFromCall(recordId) {
  var rec = (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') ? vxResolveDprRecord(recordId) : null;
  if (!rec || !rec.fields) {
    if (typeof openManualTaskModal === 'function') openManualTaskModal();
    return;
  }
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  _vxPendingManualTaskSource = { source_type: 'call', source_call_id: String(recordId || '') };
  openManualTaskModal();
  var titleEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-title');
  var typeEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-type');
  var noteEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-note');
  var phoneEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-phone');
  var caller = String(f.caller_name || f.company_name || '').trim();
  var callback = f.callback_requested === true || String(f.callback_requested).toLowerCase() === 'true' || vxTodayTextSuggestsCallback(f.next_action || '');
  if (typeEl) typeEl.value = callback ? 'callback' : 'general';
  if (titleEl) titleEl.value = callback
    ? ((caller || 'Kontakt') + ' zurückrufen')
    : ('Anfrage' + (caller ? ' von ' + caller : '') + ' prüfen');
  if (noteEl) noteEl.value = String(f.call_summary || f.next_action || f.notes_customer_voxera || '').trim();
  if (phoneEl) phoneEl.value = String(f.caller_phone || f.tel_link || '').trim();
}
window.openManualTaskFromCall = openManualTaskFromCall;

function openManualTaskModal() {
  if (customerContractState.hasContract && isContractRestrictedState()) {
    toast(getRestrictedContractMessage('Neue Aufgaben'), 'warning');
    return;
  }
  rememberOverlayFocusReturn('manual-task-overlay');
  vxTaskModalMode = 'create';
  editingManualTaskRecordId = null;
  if (!_vxPendingManualTaskSource) _vxPendingManualTaskSource = null;
  document.getElementById('manual-task-title').value = '';
  document.getElementById('manual-task-type').value = 'callback';
  document.getElementById('manual-task-note').value = '';
  var taskOwnerEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-owner');
  if (taskOwnerEl) taskOwnerEl.value = '';
  var taskPhoneEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-phone');
  if (taskPhoneEl) taskPhoneEl.value = '';
  // [PATCH v2.20 — Neues Formular: datetime-local statt getrennter date+time Inputs]
  var dtInput = document.getElementById('manual-task-datetime');
  if (dtInput) {
    // Default: Morgen 09:00
    var tomorrow9 = new Date();
    tomorrow9.setDate(tomorrow9.getDate() + 1);
    tomorrow9.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
    dtInput.value = (typeof vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput === 'function')
      ? vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(tomorrow9)
      : (tomorrow9.getFullYear() + '-' + String(tomorrow9.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0') + '-' + String(tomorrow9.getDate()).padStart(2,'0') + 'T09:00');
    dtInput.style.display = '';
  }
  // Quick-Chips zurücksetzen, Morgen 09:00 aktiv
  document.querySelectorAll('#manual-task-quick-chips .vx-cd-due-chip').forEach(function(c) {
    c.classList.toggle('is-active', c.dataset.dueMode === 'tomorrow9');
  });
  // Legacy-Felder falls noch im DOM
  var legacyDue = document.getElementById('manual-task-due');
  var legacyTime = document.getElementById('manual-task-due-time');
  var legacyStatus = document.getElementById('manual-task-status');
  var legacyPriority = document.getElementById('manual-task-priority');
  if (legacyDue) legacyDue.value = '';
  if (legacyTime) legacyTime.value = '';
  if (legacyStatus) legacyStatus.value = 'new';
  if (legacyPriority) legacyPriority.value = '';
  const modalHeading = document.getElementById('manual-task-title-heading');
  if (modalHeading) modalHeading.textContent = 'Aufgabe erstellen';
  var subtitle = document.getElementById('manual-task-subtitle');
  if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = 'Erstelle eine interne Aufgabe für diesen Eintrag.';
  const modeHint = document.getElementById('manual-task-mode-hint');
  if (modeHint) modeHint.style.display = 'none';
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById('manual-task-save-btn');
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    saveBtn.textContent = 'Aufgabe erstellen';
  }
  document.getElementById('manual-task-overlay').classList.add('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('manual-task-overlay', false);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function openManualTaskEditModal(recordId) {
  if (customerContractState.hasContract && isContractRestrictedState()) {
    toast(getRestrictedContractMessage('Aufgaben'), 'warning');
    return;
  }
  rememberOverlayFocusReturn('manual-task-overlay');
  vxTaskModalMode = 'edit';
  const rec = allManualTaskRecords.find(function(item) { return String(item.id) === String(recordId); });
  if (!rec || !rec.fields) {
    toast('Aufgabe nicht gefunden.');
    return;
  }
  const f = rec.fields;
  editingManualTaskRecordId = rec.id;
  document.getElementById('manual-task-title').value = String(f.title || f.caller_name || '').trim();
  document.getElementById('manual-task-type').value = mapManualTaskTypeUiToDb(f.type || 'general');
  document.getElementById('manual-task-note').value = String(f.note || f.notes || '').trim();
  var taskPhoneElEdit = document.getElementById('manual-task-phone');
  if (taskPhoneElEdit) taskPhoneElEdit.value = String(f.phone || f.phone_number || '').trim();
  var taskOwnerElEdit = document.getElementById('manual-task-owner');
  if (taskOwnerElEdit) taskOwnerElEdit.value = String(f.owner || '').trim();

  // [PATCH v2.21 — Edit-Modal: datetime-local aus due_at + due_time zusammensetzen]
  var dtInput = document.getElementById('manual-task-datetime');
  if (dtInput) {
    var dueAt = String(f.due_at || '').trim();
    var dueTime = String(f.due_time || '').trim();
    if (dueAt) {
      var combined = dueAt + (dueTime ? 'T' + dueTime.slice(0,5) : 'T09:00');
      var d = new Date(combined);
      dtInput.value = (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) && typeof vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput === 'function')
        ? vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(d)
        : combined;
      dtInput.style.display = '';
    } else {
      // Kein Datum gesetzt — Default Morgen 09:00
      var tomorrow9 = new Date();
      tomorrow9.setDate(tomorrow9.getDate() + 1);
      tomorrow9.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
      dtInput.value = (typeof vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput === 'function')
        ? vxFormatLocalDateTimeForInput(tomorrow9)
        : '';
      dtInput.style.display = '';
    }
    // Quick-Chips: kein Default aktiv beim Edit (Datum ist bereits gesetzt)
    document.querySelectorAll('#manual-task-quick-chips .vx-cd-due-chip').forEach(function(c) {
      c.classList.remove('is-active');
    });
  }

  // Legacy-Felder für Fallback im saveManualTask
  var legacyDue = document.getElementById('manual-task-due');
  var legacyTime = document.getElementById('manual-task-due-time');
  var legacyStatus = document.getElementById('manual-task-status');
  var legacyPriority = document.getElementById('manual-task-priority');
  if (legacyDue) legacyDue.value = String(f.due_at || '').trim();
  if (legacyTime) legacyTime.value = String(f.due_time || '').trim();
  if (legacyStatus) legacyStatus.value = mapManualTaskStatusUiToDb(f.status || f.dashboard_status || 'new');
  if (legacyPriority) legacyPriority.value = mapManualTaskPriorityDbToUi(f.priority || '');

  const modalHeading = document.getElementById('manual-task-title-heading');
  if (modalHeading) modalHeading.textContent = 'Aufgabe bearbeiten';
  var subtitle = document.getElementById('manual-task-subtitle');
  if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = 'Passe Titel, Fälligkeit, Zuständigkeit und Notiz an.';
  const modeHint = document.getElementById('manual-task-mode-hint');
  if (modeHint) modeHint.style.display = 'none';
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById('manual-task-save-btn');
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    saveBtn.textContent = 'Änderungen speichern';
  }
  document.getElementById('manual-task-overlay').classList.add('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('manual-task-overlay', false);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function closeManualTaskModal() {
  vxTaskModalMode = 'create';
  editingManualTaskRecordId = null;
  _vxPendingManualTaskSource = null;
  var modeHint = document.getElementById('manual-task-mode-hint');
  if (modeHint) modeHint.style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('manual-task-overlay').classList.remove('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('manual-task-overlay', true);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
  restoreOverlayFocus('manual-task-overlay');
}

function openManualRequestModal() {
  rememberOverlayFocusReturn('manual-request-overlay');
  document.getElementById('manual-request-name').value = '';
  document.getElementById('manual-request-phone').value = '';
  document.getElementById('manual-request-category').value = 'anruf';
  document.getElementById('manual-request-priority').value = 'hot';
  document.getElementById('manual-request-summary').value = '';
  document.getElementById('manual-request-inbox').checked = true;
  document.getElementById('manual-request-overlay').classList.add('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('manual-request-overlay', false);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
}

function closeManualRequestModal() {
  document.getElementById('manual-request-overlay').classList.remove('open');
  setOverlayAriaHidden('manual-request-overlay', true);
  syncOverlayScrollLock();
  restoreOverlayFocus('manual-request-overlay');
}

function saveManualRequest() {
  var btn = document.getElementById('manual-request-save-btn');
  if (vxIsActionButtonSaving(btn)) return;
  vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, true, 'Erstelle...');
  try {
  var name = String(document.getElementById('manual-request-name').value || '').trim();
  if (!name) { toast('Bitte Name / Firma ausfüllen.'); return; }
  var phone = String(document.getElementById('manual-request-phone').value || '').trim();
  var category = String(document.getElementById('manual-request-category').value || 'anruf');
  var priority = String(document.getElementById('manual-request-priority').value || 'hot');
  var summary = String(document.getElementById('manual-request-summary').value || '').trim();
  var forceInbox = !!document.getElementById('manual-request-inbox').checked;
  var nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  var rec = {
    id: 'manual_req_' + Date.now(),
    fields: {
      caller_name: name,
      company_name: name,
      phone_number: phone || null,
      category: category,
      lead_quality: priority,
      call_summary: summary || 'Manuell erfasst',
      dashboard_status: forceInbox ? 'new' : 'in_progress',
      created_at: nowIso,
      updated_at: nowIso
    }
  };
  allRecords = Array.isArray(allRecords) ? allRecords : [];
  allRecords.unshift(rec);
  closeManualRequestModal();
  renderDashboard(allRecords, allManualTaskRecords);
  renderAnrufe();
  vxUpdateAnfragenCount();
  toast('Anfrage erstellt');
  } finally {
    vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, false);
  }
}

async function saveManualTask() {
  if (customerContractState.hasContract && isContractRestrictedState()) {
    toast(getRestrictedContractMessage('Aufgaben'), 'warning');
    return;
  }
  const title = String(document.getElementById('manual-task-title').value || '').trim();
  const type = String(document.getElementById('manual-task-type').value || '').trim();
  const note = String(document.getElementById('manual-task-note').value || '').trim();
  var taskPhoneElSave = document.getElementById('manual-task-phone');
  const phone = String((taskPhoneElSave && taskPhoneElSave.value) || '').trim();
  var taskOwnerElSave = document.getElementById('manual-task-owner');
  const owner = String((taskOwnerElSave && taskOwnerElSave.value) || '').trim();

  // [PATCH v2.20 — Neues datetime-local Feld als primäre Quelle für Fälligkeit]
  // Fallback auf Legacy-Felder (due + due_time) für rückwärtskompatibles Edit-Modal
  var dtInput = document.getElementById('manual-task-datetime');
  var legacyDue = document.getElementById('manual-task-due');
  var legacyDueTime = document.getElementById('manual-task-due-time');
  var dueAt = '';
  var dueTime = null;
  if (dtInput && dtInput.value) {
    // Neues Format: datetime-local → ISO aufteilen
    var dtVal = new Date(dtInput.value);
    if (!Number.isNaN(dtVal.getTime())) {
      dueAt = dtInput.value.slice(0, 10); // YYYY-MM-DD
      dueTime = dtInput.value.slice(11, 16); // HH:MM
    }
  } else if (legacyDue) {
    dueAt = String(legacyDue.value || '').trim();
    const dueTimeRaw = String((legacyDueTime && legacyDueTime.value) || '').trim();
    dueTime = normalizeManualTaskDueTime(dueTimeRaw);
  }

  const legacyStatus = document.getElementById('manual-task-status');
  const legacyPriority = document.getElementById('manual-task-priority');
  const status = mapManualTaskStatusUiToDb((legacyStatus && legacyStatus.value) || 'new');
  const priority = String((legacyPriority && legacyPriority.value) || '').trim();

  if (!title || !type) {
    toast('Bitte Titel und Typ ausfüllen.');
    return;
  }
  if (!dueAt) {
    toast('Bitte einen Zeitpunkt wählen.');
    return;
  }
  if (dueTime === null && legacyDue) {
    toast(MANUAL_TASKS_DUE_TIME_INVALID_MESSAGE);
    return;
  }
  if (!dueTime) {
    toast(MANUAL_TASKS_DUE_TIME_REQUIRED_MESSAGE);
    return;
  }
  const btn = document.getElementById('manual-task-save-btn');
  if (vxIsActionButtonSaving(btn)) return;
  const isEditMode = String(vxTaskModalMode || '').toLowerCase() === 'edit';
  const activeEditId = (isEditMode && editingManualTaskRecordId) ? String(editingManualTaskRecordId) : null;
  let savedTaskId = activeEditId;
  vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, true, isEditMode ? 'Speichert...' : 'Erstellt...');
  try {
    const basePayload = {
      title: title,
      type: type,
      due_at: dueAt,
      due_time: dueTime,
      status: status,
      priority: mapManualTaskPriorityUiToDb(priority),
      note: note || null,
      owner: owner || null,
      phone: phone || null
    };
    if (!isEditMode && _vxPendingManualTaskSource) {
      basePayload.source_type = _vxPendingManualTaskSource.source_type || 'call';
      basePayload.source_call_id = _vxPendingManualTaskSource.source_call_id || null;
    }
    if (isEditMode) {
      if (!editingManualTaskRecordId) throw new Error('Ungültiger Bearbeitungsmodus ohne Aufgaben-ID.');
      const updatedTask = await updateManualTask(editingManualTaskRecordId, basePayload);
      const idx = allManualTaskRecords.findIndex(function(item) { return String(item.id) === String(editingManualTaskRecordId); });
      if (idx >= 0) allManualTaskRecords[idx] = updatedTask;
      toast('Änderungen gespeichert');
    } else {
      const createdTask = await createManualTask(basePayload);
      if (createdTask && createdTask.id) savedTaskId = String(createdTask.id);
      allManualTaskRecords.unshift(createdTask);
      toast('Task erstellt');
    }
    vxAfterActionSuccess({
      recordId: isEditMode ? activeEditId : '',
      createdRecordId: !isEditMode ? savedTaskId : '',
      modal: 'manual-task'
    });
    if (activeEditId && typeof vxRefreshOpenTaskDetail === 'function') {
      setTimeout(function(){ vxRefreshOpenTaskDetail(activeEditId); }, 0);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[manual-task-save]', {
      mode: vxTaskModalMode,
      editingTaskId: editingManualTaskRecordId,
      error: e
    });
    toast(toManualTaskUserMessage(e, isEditMode ? 'Task konnte nicht gespeichert werden.' : 'Task konnte nicht gespeichert werden.'));
  } finally {
    vxSetActionButtonLoading(btn, false);
  }
}


function vxEmailForward(recordId) {
  var rec = allRecords.find(function(r){ return r.id === recordId; });
  if (!rec) return;
  var f = rec.fields || {};
  var name = f.caller_name || 'Unbekannter Anrufer';
  var phone = f.phone_number || f.caller_phone || '';
  var category = f.category || '';
  var duration = f.duration_seconds ? Math.round(Number(f.duration_seconds)/60) + ' Min.' : '';
  var summary = f.call_summary || '';
  var notiz = f.notes_customer_voxera || '';
  var quality = f.lead_quality || '';
  var date = fmtExactDateTime(getRecordTimestamp(rec)) || '';
  var status = f.dashboard_status || '';
  var nextAction = f.next_action || '';
  var callbackReq = f.callback_requested ? 'Ja' : 'Nein';

  var subject = encodeURIComponent('Voxera Anruf: ' + name + (category ? ' — ' + category : ''));
  var lines = [
    'ANRUF-BERICHT - VOXERA',
    '======================',
    '',
    'ANRUFER',
    'Name:           ' + name
  ];
  if (phone) lines.push('Telefon:        ' + phone);
  if (date)  lines.push('Zeitpunkt:      ' + date);
  if (duration) lines.push('Gespraechsdauer: ' + duration);
  lines.push('', 'DETAILS');
  if (category)   lines.push('Kategorie:      ' + category);
  if (quality)    lines.push('Lead-Qualitaet: ' + quality);
  lines.push('Rueckruf:       ' + callbackReq);
  if (status)     lines.push('Status:         ' + status);
  if (nextAction) lines.push('Naechste Aktion: ' + nextAction);
  if (summary) { lines.push('', 'ZUSAMMENFASSUNG'); lines.push(summary); }
  if (notiz)   { lines.push('', 'INTERNE NOTIZ');   lines.push(notiz); }
  lines.push('', '--', 'Erstellt von Voxera - dashboard.voxera.ch');
  var body = encodeURIComponent(lines.join('\n'));
  window.location.href = 'mailto:?subject=' + subject + '&body=' + body;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// VX FILTER BAR — Next-Level Filter/Search/Sort System
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var _vxFbState = {
  anrufe:   { filter: 'alle', sort: 'datum-neu', search: '' }
};

var _vxFbListMap = {
  anrufe:   'anrufe-list'
};

var _vxFbSortLabels = {
  'datum-neu': 'Neueste zuerst',
  'datum-alt': 'Älteste zuerst',
  'prioritaet': 'Priorität',
  'name': 'Name A–Z',
  'dauer': 'Dauer',
  'all': 'Alle',
  'today': 'Heute',
  'week': 'Diese Woche',
  'month': 'Dieser Monat'
};

function vxFbSearch(key, value) {
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  if (!state) return;
  state.search = value;
  if (key === 'anrufe') window._anrufeSearchQuery = String(value || '');
  var listId = _vxFbListMap[key];
  var clearBtn = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-clear');
  var hintEl = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-searchbox');
  var hint = hintEl ? hintEl.querySelector('.vxfb-search-hint') : null;
  if (clearBtn) clearBtn.style.display = value ? 'flex' : 'none';
  if (hint) hint.style.display = value ? 'none' : '';
  if (key === 'anrufe') {
    if (typeof renderAnrufe === 'function') renderAnrufe();
  } else {
    searchList(listId, value);
  }
  vxFbUpdateCount(key);
  vxFbUpdateActiveRow(key);
}

function vxFbClearSearch(key) {
  var input = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-input');
  if (input) { input.value = ''; input.focus(); }
  vxFbSearch(key, '');
}

function vxFbFilter(key, filter, btn) {
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  if (!state) return;
  var listId = _vxFbListMap[key];

  // Update chip active states
  var fb = document.getElementById('fb-' + key);
  if (fb) {
    fb.querySelectorAll('.vxfb-chip').forEach(function(c) {
      var isActive = c.getAttribute('data-filter') === filter;
      c.classList.toggle('vxfb-chip--active', isActive); c.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', isActive);
      // Keep danger class for dringend chip when active
      if (c.getAttribute('data-filter') === 'dringend') {
        c.classList.toggle('vxfb-chip--active', isActive); c.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', isActive);
        if (isActive) c.classList.add('vxfb-chip--danger');
        else c.classList.remove('vxfb-chip--danger');
      }
    });
  }

  state.filter = filter;

  // Map filter keys to filterList keys
  var filterMap = { 'alle': 'alle', 'neu': 'neu', 'dringend': 'dringend', 'inbearbeitung': 'inbearbeitung', 'rueckruf': 'rueckruf' };
  var fKey = filterMap[filter] || filter;

  // Use existing filterList but pass a fake btn
  var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#' + listId + ' .call-row');
  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (fKey === 'alle') {
      row.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else if (fKey === 'dringend') {
      row.classList.toggle('hidden', row.dataset.priority !== 'urgent');
    } else if (fKey === 'neu') {
      row.classList.toggle('hidden', row.dataset.status !== 'new');
    } else if (fKey === 'inbearbeitung') {
      row.classList.toggle('hidden', !['in_progress','follow_up_scheduled'].includes(row.dataset.status));
    } else if (fKey === 'rueckruf') {
      // Filter by callback badge
      row.classList.toggle('hidden', !row.querySelector('.chip-callback, .chip-rueckruf'));
    }
  });

  vxFbUpdateCount(key);
  vxFbUpdateActiveRow(key);
}

function vxFbToggleSort(key) {
  var opts = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-sortopts');
  var btn = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-sortbtn');
  var chevron = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-sortchevron');
  if (!opts) return;
  var isOpen = opts.style.display !== 'none';
  opts.style.display = isOpen ? 'none' : 'flex';
  if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('vxfb-sort-btn--open', !isOpen);
  if (chevron) {
    // Phosphor: Klasse statt data-lucide Attribut
    chevron.className = chevron.className.replace(/\bph-caret-(?:down|up)\b/g, '');
    chevron.classList.add(isOpen ? 'ph-caret-down' : 'ph-caret-up');
  }
}

function vxFbSetSort(key, sort, btn) {
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  if (!state) return;
  state.sort = sort;

  // Update sort opt active states
  var opts = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-sortopts');
  if (opts) {
    opts.querySelectorAll('.vxfb-sort-opt').forEach(function(o) {
      o.classList.toggle('vxfb-sort-opt--active', o.getAttribute('data-sort') === sort);
    });
  }

  // Update sort button label
  var label = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-sortlabel');
  if (label) label.textContent = _vxFbSortLabels[sort] || sort;

  // Close sort options
  vxFbToggleSort(key);

  // Apply sort via existing system
  if (key === 'anrufe') { _anrufeSort = sort; renderAnrufe(); }
  else if (key === 'archiv') { _archivSort = sort; filterArchiv(_vxFbState.archiv.filter, null); }

  // Re-apply search if active
  if (state.search) searchList(_vxFbListMap[key], state.search);
  vxFbUpdateCount(key);
  vxFbUpdateActiveRow(key);
}

function vxFbUpdateCount(key) {
  var listId = _vxFbListMap[key];
  var countEl = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-count');
  if (!countEl) return;
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  var hasSearch = !!(state && state.search);
  var hasFilter = !!(state && state.filter && state.filter !== 'alle' && state.filter !== 'all');
  if (!hasSearch && !hasFilter) { countEl.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  var rows = document.querySelectorAll('#' + listId + ' .call-row:not(.hidden)');
  countEl.textContent = rows.length + (rows.length === 1 ? ' Treffer' : ' Treffer');
  countEl.style.display = '';
}

function vxFbUpdateActiveRow(key) {
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  var activeRow = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-active');
  var pillsEl = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-pills');
  if (!activeRow || !pillsEl || !state) return;

  var pills = [];

  // Filter pill
  if (state.filter && state.filter !== 'alle' && state.filter !== 'all') {
    var filterLabels = { neu: 'Neu', dringend: 'Dringend', inbearbeitung: 'In Bearbeitung', rueckruf: 'Rückruf', today: 'Heute', week: 'Diese Woche', month: 'Dieser Monat' };
    var fLabel = filterLabels[state.filter] || state.filter;
    var isDanger = state.filter === 'dringend';
    pills.push('<span class="vxfb-pill' + (isDanger ? ' vxfb-pill--danger' : '') + '">'
      + fLabel
      + ' <span class="vxfb-pill-x" onclick="vxFbFilter(\'' + key + '\',\'alle\',null);vxFbChipResetAll(\'' + key + '\')">×</span></span>');
  }

  // Search pill
  if (state.search) {
    pills.push('<span class="vxfb-pill vxfb-pill--neutral">'
      + 'Suche: &ldquo;' + state.search.replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '&rdquo;'
      + ' <span class="vxfb-pill-x" onclick="vxFbClearSearch(\'' + key + '\')">×</span></span>');
  }

  if (pills.length === 0) { activeRow.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  pillsEl.innerHTML = pills.join('');
  activeRow.style.display = 'flex';
}

function vxFbChipResetAll(key) {
  var fb = document.getElementById('fb-' + key);
  if (!fb) return;
  fb.querySelectorAll('.vxfb-chip').forEach(function(c) {
    var isAlle = c.getAttribute('data-filter') === 'alle' || c.getAttribute('data-filter') === 'all';
    c.classList.toggle('vxfb-chip--active', isAlle); c.classList.toggle('vx-chip--active', isAlle);
    c.classList.remove('vxfb-chip--danger');
  });
}

function vxFbClearAll(key) {
  var state = _vxFbState[key];
  if (state) { state.filter = 'alle'; state.search = ''; }
  vxFbClearSearch(key);
  vxFbFilter(key, 'alle', null);
  vxFbChipResetAll(key);
  var activeRow = document.getElementById('fb-' + key + '-active');
  if (activeRow) activeRow.style.display = 'none';
  vxFbUpdateCount(key);
}

// ⌘K shortcut to focus anrufe search
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
  if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
    var activeTab = document.querySelector('.tab-page.active');
    if (!activeTab) return;
    var tabId = activeTab.id;
    var inputId = tabId === 'tab-anrufe' ? 'fb-anrufe-input' : null;
    if (!inputId) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var input = document.getElementById(inputId);
    if (input) { input.focus(); input.select(); }
  }
});

function escHtml(v){ return String(v||'').replace(/[&<>"']/g, s => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[s])); }
// PATCH v2.10 — CTA- und Task-Action-Stabilisierung
function getTaskTypeUiMeta(rawType) {
  var type = String(rawType || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (type === 'callback' || type === 'rueckruf' || type === 'rückruf') {
    return {
      kind: 'callback',
      label: 'Rückruf',
      primary: 'Jetzt anrufen',
      primaryAction: 'call',
      sectionTitle: 'Rückruf',
      descriptionFallback: 'Kundin oder Kunden zurückrufen und Anliegen klären.',
      emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzliche Rückrufnotiz vorhanden.',
      actionHelp: 'Startet direkt einen Anruf über die hinterlegte Telefonnummer.'
    };
  }
  if (type === 'information' || type === 'info' || type === 'info_senden' || type === 'information_senden') {
    return {
      kind: 'information',
      label: 'Info',
      primary: 'Info senden',
      primaryAction: 'edit',
      sectionTitle: 'Information',
      descriptionFallback: 'Information an Kundin oder Kunden senden und Versand dokumentieren.',
      emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzliche Information hinterlegt.',
      actionHelp: 'Öffnet die Aufgabe zur Bearbeitung und Dokumentation.'
    };
  }
  if (type === 'offerte' || type === 'offer') {
    return {
      kind: 'offer',
      label: 'Offerte',
      primary: 'Offerte vorbereiten',
      primaryAction: 'edit',
      sectionTitle: 'Offerte vorbereiten',
      descriptionFallback: 'Offerte vorbereiten, Angaben prüfen und nächsten Schritt festhalten.',
      emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzlichen Angaben zur Offerte vorhanden.',
      actionHelp: 'Öffnet die Aufgabe zur Bearbeitung. Ein separater Offerten-Editor ist aktuell nicht hinterlegt.'
    };
  }
  if (type === 'appointment' || type === 'termin') {
    return {
      kind: 'appointment',
      label: 'Termin',
      primary: 'Termin planen',
      primaryAction: 'edit',
      sectionTitle: 'Termin planen',
      descriptionFallback: 'Terminwunsch prüfen, Zeitpunkt festlegen und Rückmeldung vorbereiten.',
      emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzlichen Angaben zum Termin vorhanden.',
      actionHelp: 'Öffnet die Aufgabe, damit Fälligkeit und Notiz angepasst werden können.'
    };
  }
  if (type === 'follow_up' || type === 'nachfassen') {
    return {
      kind: 'followup',
      label: 'Nachfassen',
      primary: 'Nachfassen öffnen',
      primaryAction: 'edit',
      sectionTitle: 'Nachfassen',
      descriptionFallback: 'Nachfassaktion prüfen und nächsten Schritt festhalten.',
      emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzliche Nachfassnotiz vorhanden.',
      actionHelp: 'Öffnet die Nachfassaufgabe zur Bearbeitung.'
    };
  }
  return {
    kind: 'task',
    label: 'Aufgabe',
    primary: 'Aufgabe öffnen',
    primaryAction: 'edit',
    sectionTitle: 'Aufgabe',
    descriptionFallback: 'Aufgabe prüfen und nächsten Schritt festhalten.',
    emptyNote: 'Keine zusätzliche Beschreibung vorhanden.',
    actionHelp: 'Öffnet die Aufgabe zur Bearbeitung.'
  };
}

function vxNormalizeTelForHref(phone) {
  var raw = String(phone || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return '';
  var plus = raw.charAt(0) === '+';
  var digits = raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
  if (!digits) return '';
  return (plus ? '+' : '') + digits;
}

function vxOpenTelHref(phone, fallbackMessage) {
  var dial = vxNormalizeTelForHref(phone);
  if (!dial) {
    vxTaskDetailNotify(fallbackMessage || 'Keine gültige Telefonnummer vorhanden', 'warning');
    return false;
  }
  try { window.location.href = 'tel:' + dial; } catch(e) { window.location.assign('tel:' + dial); }
  return true;
}

function vxTaskActionButton(handler, icon, label, variant, helpText) {
  // [PATCH v2.22 — kompakte Action-Buttons statt klobiger Kacheln]
  var btnClass = variant === 'primary' ? 'btn btn--primary' : 'btn btn--secondary';
  var html = '<button type="button" class="' + btnClass + '" style="justify-content:flex-start;gap:8px;" onclick="event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();' + handler + '">';
  html += '<i class="ph-bold ph-' + escHtml(icon || 'arrow-right') + '" style="flex-shrink:0;"></i>';
  html += '<span>' + escHtml(label || 'Öffnen') + '</span>';
  html += '</button>';
  return html;
}
function vxFindManualTaskRecord(taskId) {
  var id = String(taskId || '');
  if (typeof vxResolveDprRecord === 'function') {
    var resolved = vxResolveDprRecord(id);
    if (resolved && isManualTaskRecord(resolved)) return resolved;
  }
  var raw = id.indexOf('case:') === 0 ? id.slice(5) : id;
  var manual = (typeof allManualTaskRecords !== 'undefined' && allManualTaskRecords) ? allManualTaskRecords : [];
  var calls = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : [];
  return manual.find(function(r) {
      var f = (r && r.fields) || {};
      return String(r.id) === id || String(r.id) === raw || String(f.task_entity_id || '') === id || String(f.task_entity_id || '') === raw;
    })
    || calls.find(function(r) { return (String(r.id) === id || String(r.id) === raw) && isManualTaskRecord(r); })
    || null;
}

function vxTaskDetailNotify(message, type) {
  if (typeof toast === 'function') { toast(message, type || 'info'); return; }
  if (typeof showToast === 'function') { showToast(message); }
}

function vxTaskDetailPhone(fields) {
  return String((fields && (fields.phone || fields.phone_number || fields.caller_phone)) || '').trim();
}

function vxTaskDetailRelatedCall(fields) {
  var f = fields || {};
  var records = (typeof allRecords !== 'undefined' && allRecords) ? allRecords : [];
  var relatedCallId = String(f.related_call_id || f.origin_call_id || '').trim();
  if (relatedCallId) return records.find(function(r) { return String(r.id) === relatedCallId; }) || null;

  // Schema heute: customer_tasks hat keine related_call_id. Deshalb nur dann einen
  // Originalanruf anbieten, wenn die Telefonnummer exakt EINEN passenden Call hat.
  var phone = vxTaskDetailPhone(f);
  var normalize = (typeof normalizePhoneForMatch === 'function') ? normalizePhoneForMatch : function(v){ return String(v || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''); };
  var clean = normalize(phone);
  if (!clean) return null;
  var matches = records.filter(function(r) {
    var rf = (r && r.fields) || {};
    return normalize(rf.caller_phone || '') === clean;
  });
  return matches.length === 1 ? matches[0] : null;
}

function vxTaskDetailIconMeta(taskMeta) {
  var label = String((taskMeta && taskMeta.label) || '').toLowerCase();
  if (label === 'rückruf')   return { icon: 'phone-outgoing',  color: '#94A3B8' };
  if (label === 'offerte')   return { icon: 'receipt',          color: '#94A3B8' };
  if (label === 'termin')    return { icon: 'calendar-plus',    color: '#94A3B8' };
  if (label === 'nachfassen') return { icon: 'clock-countdown', color: '#94A3B8' };
  return                              { icon: 'check-circle',   color: '#94A3B8' };
}

function vxRefreshOpenTaskDetail(taskId) {
  try {
    if (window._vxCurrentDetailType !== 'task') return;
    if (String(window._vxCurrentTaskId || '') !== String(taskId || '')) return;
    var page = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
    if (!page || page.style.display !== 'block') return;
    var rec = vxFindManualTaskRecord(taskId);
    if (!rec) return;
    var phone = vxTaskDetailPhone(rec.fields || {});
    if (phone && typeof buildCallHistory === 'function') {
      buildCallHistory(phone).then(function(history) { renderTaskDetailPage(rec, history || []); }).catch(function(){ renderTaskDetailPage(rec, []); });
    } else {
      renderTaskDetailPage(rec, []);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[task-detail-refresh]', e);
  }
}
window.vxRefreshOpenTaskDetail = vxRefreshOpenTaskDetail;

function vxTaskDetailApplyEditMode(mode) {
  var normalized = String(mode || 'edit').toLowerCase();
  var heading = document.getElementById('manual-task-title-heading');
  var saveBtn = document.getElementById('manual-task-save-btn');
  var body = document.querySelector('#manual-task-overlay .modal-body');
  var hint = document.getElementById('manual-task-mode-hint');
  if (!hint && body) {
    hint = document.createElement('div');
    hint.id = 'manual-task-mode-hint';
    hint.style.cssText = 'margin:0 0 12px;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--surface2);font-size:12px;line-height:1.45;color:var(--ink2);';
    body.insertBefore(hint, body.firstChild);
  }
  var focusEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-title');
  var text = 'Bearbeite die Aufgabe und speichere die Änderungen.';
  if (normalized === 'prepare') {
    if (heading) heading.textContent = 'Aufgabe vorbereiten';
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.textContent = 'Vorbereitung speichern';
    text = 'Nutze diese Ansicht, um Titel, Typ, Telefonnummer und Notiz der Aufgabe zu präzisieren.';
    focusEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-note') || document.getElementById('manual-task-title');
  } else if (normalized === 'reschedule') {
    if (heading) heading.textContent = 'Fälligkeit anpassen';
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.textContent = 'Fälligkeit speichern';
    text = 'Passe Fälligkeitsdatum, Uhrzeit oder Priorität an. Inhalt und Status bleiben unverändert, sofern du sie nicht änderst.';
    focusEl = document.getElementById('manual-task-due') || document.getElementById('manual-task-due-time');
  } else {
    if (heading) heading.textContent = 'Aufgabe bearbeiten';
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.textContent = 'Änderungen speichern';
  }
  if (hint) {
    hint.textContent = text;
    hint.style.display = '';
  }
  if (focusEl && focusEl.focus) setTimeout(function(){ try { focusEl.focus(); if (focusEl.select && normalized !== 'reschedule') focusEl.select(); } catch(e) {} }, 40);
}
window.vxTaskDetailApplyEditMode = vxTaskDetailApplyEditMode;

function vxTaskDetailOpenEdit(taskId, mode) {
  // Detailseite bewusst offen lassen.
  // Das Edit-/Verschieben-Overlay liegt darüber; beim Schliessen landet der Nutzer
  // dadurch wieder auf derselben Detailseite statt auf der Übersicht.
  if (typeof openManualTaskEditModal === 'function') {
    openManualTaskEditModal(taskId);
    vxTaskDetailApplyEditMode(mode || 'edit');

    // [PATCH v2.25 — Verschieben: nur Zeitpunkt-Felder sichtbar]
    var normalized = String(mode || 'edit').toLowerCase();
    if (normalized === 'reschedule') {
      // Felder ausblenden die beim Verschieben nicht relevant sind
      var hideIds = ['manual-task-title', 'manual-task-phone', 'manual-task-note'];
      var hideLabels = ['NAME / TITEL', 'TELEFONNUMMER', 'NOTIZ'];
      var modalBody = document.querySelector('#manual-task-overlay .modal-body');
      if (modalBody) {
        // Alle modal-field Divs prüfen
        modalBody.querySelectorAll('.modal-field, .modal-grid').forEach(function(el) {
          var label = el.querySelector('label');
          var labelText = label ? (label.textContent || '').trim().replace(/\s*\*.*/, '').toUpperCase() : '';
          var inputId = el.querySelector('input, select, textarea') ?
            (el.querySelector('input, select, textarea').id || '') : '';
          var isHide = hideIds.indexOf(inputId) >= 0 ||
            hideLabels.some(function(l) { return labelText.indexOf(l) >= 0; });
          // Typ-Feld auch ausblenden bei reschedule
          if (inputId === 'manual-task-type' || labelText.indexOf('TYP') >= 0) isHide = true;
          if (isHide) {
            el.style.display = 'none';
            el._vxRescheduleHidden = true;
          }
        });
      }
      // Bei closeManualTaskModal wieder einblenden — via Observer
      var overlay = document.getElementById('manual-task-overlay');
      if (overlay && !overlay._vxRescheduleObserver) {
        var obs = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
          mutations.forEach(function(m) {
            if (m.type === 'attributes' && m.attributeName === 'class') {
              if (!overlay.classList.contains('open')) {
                // Modal geschlossen — alle hidden Felder wieder einblenden
                var modalBody2 = overlay.querySelector('.modal-body');
                if (modalBody2) {
                  modalBody2.querySelectorAll('[data-vx-reschedule-hidden], .modal-field, .modal-grid').forEach(function(el) {
                    if (el._vxRescheduleHidden) {
                      el.style.display = '';
                      el._vxRescheduleHidden = false;
                    }
                  });
                }
              }
            }
          });
        });
        obs.observe(overlay, { attributes: true });
        overlay._vxRescheduleObserver = obs;
      }
    }
  }
}
window.vxTaskDetailOpenEdit = vxTaskDetailOpenEdit;

function vxTaskDetailCall(taskId) {
  var rec = vxFindManualTaskRecord(taskId);
  var f = (rec && rec.fields) || {};
  var phone = vxTaskDetailPhone(f);
  if (!phone) { vxTaskDetailNotify('Keine Telefonnummer vorhanden', 'warning'); return; }
  if (typeof vxOpenTelHref === 'function') vxOpenTelHref(phone, 'Keine gültige Telefonnummer vorhanden');
  else window.location.href = 'tel:' + String(phone).replace(/[^+0-9]/g, '');
}
window.vxTaskDetailCall = vxTaskDetailCall;

function vxTaskDetailDone(taskId) {
  if (typeof confirmTaskDone !== 'function') return;
  confirmTaskDone(taskId, null, 'task-detail-page', function() {
    vxTaskDetailNotify('Aufgabe erledigt', 'success');
    if (typeof vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion === 'function') vxCloseAfterSuccessfulCompletion();
    if (typeof loadData === 'function') setTimeout(loadData, 300);
  });
}
window.vxTaskDetailDone = vxTaskDetailDone;

function vxTaskDetailOpenRelated(taskId) {
  var rec = vxFindManualTaskRecord(taskId);
  var related = vxTaskDetailRelatedCall((rec && rec.fields) || {});
  if (!related) { vxTaskDetailNotify('Kein Originalanruf verknüpft', 'warning'); return; }
  if (typeof showCallDetail === 'function') showCallDetail(String(related.id));
}
window.vxTaskDetailOpenRelated = vxTaskDetailOpenRelated;

function renderTaskDetailPage(taskRecord, history) {
  if (!taskRecord || !taskRecord.id) return;
  var f = taskRecord.fields || {};
  var meta = getTaskTypeUiMeta(f.type);
  var visual = vxTaskDetailIconMeta(meta);
  var phone = vxTaskDetailPhone(f);
  var relatedCall = vxTaskDetailRelatedCall(f);
  var contact = vxGetTaskContactDisplayName(f);
  var dueLabel = formatTaskDueLabel(f, { emptyLabel: 'Ohne Fälligkeit' });
  var statusText = (typeof vxGetInquiryDisplayStatus === 'function')
    ? vxGetInquiryDisplayStatus(taskRecord)
    : statusLabel(f.dashboard_status || f.status || 'new');
  var sourceText = relatedCall ? 'Aus Anruf' : 'Manuell';
  var titleText = meta.sectionTitle || meta.primary || meta.label || 'Aufgabe';
  var rawTitle = String(f.title || '').trim();
  var rawNote = String(f.note || f.notes || '').trim();
  var meaningfulTitle = (!vxDashIsGenericLabel(rawTitle) && rawTitle !== contact && rawTitle.indexOf(':') < 0) ? rawTitle : '';
  var description = rawNote || meaningfulTitle || meta.descriptionFallback || 'Keine Beschreibung vorhanden.';
  var infoHint = meta.emptyNote || '';
  var createdAt = getRecordTimestamp(taskRecord) || f.created_at || '';
  var dateRight = dueLabel && dueLabel !== 'Ohne Fälligkeit' ? dueLabel : (createdAt ? fmtDate(createdAt) : '');
  var safeTaskId = escHtml(taskRecord.id);

  window._vxCurrentDetailType = 'task';
  window._vxCurrentTaskId = String(taskRecord.id);
  window._vxCurrentCallId = null;
  window._vxActiveRecordId = String(taskRecord.id);
  window._vxLastAnfragenRecordId = String(taskRecord.id);

  // ── Voxera Detail Header für Task (clean-rebuild 2026-05-17) ──
  // Tasks teilen sich die Detail-Shell mit Anrufen, brauchen aber einen eigenen,
  // schlankeren Header. Hier wird das Banner mit dem .vxd-header-Layout befüllt.
  try {
    var _bannerTask = document.getElementById('call-detail-banner');
    if (_bannerTask) {
      var _escT = (typeof escapeHtml === 'function') ? escapeHtml : function(v){
        return String(v == null ? '' : v).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){
          return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c] || c;
        });
      };
      var _tIsFocus = !!(document.body && document.body.classList && document.body.classList.contains('vx-requests-focus-mode'));
      var _tFocusLabel = _tIsFocus ? 'Split' : 'Fokus';
      var _tFocusTitle = _tIsFocus ? 'Zur Split-Ansicht wechseln' : 'Fokusmodus öffnen';
      var _tFocusIcon = _tIsFocus ? 'ph-arrows-in' : 'ph-arrows-out';
      var _tMetaParts = [];
      _tMetaParts.push(_escT(meta.label || 'Aufgabe'));
      if (phone) _tMetaParts.push(_escT(phone));
      if (dueLabel && dueLabel !== 'Ohne Fälligkeit') _tMetaParts.push(_escT(dueLabel));
      var _tMeta = _tMetaParts.join(' · ');
      var _tMetaHtml = _tMetaParts.map(function(part){
        return '<span class="vxd-meta-item">' + _escT(part) + '</span>';
      }).join('');

      // Status-Pill für Task
      var _tIsDone = (f.status === 'closed' || f.dashboard_status === 'closed');
      var _tStatusPillColor = _tIsDone ? '#059669' : '#1A6FE8';
      var _tPillsHtml = '<span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;white-space:nowrap;line-height:1;color:' + _tStatusPillColor + ';background:' + _tStatusPillColor + '14;border:0.5px solid ' + _tStatusPillColor + '33;">' + _escT(statusText) + '</span>';

      var _tSecondaryLabel = phone ? 'Rückruf' : (typeText || 'Aufgabe');
      var _tPillHtml = '<span class="vxdr-pill' + (_tIsDone ? ' vxdr-pill--success' : ' vxdr-pill--status') + '">' + _escT(statusText) + '</span>'
        + (_tSecondaryLabel ? '<span class="vxdr-pill vxdr-pill--context">' + _escT(_tSecondaryLabel) + '</span>' : '');
      var _tPageForFocus = document.getElementById('call-detail-page');
      var _tIsEmbeddedSplit = !!(_tPageForFocus && document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right') && document.getElementById('anrufe-split-right').contains(_tPageForFocus));
      var _tFocusMode = !!((document.body && document.body.classList && document.body.classList.contains('vx-requests-focus-mode')) || (_tPageForFocus && !_tIsEmbeddedSplit && (_tPageForFocus.classList.contains('is-fullscreen') || _tPageForFocus.classList.contains('vx-detail-focus'))));
      var _tLayoutClass = _tFocusMode ? 'vxdr-focus' : 'vxdr-split';
      var _tPrimaryLabel = phone ? 'Anrufen' : 'Erledigen';
      var _tPrimaryIcon = phone ? 'ph-phone' : 'ph-check';
      var _tPrimaryId = phone ? 'vx-cd-qa-call' : 'vx-cd-qa-done';
      var _tPrimaryAction = phone ? "vxTaskDetailCall('" + safeTaskId + "')" : "vxTaskDetailDone('" + safeTaskId + "')";
      _bannerTask.className = 'vxdr-banner';
      _bannerTask.removeAttribute('style');
      _bannerTask.innerHTML = ''
        + '<section class="vxdr-header ' + _tLayoutClass + '" aria-label="Aufgabendetails">'
        +   '<button type="button" class="vxdr-back" aria-label="Zurück zu Anfragen" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxHandleDetailBack();"><i class="ph-bold ph-arrow-left" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Anfragen</span></button>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-identity">'
        +     '<div class="vxdr-avatar" id="call-detail-avatar"><i class="ph-bold ph-' + _escT(visual.icon) + '" aria-hidden="true"></i></div>'
        +     '<div class="vxdr-copy">'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-title-row"><div class="vxdr-title" id="call-detail-title">' + _escT(contact) + '</div></div>'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-meta" id="call-detail-subtitle" aria-label="' + _escT(_tMeta) + '">' + _tMetaHtml + '</div>'
        +       '<div class="vxdr-pills">' + _tPillHtml + '</div>'
        +     '</div>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-actions">'
        +     '<button type="button" class="vxdr-btn vxdr-primary" id="' + _tPrimaryId + '" onclick="event.stopPropagation();' + _tPrimaryAction + ';" title="' + _escT(_tPrimaryLabel) + '"><i class="ph-bold ' + _tPrimaryIcon + '" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="vxdr-btn-label">' + _escT(_tPrimaryLabel) + '</span></button>'
        +     (_tFocusMode
        ? '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn vxdr-focus-toggle" id="vx-cd-qa-expand" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxCallDetailExpand();" aria-label="Ansicht verkleinern" title="Ansicht verkleinern"><i class="ph-bold ph-arrows-in" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>'
        : '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn vxdr-focus-toggle" id="vx-cd-qa-expand" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxCallDetailExpand();" aria-label="' + _escT(_tFocusTitle) + '" title="' + _escT(_tFocusTitle) + '"><i class="ph-bold ' + _tFocusIcon + '" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>')
        +     '<button type="button" class="vxdr-iconbtn" id="vxd-menu-trigger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdToggleMenu(event);" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Weitere Aktionen" title="Weitere Aktionen"><i class="ph-bold ph-dots-three-vertical" aria-hidden="true"></i></button>'
        +   '</div>'
        +   '<div class="vxdr-menu" id="vxd-menu" role="menu" hidden>'
        +     '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxTaskDetailOpenEdit(\'' + safeTaskId + '\',\'edit\');"><i class="ph-bold ph-pencil-simple" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Bearbeiten</span></button>'
        +     (phone && !_isDoneEarly() ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxTaskDetailDone(\'' + safeTaskId + '\');"><i class="ph-bold ph-check" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Als erledigt markieren</span></button>' : '')
        +     (relatedCall ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" onclick="event.stopPropagation();vxdCloseMenu();vxTaskDetailOpenRelated(\'' + safeTaskId + '\');"><i class="ph-bold ph-phone-transfer" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Originalanruf anzeigen</span></button>' : '')
        +   '</div>'
        + '</section>';
    }
